Separate cases pin the other two classes the ticket names — a detail answering **HTTP 200 with a `fault` envelope** (failure kind `fault`, fault code/message verbatim, no facts from that item) and a detail whose connection is **reset** (failure kind `transport`, a genuine `HttpRequestException`) — with the identical expected shape.
- Expected: the walk completes — the unfetchable item is **quarantined with evidence** (the gateway's error verbatim in the quarantine record) and **counted in the run stats with its named reason** (`DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE`-class, the disclosure index recorded); **all fetchable items' rows/cursors are written**; the run terminates an **honest partial** — never `failed`, never a silent omission. At chain level, a partial family step does not block `metrics-recompute`/`market-snapshot`/`medians` (the composition asserted in the chain test lane). *(Red before the fix: the `SourceFetchException` propagates out of the shared `VykDisclosureWalk` into the adapter and job → run `failed` → the EOD chain fail-stops before the snapshot steps — the 2026-10-22 evidence, runs 185/190/195.)*
- Dependencies: —

**TC-MDF-095 — Per-item hang bounded: a stalled detail fetch is timed out and takes the same honest skip path** (new v1.12)
- Steps: with the per-item fetch timeout configured (the ticket's config constant, a low-minutes default), double ONE disclosure detail endpoint that accepts the connection and never responds (the 90-minute hang shape of runs 185/190). Run the family job; assert the wall-clock bound of the stalled item's fetch, the skip path it takes, and the integrity of the rest of the walk. Separately, run a healthy paced walk (the ~12 s/item pace of run 173) under the same timeout and assert no false timeout.
- Expected: the stalled item's fetch is **bounded by the configured per-item timeout** and then takes the **identical quarantine-and-continue skip path as the 500 case** (named reason, evidence, counted); the run completes honest-partial with every fetchable item's rows written. A healthy paced walk is **never falsely timed out** (the timeout constant vs the pace asserted). *(Red before the fix: the hang propagates to the run level, where only the 041 watchdog bounds it — a `RUN_HUNG` row and a lost whole run, the 2026-10-22 evidence.)*
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-094.

## 14. Share-count store from VYK `/memberSecurities` (TKT-foundation-058; supersedes the TKT-foundation-033 goal)

**Lane posture.** Both cases are **L2** and **doubles-only, never live sources**: the `/memberSecurities`
payload is the committed real-shape double `CannedSourceCatalog.MemberSecuritiesOk`, derived from the
live 2026-10-22 dev-gateway capture (the `/tmp` snapshot is ephemeral and is never referenced). The
double carries the real archive's shapes — multi-tertip members whose lines sum to the real counts
(ASELS A+B = 4.56B, THYAO C+A = 1.38B, ISDMR A+B = 2.9B), a trading-open flag per line, an
ISIN-fallback member, a closed-only member, a codeless/odd member and no-line-at-all members — plus
two **clearly labeled derived anomalies** (a line with no `currentCapital` in a group, and a sole line
with no `currentCapital`), because the capture happened to have zero such lines while the ticket's
pinned contract requires the skip path to be exercised. The store is driven through the production
`IIngestionJobRunner` over a migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL; the **live unit/aggregation
cross-validation** (ASELS/THYAO/TUPRS/GARAN/AEFES) is the ticket's post-merge orchestrator step, not
this suite's job — the suite pins the decode/aggregation/store contract against the capture's shapes.

**TC-MDF-092 — The `shares` lane decodes `/memberSecurities` into an honest per-instrument store** (new v1.13)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002/003 (VYK gateway ingest — the new endpoint), FR-MDF-008 (provenance), FR-MDF-010/011 (skips counted, never hidden), `01` v1.5.36 §10.9.11 (the endpoint + `shares` lane) · Level: L2 · Design: decision table — security-line class ∈ {single trading-open line, multi-tertip mixed open/closed, multi-tertip all open, closed-only, no line at all, sole line missing `currentCapital`, group with one line missing `currentCapital`} × store outcome; plus state transitions first run → identical re-run → changed-input re-run, and the failure posture
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed; the double's instruments bound by symbol (`ASELS`, `THYAO`, `TUPRS`, `GARAN`, `AEFES`, `ISDMR`, `DRVFM`, `OTHER`, `GLRMK`, `CLSDR` — `DRVFM` carrying the fallback line's ISIN, `OTHER` carrying `ASELS`'s ISIN as a decoy); `CannedSourceCatalog.MemberSecuritiesOk` served on `/memberSecurities` through WireMock; `RequestDelayMs` = 0; `FakeTimeProvider` at T, advanced to T+12h for the repair re-run.
- Steps: (i) run `shares`; read `share_counts`, the `ingest_runs` row and `quarantined_facts`. (ii) read the WireMock request log for `/memberSecurities` before/after two runs. (iii) re-run unchanged; then change one tertip (`2138181818.184` → `2138181819.184`, ASELS's B line) and re-run. (iv) stub HTTP 500; then stub a body that is not the memberSecurities shape.
- Expected: (i) the run is `succeeded` with `written: 7` / `skipped: 2`; one row per **matched** instrument with `shares_outstanding` = the sum of that instrument's security lines' `currentCapital` — ASELS 4,560,000,000 (A closed + B open), THYAO 1,380,000,000, TUPRS 1,926,795,598, GARAN 4,200,000,000, AEFES 592,105,263, ISDMR 2,900,000,000 (both tertips open), DRVFM 1,234,567 via the **ISIN fallback**; every row carries `source_ref = kap` and `recorded_at` = the run instant; matching is `borsaKodu`-primary, so `OTHER` (sharing `ASELS`'s ISIN) is **never** a candidate and gets no row; **no row** for `GLRMK` (no line) or `CLSDR` (only closed lines) and **no zero row anywhere**; the two `currentCapital`-less lines are counted as `skipped: 2`, `NOCAP` (sole line) gets no row and `DRVE`'s matched group is **withheld** (a partial tertip sum would be a fabricated count) — never `0`; the skip count rides `ingest_runs.stats_json` and the run stays `succeeded` (the gap is counted, not hidden behind a failed run). (ii) exactly **one** paced `/memberSecurities` call per run — no per-instrument loop, no second request; two runs = two requests; the code default path is `/memberSecurities`. (iii) the identical re-run writes nothing (`written: 0`, `unchanged: 7`, `skipped: 2`) and every `recorded_at` stays put — the served `asOf` does not lie; the changed-input re-run reports `written: 1` / `unchanged: 6`, the repaired row carries the new value (4,560,000,001) and the later instant, the untouched rows keep theirs, and one row per instrument holds. (iv) HTTP 500 → the run row is `failed`, no `share_counts` row and **no** quarantine (transport is not a parse event); a non-memberSecurities body → `UNPARSEABLE_PAYLOAD` quarantine, no rows.
- Dependencies: —

**TC-MDF-093 — The balance-sheet payload serves the stored share count and honest-null when absent** (new v1.13)
- Traces: FR-MDF-011 (public-side honest surface), FR-MOV-006 lane (the downstream market-P/E unblock — seam only), TKT-foundation-031/052 doctrine, `01` v1.5.36 §10.9.11 (serving seam) · Level: L2 · Design: decision table — instrument ∈ {stored count, no stored count} × served field
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed, `derived_metrics` recomputed at T; `share_counts` cleared and one row seeded directly for `ALFA` (4,560,000,000 at T 06:00Z, `source_ref = kap`) — the ingest path is TC-MDF-092's observable, this case pins the seam; `LAMDA` (current XU100 constituent with a FY balance sheet) has **no** stored count.
- Steps: `GET /api/v1/stocks/ALFA/balance-sheet`; `GET /api/v1/stocks/LAMDA/balance-sheet`.
- Expected: both are `200` with the public cache headers and the honest envelope unchanged (`asOf` = T). `ALFA.shareCount.sharesOutstanding` = 4,560,000,000 and `ALFA.shareCount.asOf` = T's date. `LAMDA.shareCount.sharesOutstanding` and `.asOf` are JSON `null` — honest absence, never `0` (a dash downstream). The **statement-reported** `lines.sharesOutstanding` / `lines.sharesDiluted` and `bookValuePerShare` are byte-identical to TC-RES-012's goldens (the market count is an additive field, not a replacement) — the market-data lane never rewrites the statement's own figures.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-092 (the store's shape).

## 15. Statements-lane isolation and stale-disclosure-cursor recovery (TKT-foundation-063, architecture v1.5.42 reserved; extended by TKT-foundation-069 — the family-lane cursor checkpoint — v1.5.84)

**Lane posture.** Both cases are **L2, doubles only, never live sources**: the VYK `/members`, `/disclosures` and `/disclosureDetail` routes are the WireMock doubles the 034/046/061 lanes already use (the list-body ER005 double is committed with the tests, derived from the live 2026-10-23 gateway answer). The statements lane runs through the production `IIngestionJobRunner` over a migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL; pacing is bound to 0; the fake clock is the run instant. The fix unifies the statements walk on the shared `VykDisclosureWalker` (061), so these cases pin the walk's **statement-specific** consequences, not a second implementation.

**TC-MDF-096 — Statements per-company / per-detail isolation: an un-fetchable disclosure or an all-company skip world is an honest `partial`, never a run-fatal `failed`** (new v1.15)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (statements ingest), FR-MDF-008 (identity/provenance), FR-MDF-010/011 (skips counted, never hidden), FR-MDF-012 (isolation), FR-MDF-016 (pipeline health — the chain completes), `01` v1.5.42/§10.9.15 · Level: L2 · Design: fault injection — detail failure class × per-company/whole-run outcome
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed, the current XU100 statements bound (or a small bound with a fixture universe of, say, three companies where one is doubled to fail); the VYK directory + list doubles answer normally; `RequestDelayMs = 0`; the shared walker's per-item timeout at a small configured value for the hang case.
- Steps: (i) one company's detail returns HTTP 500 (the gateway's error body verbatim) while the other companies' details answer → run `statements`; read `ingest_runs`, `quarantined_facts`, `financial_statements`, the per-symbol stats. (ii) separately, one detail connection is reset (transport). (iii) an all-isolated world: every company's detail returns HTTP 500 (no write possible) → run `statements`.
- Expected: (i)/(ii) the run completes; the failing item is **quarantined with evidence** (`DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE`, the disclosure index, the failure kind `http`/`transport`, the gateway's error verbatim) and counted; every other company's statement rows are written; the run status is **`partial`** — never `failed`. (iii) the zero-write all-isolated world is **`partial`**, with one evidence-carrying skip per company and no silent omission (red today: 24 `failed` + 6 quarantined → `failed`, the 2026-10-23 run-212 shape). A genuine source-down (directory/list transport or a non-ER005 HTTP status on **every** company) still ends `failed` so the retry ladder applies.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-094 (the shared walker's behaviour this lane now reuses).

**TC-MDF-097 — A stale disclosure cursor answers ER005 once, resets from evidence, persists the correction, and does not repeat** (new v1.15)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002, FR-MDF-010/011 (incremental cursor + honest depth), `01` v1.5.42/§10.9.11 · Level: L2 · Design: state transition — stale persisted cursor → ER005 → one reset → corrected cursor; plus the non-ER005 negative
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed; one instrument's `kap-disclosure-cursor` coverage row seeded **past the gateway's tail**; the `/disclosures` double answers HTTP 400 with the verbatim body `{"code":"ER005","message":"Bildirim bulunamadı. Disclosure not found."}` for that index and lists the early-index disclosures normally; a second instrument's list answers a plain HTTP 500 (not ER005).
- Steps: (i) run `statements`; read `ingest_runs`, the run's structured log/stats evidence, `financial_statements`, the cursor row. (ii) run `statements` again on the same world. (iii) assert the non-ER005 company's status.
- Expected: (i) **exactly one** reset for the stale company — from the persisted cursor to `EarlyDisclosureIndex`; the re-walk's facts are written; the reset is recorded **verbatim** (symbol, from → to, gateway code and body, in the run evidence/log and the stats/log line); the corrected cursor is persisted as the last emitted index. (ii) the second run makes **one** list call for that company with **no** stale reset and no ER005 loop (or, when the reset re-walk was itself honest-empty, the cursor row was cleared, so the second run starts from the early index and still makes one list call); the run is `failed` only for the non-ER005 company (a genuine source failure), never for the recovered one. (iii) the non-ER005 list failure is **not** recovered as an honest empty — it remains a source failure (no false reset).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-096 (the same run harness).

**TC-MDF-130 — A store failure after the walk emitted facts never advances a family cursor past them, the next run re-walks and writes them, and the replay duplicates nothing** (new v1.57, TKT-foundation-069)
- Traces: FR-MDF-003/004/005 (the three family lanes' incremental correctness), FR-MDF-010/011 (no silent gaps — the class this closes is exactly a silent gap), FR-MDF-016, `01` v1.5.84 §10.9.15/§10.9.11 · Level: L2 · Design: fault injection — a genuine store failure at three write points (the fact-table insert of each family) × the cursor state and the replay that follows; plus a state transition on a **pre-existing** cursor (advance refused, then completed)
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed, the test-local single-member bound (`ALFA` → VYK `companyId` `1001`); the 034 verified-route doubles (`/members`, `/disclosures`, `/disclosureDetail`), the family detail bodies (BİM `KP` 1138986, Tüpraş `SA` 1138796, the generic disclosure body); `RequestDelayMs = 0`; the failure injected by a PostgreSQL **trigger that raises on the fact table's `INSERT`** (`dividends`, `corporate_actions`, `kap_disclosures`), created and dropped by the test, so the walk really emits its facts and the store really refuses them — the window the residual described, not a stand-in for it
- Steps: (i) for each family (`dividends`, `corporate-actions`, `disclosures`) with the trigger active, run the job; read the exception, the fact table and the `coverage_metadata` cursor row (the run ledger is read for the record only — see the note below), then drop the trigger and run again; read the facts and the cursor; delete the cursor row (a rewind) and run a third time. (ii) separately on `dividends`: complete a run (cursor at `1138986`), add a second disclosure at `1138987`, activate the trigger, run; read the cursor and the facts; drop the trigger, run again.
- Expected: **(i)** every family throws a `DbUpdateException` carrying the injected marker — never a `succeeded`/`partial` result and never a silently empty run; **no fact row** is written and **no cursor row exists** (the old behaviour wrote the cursor first: red as `Expected: 0, Actual: 1`); with the trigger dropped, the next run re-walks from the early index and writes the facts (2 dividends / 1 corporate action / 1 disclosure), the cursor lands at `1138986`; after the rewind the third run re-emits the identical facts and reports `written: 0` with every fact `unchanged`, the row count unchanged — **no duplicate row** (acceptance (iv)). **(ii)** the failed incremental run leaves the cursor at **`1138986`** — the index of the facts that *were* written, never at `1138987`, whose facts the store never got — and the next run writes the two lost facts and only then advances the cursor to `1138987`. The ER005 stale-cursor reset (TC-MDF-097) is untouched by any of this and re-runs green: it is the **ER005** path only, and this window's recovery is the unadvanced cursor, not a reset (acceptance (ii)). Recorded, deliberately not pinned: with the trigger active the runner's own ledger write re-attempts the inserts EF still tracks and fails identically, so no `ingest_runs` row is written for that failed run — the pre-existing `Jobs.cs` posture (TKT-foundation-044's scope), which this case records rather than asserts, so a later fix there cannot turn it red.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-094 (the shared-walker family harness), TC-MDF-097 (the ER005 reset this leaves unchanged), TC-MDF-099/100 (the disclosures cursors that ride the same checkpoint).

## 16. Scheduler per-step bound (TKT-foundation-064, architecture v1.5.43 reserved)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only, fake clock — the 041/059/062 harness (`BuildStarvationHost`/the `RecordingJobRunner` double). The shipped defaults are asserted as constants (step 120 min, chain 4 h); the cases configure small bounds on the fake clock so the elapsed behaviour is deterministic. 041/059/062's existing cases are the non-regression contract.

**TC-MDF-098 — Each chain step is bounded on its own; a slow-but-healthy step is not hung, a hung step is cancelled and named, and the whole-chain ceiling still bites** (new v1.15)
- Traces: FR-MDF-009 (scheduler/retry machinery), FR-MDF-016 (pipeline health — the watchdog's honesty), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole), `01` v1.5.43/§10.9.17 · Level: L2 · Design: boundary analysis on two independent bounds (step vs chain) + fault injection
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed; the real scheduler engine on a `FakeTimeProvider`; the EOD/chain stream bound to `[prices, slow-step, tail-step]` doubles; the shipped `IngestionSchedulerOptions` defaults asserted in a constant case (step `120 min`, chain `4 h`).
- Steps: (a) a chain whose preceding steps consume ~40 min and whose slow step consumes ~60 min (total ~100 min) under the shipped defaults; (b) a step that never completes under a small configured step bound; (c) individually-healthy steps whose total exceeds a small configured chain bound; read `ingest_runs`, the mail double, the captured log events, and the retry rows.
- Expected: (a) **one** ledger row, `succeeded`, **zero** `RUN_HUNG`; the slow step's own duration is asserted against the step bound (red today: the 90-min whole-attempt bound fires mid-step — the healthy-chain-never-completes defect). (b) **exactly one** `RUN_HUNG` row naming **that** step, with `bound: "step"`, `maxStepDurationSeconds` recorded, one alert naming the step; the retry ladder starts from the attempt's end and the next attempt's healthy step lands `succeeded` with no second `RUN_HUNG`. (c) **exactly one** `RUN_HUNG` row naming the in-flight step with `bound: "chain"` and `maxRunDurationSeconds` recorded — the ceiling is not lost. Non-regression: `SchedulerTests` (TC-MDF-079 with its 10-min `MaxRunDuration`, 059's DR-1/2/3/5, TC-MDF-011/012, TC-MDF-081) and `EodStallWatchdogTests` re-run green, no assertion weakened.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-079 (the retry/watchdog baseline).

**TC-MDF-113 — The watchdog's post-cancel settle window: a queued continuation still yields one attempt/one row, a genuinely hung step is still named, and the grace is bounded, honest and configuration** (new v1.24, TKT-foundation-085)
- Traces: FR-MDF-009 (the retry decision must follow the attempt's true terminal state), FR-MDF-016 (pipeline health — the watchdog's honesty), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent — and no *duplicate* — ledger hole), `01` v1.5.59/§10.9.17 (amended: the re-check is an interval) · Level: L2 · Design: concurrency-interleaving analysis on the cancel window (queued vs inline continuation) + boundary analysis on the grace + configuration/constant guard
- Preconditions: migrated container; the real scheduler engine on a `FakeTimeProvider`; the 059 `RecordingJobRunner` double and the edge/chain harnesses; **doubles only, fake clock, no real `Task.Delay` bounds any case**; the shipped `IngestionSchedulerOptions` defaults asserted as constants.
- Steps: (a) the swallowing step **plus one `await Task.Yield()`** — its post-cancellation continuation is queued to the thread pool, as an adapter's own `await` is — under a 10-min `MaxRunDuration`: the bound expires and the step finishes inside the watchdog's settle window; read `ingest_runs`, the mail double, the captured log events, and sweep the ledger past the ladder. (b) a never-completing doubled step under a 10-min per-step bound with a configured 30 s `PostCancelSettleWindow`: advance the fake clock past the bound and the grace; read the row, the alert and the log, then walk the ladder to the retry's own instant. (c) assert the shipped default (5 s) and its configuration binding, its distance from the shortest retry rung (5 min) and both bounds, and the `0` opt-out (a hung step's verdict reached with no clock advance past a grace).
- Expected: (a) **exactly one** ledger row — the step's own `succeeded` row on `attempt: 1`, no `reasonCode`, **no** `RUN_HUNG` row, **no** `RUN_HUNG` alert, no retry, no exhausted alert (red on the pre-085 code: the queued continuation leaves the single synchronous re-check seeing a pending task, so the collection holds **two** rows — the success row *and* `RUN_HUNG` — deterministically, not as a flake). The DR-2 contract is asserted verbatim and nothing is deleted, skipped or relaxed; TC-MDF-079 DR-2 (the inline-resume half) is kept as well. (b) **exactly one** `RUN_HUNG` row naming the pre-cancel step with `bound: "step"` or `"chain"`, `maxStepDurationSeconds` and **`settleWindowSeconds: 30`**, one alert naming the step, the bound and the grace, the `Hung run detected` line saying the same, `finished_at` = the bound's instant (the grace never moves the record or the ladder), the retry ladder taking over from the attempt's end, and the next attempt's healthy step `succeeded` with no second `RUN_HUNG` — 064's ability to name and cancel a genuinely hung step is **not** weakened. (c) the default is 5 s and binds from `Ingestion:Scheduler:PostCancelSettleWindow`; `5 s < 5 min` (the shortest ladder rung), `< 120 min` (step bound) and `< 4 h` (chain ceiling); `0` disables the window (verdict with no advance, `settleWindowSeconds: 0`, no grace claimed in the error or the alert). Non-regression: 059's DR-1/2/3/5a/5b, TC-MDF-079, TC-MDF-081 (both cases), TC-MDF-098 (all cases), 062's stream-independence suite, `EodChainTests`, `MorningChainTests`, `EodStaleServingTests`, `FundSchedulingCoverageTests` re-run green, no assertion weakened — the **only** harness delta is premise-preserving: hung-path cases that end in `RUN_HUNG` complete their tick through an overload that advances the fake clock past the settle window (the same premise they already make for the bounds; 064's TC-MDF-081(b) precedent), with no expected value changed.
- Residual (recorded, not asserted away): a cancellation-swallowing step completing *after* the window still lands its own row beside `RUN_HUNG`; the production runner writes through the attempt token, so the token-cancelled EF write throws and no second row is written. If it is ever observed live, the declared durable follow-up is the single per-attempt claim (architecture `01` §10.9.17, v1.5.59).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-079 (the 059 DR semantics this strengthens), TC-MDF-098 (064's bounds, which stay load-bearing).

## 17. Disclosures-lane throughput: the bounded bootstrap window and the market-wide feed (TKT-foundation-078, architecture v1.5.52)

**Lane posture.** L2, **doubles only, never a live gateway call**. The `/members`, `/lastDisclosureIndex`, `/disclosures` and `/disclosureDetail` routes are the WireMock doubles the 034/046/061/063 lanes already use; the huge-range and market archives are constructed from the measured live shapes (2026-10-24 probes recorded on the ticket: ASELS 1092199→1231017 = 138,818 positions ≈ 39 pages; a market page ≈ 48 dense rows reaching the head). Every run goes through the production `IIngestionJobRunner` over a migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL, pacing bound to 0, the fake clock at the run instant. The pre-078 behaviour is reconstructible in one place (`DisclosuresSourceAdapter.ResolveEarlyIndex` returning `EarlyDisclosureIndex`), which is what the red run pins.

**TC-MDF-099 — A cursor-less company bootstraps from the platform head's configured window, so one pass is finite; the window is configuration, and the bootstrap is provenance, never an emptiness claim** (new v1.17)
- Traces: FR-MDF-005 (disclosures ingest completes), FR-MDF-010/011 (achieved depth honest), `01` v1.5.52/§10.9.15 · Level: L2 · Design: boundary/throughput — page-budget exhaustion of the from-zero walk vs the bounded window
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed, one bound constituent (`ALFA`/`1001`); the `/members` + `/lastDisclosureIndex` doubles answer (`1231017`); the company archive double spans a **huge** index range (2,000 dense non-target rows at 1,000,000…1,001,999 — ≈41 pages when walked from zero, the run-208 shape — plus two target `ODA` disclosures inside the head's 5,000-position window at 1,226,100 and 1,230,900); `Ingestion:Disclosures:Feed = company`, `BootstrapLookback = 5000`, `RequestDelayMs = 0`.
- Steps: (i) no persisted cursor → run `disclosures`; read the run status/counts, the list requests, `kap_disclosures`, the cursor row, the run's structured log and `stats_json`. (ii) the same world with `BootstrapLookback = 200` (window start 1,230,817). (iii) assert the shipped default constants.
- Expected: (i) `succeeded`; the **first** list request is `head − 5000` (`1226017`) and **never** `0`; the walk stays inside the configured page budget (red: from zero the walk exhausts 24 pages inside the dense archive and writes **0** rows instead of 2); both in-window disclosures are written; the cursor is the last disclosure read (`1230900`); the bootstrap is recorded as **provenance** — a counted `bootstrapped 1 cursor(s) … ALFA … BOOTSTRAPPED` log event and a per-symbol stat (`written 0`, `failed false`, reason `BOOTSTRAPPED`) — and **no** log event claims the company has "no disclosures". (ii) the single list request is `1230817` and the one disclosure above it is written: the *value* decides the depth, not a magic number. (iii) shipped defaults: 5,000 (company), 1,000 (market), 24 list pages, 200 details.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-083/094 (the same run harness and stat shape).

**TC-MDF-100 — A persisted cursor always wins: one incremental page, no re-walk, no re-bootstrap; a stale cursor resets once to the bounded window, never to index 0; a reset that lands empty clears the row; and an incremental run never needs the anchor** (new v1.17, extended v1.19)
- Traces: FR-MDF-005, FR-MDF-010/011 (incremental cursor semantics unchanged — the 063 precedent), `01` v1.5.52/§10.9.15/§10.9.11 · Level: L2 · Design: state transition — persisted cursor → incremental page; stale cursor → one bounded reset; reset-empty → cursor cleared; anchor down → unchanged incremental page
- Preconditions: as TC-MDF-099; (i) a `kap-disclosures-cursor` row seeded below both target disclosures; (ii) a cursor row seeded **past the tail** (`1231018`) whose first request answers HTTP 400 `ER005`, with the window start and the final cursor index stubbed; (iii) the same stale row in a world whose archive ends **below** the window, so both the stale cursor and the window answer `ER005`; (iv) a persisted cursor with `/lastDisclosureIndex` answering HTTP 500.
- Steps: (i) run `disclosures` twice; read list requests, counts, cursor row, log. (ii) run with the stale cursor; read requests, counts, cursor; then run again. (iii) run with the stale cursor and an empty bounded window, read the cursor row, then run again. (iv) run with the anchor down.
- Expected: (i) first run: exactly **one** list request at the persisted index (no reset to the window, none to zero), the new disclosures written, cursor advanced to the last read index, **no** `bootstrapped` event; second run: exactly **one** request at the stored cursor, `written 0`, `unchanged 0` (the disclosures lane does not replay its cursor row — the 046 lane's proven semantics), no re-walk. (ii) exactly `["1231018", "1226017"]` — the stale cursor then **one** reset to the bounded window, never `0`; both in-window disclosures written and the corrected cursor persisted; a third run makes a single call at the corrected index with no second reset. (iii) `succeeded` with zero rows; requests exactly `["1231018", "1226017"]`; the cursor row is **removed** (never left to repeat the identical two-call reset every run), and the next run bootstraps with **one** call at the window — no repeated reset. (iv) `succeeded`, two disclosures written, exactly **one** request at the persisted cursor, no `bootstrapped` event: a missing `/lastDisclosureIndex` is a named warning (`BOOTSTRAP_ANCHOR_UNAVAILABLE`), never a failed incremental run.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-099.

**TC-MDF-101 — The market-wide feed is the normal pass: one cursor over `GET /disclosures?disclosureIndex={n}`, bound-only writes, counted out-of-bound rows, ER005 honest-empty, source-down still failed, a budget- or deadline-truncated pass resumes from its completed index, a short page is not the end of the list, and a non-positive deadline disables the bound** (new v1.17, extended v1.19, v1.20)
- Traces: FR-MDF-005, FR-MDF-008 (no fabricated identity), FR-MDF-010/011 (counted skips + honest depth), FR-MDF-016 (a pass cannot time out the chain), `01` v1.5.52/§10.9.15 · Level: L2 · Design: equivalence classes of a paged market walk (advance / honest empty / truth failure / budget / short page) + identity partition (bound vs out-of-bound)
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed, one bound constituent; `Ingestion:Disclosures:Feed = market`; the market archive double carries 60 dense rows from `head − 1000` (even offsets → the bound company `1001`, odd → the out-of-bound `9999`) plus one bound row at the newest index; details stubbed for bound rows only. (e) a market double that serves a **30-row page** at the window start, then 20 more rows at higher indexes, then the tail row — the live gateway's short-page shape. (f) six dense bound rows with a **stepping fake clock** (one minute per `TimeProvider` read) and `MaxWalkDuration = 00:10:00`. (g) the same six-row world and clock with `MaxWalkDuration = 00:00:00`.
- Steps: (a) run `disclosures` twice; read market list requests, counts, `kap_disclosures`, `quarantined_facts`, the market cursor row, logs. (b) the market list answers HTTP 400 `ER005` at the window start. (c) the market list answers HTTP 500 (Apinizer fault). (d) `MaxListPages = 1`, then a second run. (e) run the short-page world once. (f) run once under the 10-min deadline, read counts/log/cursor, then run again with a fresh clock. (g) run once with the deadline disabled.
- Expected: (a) `succeeded`; requests `["1230017", "1230066", "1230077"]` — the third is the walk's head check (this double's rows stop below `lastDisclosureIndex`, so the walk pages once more and stops on "did not advance" instead of on the 12-row page) — **no** `companyId` on any request; only bound rows written (one detail fetch each, zero quarantine); the out-of-bound rows are **counted** in `skipped` and logged as `OUTSIDE_BOUND_UNIVERSE` and the out-of-bound detail is never requested; the market cursor row is `scope = market`, no instrument, value = the last completed market index (`1230077`); the second run resumes with **one** request at `1230077`, writes nothing, and re-fetches **no** detail for its own boundary index. (b) `succeeded`, zero rows, zero quarantine, exactly one request, and **no** market cursor row persisted (an honest empty pins nothing and never resets backwards). (c) `SourceFetchException` → the run is `failed` with no rows, no quarantine and no cursor — the source-down posture is never softened into an honest empty. (d) the first pass stops at the one-page ceiling, logs `truncated True`, persists the last **completed** index `1230066`; the second run resumes exactly there and finishes at `1230077` — no page is skipped over and no run is unbounded. (e) the 30-row page is **not** terminal: 50 rows written across three requests (`["1230017", "1230046", "1230099"]`) and the cursor advances to `1230099` — a row-count stop would have abandoned the 20 rows above the short page (the live criterion's "within ~50 of `lastDisclosureIndex`" depends on it). (f) the walk crosses its wall-clock deadline after four details: `succeeded` with 4 written, `truncated True` logged, the market cursor persisted at the last **completed** index `1230020`; the next run resumes at `1230020`, does **not** re-fetch its boundary row, writes the remaining 2 and advances to `1230022` — a deadline stop loses no completed page and is never a `failed`/`RUN_HUNG` pass. (g) `MaxWalkDuration = 00:00:00` disables the deadline (documented semantics, matching the other bounds' ≤ 0 values): under the same aggressive clock all six rows are written and the cursor advances to `1230022`. The shipped-config guard (TC-MDF-099 (iii)'s config case) pins `DefaultMaxWalkDuration = 90 min < IngestionSchedulerOptions.DefaultMaxStepDuration = 120 min` and config ↔ code equality.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-099 (the same adapter/harness).

## 18. BIST holiday calendar — holiday-honest freshness and gated streams (TKT-foundation-048, architecture v1.5.44)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only. The calendar itself is pure date arithmetic over
configuration, so its cases need no container and no clock (`ConfiguredTradingCalendar` composed
through the real `TradingCalendarOptions`); the freshness and gate cases run against the L2 fixture
world, the production `market-snapshot` job and the real scheduler engine on a `FakeTimeProvider`,
exactly as TC-MOV-032 and TC-MDF-010 do. The holiday list is the **official Borsa İstanbul "Resmi
Tatil Günleri" Pay Piyasası publication** (2025 complete, remaining 2026 at the seeding date
2026-10-24): full-closure weekdays only — weekend-dated closures are already non-trading, and an
*arefe* half-day is a trading session, so neither is ever seeded.

**TC-MDF-087 — A holiday is a non-session: freshness reads it like a weekend, the calendar walks the last trading day correctly, and a gated stream explains its skip** (new v1.21)
- Traces: FR-MDF-009 (scheduler trading-day eligibility), FR-MDF-010/011 (honest achieved depth/freshness — the holiday extension), FR-MOV-001/002 (overview display freshness), FR-MOV-010/016 (last-known-good), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole), `01` v1.5.44/§10.9.13 · Level: L2 · Design: equivalence partitioning over the calendar (`weekend`, `holiday`, `holiday plateau`, `arefe`, `malformed`), state transition on the serve/gate paths
- Preconditions: for the calendar cases, nothing but `TradingCalendarOptions`; for the freshness cases, migrated container + FU L2 seed (T = 2026-10-06 Tuesday, T−1 = Monday 2026-10-05, T−2 = Friday 2026-10-02) with **Monday configured as a holiday**; for the gate cases, the FU §10 WireMock doubles and the scheduler engine on the fake clock.
- Steps: (i) assert `weekend ∪ configured holiday` non-trading, `LatestTradingDayOnOrBefore` across a weekend / a holiday Monday / a holiday plateau / a Fri–Mon closure cluster, an empty list behaving as the pure weekday calendar, the shipped list equal to the publication, the shipped `JobSchedules[].TradingDaysOnly` posture, and a malformed entry rejected; (ii) `GET /api/v1/market/overview` with Friday's snapshot served on the Tuesday clock while a stored `daily_prices` row is dated on the configured holiday; with only Friday covered; and with a newer *trading* session stored; (iii) the EOD chain's 20:30 occurrence on a configured holiday; the intraday per-job stream's four occurrences on a configured holiday and its next trading day; read `ingest_runs`, the WireMock journal, the mail double and the fact tables.
- Expected: (i) the holiday weekday is non-trading while its neighbours (including the arefe) trade; a holiday Monday resolves to the preceding **Friday**, a plateau to the arefe before it, the June 2025 cluster to 2025-06-05, and a trading day to itself; the shipped list is exactly the publication (weekdays only, no duplicates, arefes excluded); `intraday-quotes` is `TradingDaysOnly: true` while the macro/fund entries are not; a malformed date throws naming the value. (ii) **`stale: false`** with `asOf` on Friday in all three worlds except the missed-run one, where a newer stored **trading** session still yields **`stale: true`** — the calendar discredits non-trading dates only (red before: the holiday-dated row made Friday read `stale: true`). (iii) exactly **one** ledger row per gated stream per holiday — `job_code: "holiday-skip"`, `succeeded`, dated, `stats_json { gate: "trading-calendar", gateOutcome: "skipped", stream: "eod-chain" | "job:intraday-quotes", holiday }` — with **zero** source requests, **zero** facts written, **no** `RUN_HUNG` and **no** alert, while the next trading day runs at its own cadence. A **weekend** occurrence stays silent (the pre-048 contract, TC-MDF-011).
- Non-regression: `SchedulerTests` (incl. the amended `TC_MDF_011_Holiday_calendar_is_config`, 059's DR-1/2/3/5a/5b, TC-MDF-079/081/098), `EodStallWatchdogTests`, `EodChainTests`, `MorningChainTests`, `EodStaleServingTests`, `MarketEndpointsTests`, `IntradayQuotesTests`, `StockOverviewAndValuationEndpointsTests`, `MarketSnapshotTests`, `FundSchedulingCoverageTests`, `MacroIngestionTests` re-run green and unrelaxed. **One recorded contract amendment:** `TC_MDF_011_Holiday_calendar_is_config` asserted a completely empty ledger on a holiday — the silent-skip posture planner pin 3 replaces — and now asserts the single `holiday-skip` row plus "no job ran".
- Dependencies: TC-MOV-032 (the stored-prices freshness baseline this extends), TC-MDF-010/011 (the chain and its trading-day gate).

## 19. Metric definition versioning — per-row formula provenance (TKT-foundation-070, architecture v1.5.48)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only, the §11 harness: both jobs are driven through the production
`IIngestionJobRunner` over the FU §5 canonical universe in a migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL, with a
`FakeTimeProvider` supplying the run instant. The one thing a test cannot do is *change the formula* —
`CanonicalMetrics.Version` is a compile-time constant — so the "rows produced by a different
definition" state is produced the only honest way: at the DB level, exactly as
`MetricsRecomputeIdempotencyTests` already edits stored facts. No test-only hook exists in the
production code, and none is needed.

**TC-MDF-102 — A metric row records the definition that produced it: every write stamps the version, a stale/absent stamp is rewritten (values unchanged) and reported distinctly, a same-version re-run stays `unchanged`, historical rows are never reinterpreted, and the ledger records who asked for the run** (new v1.22)
- Traces: FR-MDF-013/014 (recompute mechanics + honest run accounting), FR-MDF-015/NFR-MDF-002 (append-only, one row per key, historical immutability), BR-MDF-009 (one definition per number — now recorded per row), FR-MDF-009/NFR-MDF-001 (the ledger names the run and its origin), `01` v1.5.48 §10.9.13 (definition versioning), test strategy §10 ("an intentional formula change requires updating the affected test cases and recording the change") · Level: L2 · Design: state transition — first compute → same-version re-run → stale-stamp re-run → settled re-run, on both derived-fact lanes; decision table over stored `formula_version` ∈ {absent (`NULL`), current, other}
- Preconditions: migrated container, FU L2 seed; `derived_metrics` / `sector_metric_medians` / `ingest_runs` cleared; a completed earlier trading day (T−1) computed first so historical immutability is observable; the run clock is a `FakeTimeProvider` at T and the re-runs advance it (+2 h, +4 h) so a rewrite is visible in `computed_at`; stored stamps are set with a direct `UPDATE … SET formula_version = …` (the production path cannot write a stale stamp by design).
- Steps: (a) run `metrics-recompute` for T and read `derived_metrics.formula_version`; re-run for T unchanged. (b) put T's and T−1's stamps to `NULL` (the honest pre-versioning shape), read the engine's write plan, then re-run `metrics-recompute` for T and re-read both days. (c) repeat with T's stamp set to a non-current version. (d) run `medians` for T, read `sector_metric_medians.formula_version`, set it to `NULL`, read the plan, re-run, then re-run again. (e) run `metrics-recompute` for T three times: through the scheduler's retry context, through the bare runner overload the admin trigger uses, and with an explicit request-level trigger; read the latest `ingest_runs.stats_json` each time.
- Expected: every freshly written row (both lanes) carries `CanonicalMetrics.Version`, and a same-version re-run reports `written: 0` with `unchanged` = the full row set (416 / 15) and **zero** `FormulaVersionRewritten` — identical inputs **and** identical version is still honestly unchanged. A `NULL`-stamped or different-stamped day is classified as `Rewritten` **and** `FormulaVersionRewritten` (values identical, definition moved — never counted as `unchanged`, never conflated with a fact repair), the re-run reports those rows as written, every row is restamped with the current version, and the values are byte-identical (only `computed_at` moves). The earlier day keeps its `NULL` stamp and its `computed_at` — no row is silently reinterpreted, and nothing is guessed or back-filled by the migration. The settled re-run under the same version is `unchanged` again in both lanes, and one row per key holds. The ledger reads `trigger: "scheduled"` for the context-carrying run, `"admin"` for the bare overload, and the explicit request-level value wins over the context — with the run's `written`/`unchanged` counts unaffected by it. *(Red before the implementation: `42703: column "formula_version" does not exist` on both tables and `trigger` absent from every ledger row — 5 failed / 4 passed.)*
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-088 (the same-day recompute contract this extends).

## 20. KAP public-site source — the politeness/ToS gate, the HTTP client and the honest-status contract (TKT-foundation-079, architecture v1.5.53 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only — the committed paced-capture set is the double source; **no live kap.org.tr call ever runs in CI**. Evidence base: the orchestrator's verified-live investigation 2026-10-25 (`.pipeline/plan/kap-public-source-investigation.md` — `POST /tr/api/disclosure/members/byCriteria` and `GET /tr/api/notification/export/excel/{id}` verified current and unauthenticated 2026-10-23). The public site is a **website, not a contracted API**: politeness is a first-class contract, not an operational nicety. This is a **separate source** from the VYK gateway (§10.9.11) — selectable by configuration, own cursors, own provenance (`kap-public`, never `kap`), and the two index spaces are **never mixed** (public `disclosureIndex` 1643141 ≠ VYK head 1231017 — verified different spaces).

**TC-MDF-103 — The public-site client is a polite, configurable, honestly-statused source: pacing between every request, a realistic User-Agent, bounded work and wall clock, never parallel, and the three honest statuses keep their meanings** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (source reachability), FR-MDF-008 (provenance — the evidence class is `kap-public`), FR-MDF-012 (isolation), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole), `01` v1.5.53/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: configuration equivalence + state transition over the honest-status paths (`ok`, `honest-empty`, `quarantine`, `source-down`)
- Preconditions: the committed capture set in the `RealSourceCatalog` convention (honestly labeled Captured/Constructed, capture dates recorded): a robots.txt body captured verbatim, a byCriteria list body (the committed ASELS `disclosureClass="FR"` **10-row** shape — `subjectList: []`; the investigation's 3-row result was the `subjectList=["Finansal Rapor"]` view; *delivery-planner-applied correction 2026-10-25, citing the committed `kap-public-bycriteria-asels-fr` body — the precondition's row count was stale, test-planner confirmation pending*), an excel-HTML report body (bounded trim of disclosure 1643141, real values kept, honestly labeled — the VykDetailCapture precedent), an identity-resolution body, and the OQ-1 market-wide probe result; a WireMock double at the public-site base; a fake clock.
- Steps: (i) **config wiring** — the new public-site options section binds (BaseUrl default `https://www.kap.org.tr`, `RequestDelayMs` default **3000**, a realistic `UserAgent`, list-page cap, detail cap, per-item detail timeout, walk deadline strictly below `MaxStepDuration`) and the shipped constants are pinned as config-equivalence assertions; (ii) **pacing** — on the fake clock, every consecutive request pair (list→detail, detail→detail, company→company) is separated by ≥ the configured delay, and the delay is configuration (a 200 ms bound produces 200 ms gaps); (iii) **request shape** — the client sends the realistic User-Agent header and the fixture-faithful byCriteria POST body (`fromDate`/`toDate`/`disclosureClass`/`mkkMemberOidList`), and the excel detail is fetched by `disclosureIndex`; (iv) **honest statuses** — a transport/5xx list failure is a source failure (run `failed`, no rows, nothing fabricated); a 200 body that fails shape parse quarantines `UNPARSEABLE_PAYLOAD` with a verbatim body excerpt (the content-type lies — `application/vnd.ms-excel` serving HTML — so detection is by shape, never by declared type); an empty result window is an honest empty (skip with a named reason, no cursor moved backwards); (v) **boundedness** — the walk obeys its page/work caps and its wall-clock deadline; a truncated pass persists the last completed unit and the next run resumes exactly there (the 078 `WalkBudget` semantics); (vi) **never parallel** — the WireMock journal shows strictly sequential, never overlapping, requests.
- Expected: every pin holds; red before implementation (no public-site client exists). The evidence class `kap-public` is the client's `SourceName` from the first request — never `kap`.
- Non-regression: the VYK lanes (`VykStatementsSourceTests`, `StatementsAndEventsIngestionTests`, `VykCompanyDirectoryTests`) re-run green unchanged — this ticket adds a source, it does not touch the VYK wiring.
- Dependencies: none.

**TC-MDF-104 — The ToS/robots gate and the OQ-1 market-wide probe are recorded evidence, and the gate is checkable, not prose** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-008 (provenance/evidence recording), NFR-MDF-001, the investigation's OQ-1/OQ-2 · Level: L2 for the recorded-evidence and gate assertions; the captures themselves are the orchestrator's paced live steps recorded verbatim · Design: evidence-recording + a refusal path
- Steps: (i) the captured robots.txt body is byte-present in the committed catalog and its ToS judgement is recorded verbatim on the ticket + `01` §10.9.19 (the text, and the pinned reading of it); (ii) the OQ-1 probe result — one paced byCriteria call with an **empty** `mkkMemberOidList` — is committed and honestly labeled with whatever it showed (works / errors / empty), because TKT-foundation-084 is gated on that evidence; (iii) the lane has a checkable refusal posture: a configured/recorded `forbidden` ToS posture makes the client refuse to issue requests (a test seam exercises the refusal), so the gate is real behavior, never a silently-ignored note.
- Expected: the evidence is in the repo, the refusal path is tested, and — if the recorded posture forbids automated access — the lane stops and surfaces to the user rather than proceeding.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-103 (the same harness).

## 21. Public-site company identity — the mapping is stored, never assumed (TKT-foundation-080, architecture v1.5.54 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only (the committed identity capture is the double). The public site's company oid (e.g. ASELS `4028e4a1413b7ef401413bc2251e0047`) is a **third identifier space** next to VYK's numeric `companyId` (ASELS `866`) and our `InstrumentId` — the mapping between them is **stored, never derived or assumed** (the investigation's OQ-4).

**TC-MDF-105 — Public-site oid ↔ instrument mapping: stored with provenance, three identifier spaces never conflated, unmapped = named + counted skip** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (company-scoped fetch), FR-MDF-008 (provenance), FR-MDF-012 (isolation — the 046 `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` doctrine, extended to this source), `01` v1.5.54/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: state transition over `resolved` / `unmapped` / `source-down`, plus the space-isolation invariant
- Preconditions: the committed identity-resolution capture; a migrated container; the bound universe (XU100) seeded.
- Steps: (i) resolution binds each bound symbol to its public oid through the committed shape and **persists** the mapping (own store, own provenance and asOf — a stored table, not an in-memory assumption); (ii) a bound symbol with no mapping is skipped, named (the 044 reason-code vocabulary, a public-source variant reason), counted in per-symbol stats, and never fetched — the loop continues; (iii) the **space-isolation invariant**: nothing ever writes a VYK `companyId` where a public oid belongs or vice versa (asserted directly against the store — ASELS's public oid and VYK id 866 coexist as recorded facts about different spaces, never as translations of each other); (iv) re-run is idempotent — identical resolution input leaves the mapping rows `unchanged`; (v) an identity-source transport failure degrades honestly (the run reports the failure; no fabricated mappings).
- Expected: as pinned; the ASELS fixture resolves end-to-end and the three-space table records all three identifiers for it.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-103 (079's client/config).

## 22. The public-site FR report decoder — excel-HTML tables onto the canonical statements, including the cash-flow statement (TKT-foundation-081; supersedes TKT-foundation-051)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only — the decoder's entire input is the committed excel-HTML capture set. **Supersession record (v1.23):** TKT-foundation-051 pinned its capture phase on the VYK dev gateway, but 051's own verified evidence shows that archive carries **no cash-flow statements at all** (72 IS / 84 BS / 74 EQ, zero CF) — the capture set it waited for cannot exist there. The public-site excel-HTML report carries the full indirect-method CF statement (`Nakit Akış Tablosu (Dolaylı Yöntem)` — the 051 unblock, investigation-verified). 051 is therefore **superseded by TKT-foundation-081** and its goal lands here as **TC-MDF-108**. **Planning defect repaired:** the TC-MDF-089/090 IDs 051 referenced were **never authored** in this plan (the v1.12 precedent class); the cases are authored here under new IDs, and 089–091 remain permanently unused. The VYK `ConceptMap` CF extension stays honestly impossible without real VYK CF shapes (never guessed spellings) — if production VYK access ever arrives carrying CF data, that is a new ticket from real captures.

**TC-MDF-106 — Header/metadata decode: report identity, period coordinates, presentation unit and currency, statement typing** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (statement ingest — decode), FR-MDF-008 (provenance), `02` §3.1 (unit/currency normalization inputs), `01` v1.5.55/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: specification-based decode of the captured header fields; equivalence over the period coordinates
- Preconditions: the committed excel-HTML capture (ASELS 2026-06 FR, disclosure 1643141, bounded trim with real values).
- Steps: decode `Gönderim Tarihi`, `Bildirim Tipi`, `Yıl`, `Periyot`, `Sunum Para Birimi` (**`1.000 TL`** → `SourceUnitScale = thousands`), `Finansal Tablo Niteliği` (Konsolide) and the `Cari Dönem`/`Önceki Dönem` period ends into the statement coordinates; each report table selects its statement type (IS/BS/CF/EQ) by its captured heading; the comparative period lands as its own statement row with its own period end.
- Expected: coordinates, unit scale, currency and typing decode exactly from the capture; a header the decoder cannot attribute is a counted honest skip, never a defaulted value.

**TC-MDF-107 — Line-item honesty: every decoded value traceable to its table/label; Turkish number formatting pinned by real fixtures; unattributable = honest-null; unmapped = `OTHER_*`** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002, FR-MDF-008, FR-MDF-012 (quarantine), `02` §6.1 (concept-map doctrine — extended to the HTML label space), `01` v1.5.55/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: the §8 parsing-fidelity posture applied to HTML tables — capture-first, never guessed
- Preconditions: the committed capture set; the existing canonical chart (`StatementMapper`) unchanged in its contract.
- Steps: (i) the label→canonical mapping is built **only** from labels present in the committed captures (the map's provenance is recorded — which capture supplied which spelling); (ii) Turkish number goldens: `1.234,56` and the `1.000 TL` presentation unit parse to the exact decimals (period thousands / comma decimal), pinned by fixture values from the capture; (iii) a captured label the map lacks lands as `OTHER_*` (recorded, ignored by metrics — the honest-gap doctrine); (iv) a value that cannot be attributed to a table+label is an honest-null drop with a counted reason — never a guess, never zero; (v) the duplicate-label rule is pinned from the capture's own behaviour (equal duplicates collapse; unequal duplicates quarantine, the TC-MDF-065 doctrine); (vi) every decoded line item's provenance answers "which table, which label" from the stored row.
- Expected: as pinned; existing statement suites (`RealSourceStatementsTests`, `StatementNormalizationTests`, `StatementVersioningTests`) re-run green with pre-listed additive deltas only (the new decoder is additive — the VYK/XBRL paths are untouched).

**TC-MDF-108 — The cash-flow statement decodes and FCF computes end-to-end — the 051 goal, re-pointed at this source** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-003 (CF statements), FR-RES-005..018 (the SNA/FCF display supply), FR-VAL-001 (DCF baseline — FCF), `CanonicalMetrics` FCF v1.3 (OCF − |CAPEX|, NULL-if-missing), `01` v1.5.55/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: end-to-end decode→map→metric over the captured CF table
- Preconditions: the committed CF table (`Nakit Akış Tablosu (Dolaylı Yöntem)` with the operating total `İŞLETME FAALİYETLERİNDEN NAKİT AKIŞLARI` and its before-tax subtotal `Faaliyetlerden Elde Edilen Nakit Akışları`, the hedging note family, current + comparative columns).
- Steps: decode OCF/CAPEX (and the companions genuinely present in the capture — investing/financing aggregates only if actually there) with correct statement typing (`CF`), units and period coordinates; then compute FCF through the locked formula; assert the missing-either → NULL path; unmapped CF concepts land `OTHER_*` honestly.
- Expected: CF-statement `fin_line_items` rows exist with `OCF`/`CAPEX` canonical codes (red today: zero CF rows anywhere); FCF computes where both exist and is NULL where either is missing (never zero-for-missing); the stock pages' SNA column populates for covered companies and shows the honest empty elsewhere. **Recorded observation duty (not scope):** if the captured report carries evidenced share-count lines (pay adedi), the finding is recorded and routed to TKT-foundation-065 — never consumed here.

## 23. Statements lane public-site mode — source selection, the bounded byCriteria walk, own cursors, and the two-source coexistence rule (TKT-foundation-082, architecture v1.5.56 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only, through the production `statements` job runner. The public-site lane is **selected by configuration** (`Ingestion:Statements:Source`); the VYK adapter stays wired and working (two-source discipline — switching back must be a config change, never a rewrite). The public list is **date-windowed** (`fromDate`/`toDate`), not index-paged: the cursor is a date watermark plus the seen-index set within the source's own space.

**TC-MDF-109 — The walk: config-selected source, per-company isolation, bounded date-windowed walk, truncation/resume, own cursors — and the two index spaces never mix** (new v1.23)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (company-scoped statements ingest), FR-MDF-010 (backfill/achieved depth honest), FR-MDF-011 (coverage metadata), FR-MDF-012 (isolation), NFR-MDF-001, `01` v1.5.56/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: the 078 `WalkBudget` pattern re-applied — state transition over `incremental` / `bootstrap` / `truncated` / `isolated-skip` / `source-down`
- Preconditions: WireMock doubles for byCriteria + excel detail; the production job runner; the identity store from §21 seeded; a migrated container.
- Steps: (i) `Source = kap-public` routes the statements job through the public-site adapter (default stays the incumbent source — flipping the shipped config is a recorded decision, never a silent default change); (ii) per-company walk: one company's unfetchable detail is a quarantine-and-continue skip with the verbatim body, counted, run `partial` (the 063 doctrine); an all-isolated zero-write run is `partial`, never `failed`; a genuine source-down is `failed`; (iii) the walk is bounded in work and wall clock with truncation+resume exactly per the 078 semantics; (iv) cursors live in **own** coverage-metadata rows (distinct data-type names) and a public-site `disclosureIndex` never appears in a VYK cursor row nor a VYK index in a public-site row — asserted directly; (v) backfill windows: a configured lookback (e.g. `fromDate` N months back) is recorded as achieved-depth provenance, never as "no statements exist" for what it did not walk.
- Expected: as pinned; the VYK statements suites re-run green unchanged (source selection is additive).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-103/105 (the client and identity store), TC-MDF-106/107/108 (the decoder).

**TC-MDF-110 — Two-source coexistence and provenance: both sources' facts coexist, the evidence class is answerable per row, and period overlap is a named, counted honest skip** (new v1.23; overlap-read clause split out to TC-MDF-111 in v1.28)
- Traces: FR-MDF-008 (provenance — `kap` vs `kap-public`), FR-MDF-011 (honest coverage across sources), FR-MDF-015/NFR-MDF-002 (historical immutability), `01` v1.5.56/§10.9.19 (the coexistence rule this case enforces) · Level: L2 · Design: invariant testing over the mixed-source world
- Preconditions: a seeded world holding VYK FY2023 facts (`SourceRef = kap`) and public-site FY2025/2026 facts (`SourceRef = kap-public`) for the same instruments.
- Steps: run the serving/metrics read paths over the mixed world; inspect `financial_statements`/`fin_line_items` provenance and the served financials payload.
- Expected: both sources' rows coexist (no deletion, no in-place overwrite across sources); for a period both sources cover, the public-site fact is **not written** and the overlap is surfaced as a named, counted `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` finding — the incumbent VYK row is retained as-is and as-of queryable, nothing is overwritten, nothing is swallowed silently, and no false `restated` flag is raised (a cross-source fact is never recorded as a restatement of the other source's filing); every row answers its evidence class; a metrics recompute over the mixed world reports per-source counts honestly. *(Amended v1.28: the overlap **read** preference this skip defers is TC-MDF-111's (§24) — the split moved the clause, it did not weaken it; the coexistence, provenance and no-cross-source-overwrite assertions above are unchanged.)*
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-109.

## 24. Coverage and downstream recompute on current data (TKT-foundation-083, architecture v1.5.57)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only — this is the acceptance lane for the user's goal (current fundamentals served), not a new ingest mechanism: the jobs exist; the ticket verifies and repairs what the mixed-source world actually does to them. Investigation-first: pin the observed gap before fixing, the 043/044 convention.

**TC-MDF-111 — Mixed-source recompute honesty: current fundamentals computed from the new lane's facts, every row stamped by the formula version, coverage honestly reported, market P/E honest — and the deferred overlap read preference** (new v1.23; the overlap read-preference clause split out of TC-MDF-110 is added here in v1.28)
- Traces: FR-MDF-013/014 (metrics recompute mechanics + honest accounting), FR-MDF-015 (append-only history), FR-MDF-011 (coverage), FR-MDF-008 (provenance — the overlap read preference), FR-MOV-006/BR-MOV-010 (market P/E — the honest-null posture), FR-RES-005..018 (served financials), `01` v1.5.57/§10.9.13+§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: end-to-end over the production recompute/median/snapshot jobs on the seeded mixed world (extended v1.28: plus the statement store's `source_ref`-in-key migration and the served read preference)
- Preconditions: the §23 mixed world; **TKT-foundation-070 merged** (the `formula_version` machinery this case stamps through); **the statement store's natural key includes `source_ref`** — one forward-only migration landed by this ticket's scope (`FinancialStatementStore`, the persistence entities/`DbContext`, one migration; `src/Api/Stocks/` for the read preference in `Pick`). TC-MDF-110's `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` skip **stands in 083** *(corrected v1.34, USER DECISION DR-1 option (ii), 2026-10-26)*: with the source in the key, two rows for the same period from two sources **can** coexist as distinct facts — storable, and served by the pinned preference wherever both rows exist (the hand-seeded proof this case runs) — but the production write of an overlapped public-site fact stays withheld by the lane exactly as TC-MDF-110 pins it, and is a recorded residue routed to a future **overlap-supersession slice** (candidate follow-up, not created).
- Steps: run `metrics-recompute`, `medians` and `market-snapshot` over the mixed world; read `derived_metrics`, `sector_metric_medians`, coverage metadata and the served stock-financials/screener payloads. **Overlap read preference (added v1.28) — the mixed-world read verification:** with `source_ref` in the natural key, seed the mixed world so both sources can hold the same period, then (i) both sources present for a period → the served financials payload selects the public-site current fact per the pinned preference; (ii) public-site absent for a period → the VYK fact is served; (iii) an as-of query reaches both sources' rows (VYK retained and as-of queryable — the preference never deletes or masks it).
- Expected: current-period metrics (margins, ROE, FCF/SNA, growth) compute from the public-site facts per the coexistence rule; every written/rewritten row carries 070's current `formula_version` (the recompute provenance is stamped, not inferred); coverage metadata reports per-source achieved depth; **market P/E and per-share metrics stay honestly null where share counts are unresolved** (the 065 Option-A posture — a `currentCapital` figure is never divided into a count); the served financials table shows the current periods with period-type-aware labels (057) and no 2023-only row is presented as current. **Overlap read preference (added v1.28):** the pinned preference rule decides the read — public-site current wins for the periods it covers; VYK rows retained and as-of queryable; the served payload selects by that preference (`StockFundamentalsEndpoints.Pick`) — the rule is recorded in `01` §10.9.19 and is never a silent latest-write and never an order-nondeterministic tie-break between equal-version rows.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-110; TKT-foundation-070 merged.

## 25. The market-wide recent-disclosures feed — the OQ-1 gate is open, and the feed is implemented against the probe (TKT-foundation-084, architecture v1.5.85 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** L2, doubles only. **The gate, recorded closed by evidence:** the market-wide shape of byCriteria (empty `mkkMemberOidList`) was OQ-1's unverified precondition; 079's committed probe (`RealSourceCatalog.KapPublicMarketProbe`, captured live 2026-10-25) answers **HTTP 200 with 12 market rows** over `2026-10-24..2026-10-25` (newest public `disclosureIndex` 1687047, class mix FR 3 / DKB 4 / ODA 3 / DG 2), so market-wide **is** served, no alternative design was needed, and the probe body is the acceptance double. Every absolute value this section's case asserts (indices, dates, classes, ticker codes) is the capture's own.

**TC-MDF-112 — The feed, delivered on the open OQ-1 evidence: date-watermarked cursor, dedupe within the public index space, bound-only writes, honest empty, and the VYK disclosures lane untouched** (new v1.23; gate closed v1.58)
- Traces: FR-MDF-005 (disclosures ingest), FR-MDF-008 (provenance `kap-public`), FR-MDF-011/012, NFR-MDF-001, `01` v1.5.85/§10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: state transition over `new` / `seen` / `out-of-bound` / `honest-empty` / `source-down`
- Preconditions: the committed market-wide probe body as the double; the identity store from §21; a migrated container.
- Steps: (i) the feed pages recent market disclosures from one date-watermarked cursor (the public list is date-windowed — the watermark, not an index, is the cursor; the seen public `disclosureIndex` set dedupes within the source's own space); (ii) only bound constituents are written; the rest are counted with the out-of-bound reason and never detail-fetched; (iii) an honest empty window persists no backwards cursor; a source-down is `failed`; (iv) every written row carries `SourceRef = kap-public` and the public `disclosureIndex` — never a VYK index, never the VYK lane's rows or cursurs (the 078 market feed and this feed coexist as separate sources); (v) re-run is incremental from the watermark with no re-read of the seen set.
- Expected: as pinned, on the open OQ-1 evidence (implemented as six cases in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicDisclosuresFeedTests.cs`, each tagged `TC_MDF_112`; the record is v1.58). Had the probe shown market-wide unserved, the ticket would have recorded the finding verbatim and blocked — no alternative design was to be invented.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-103/104/105; serializes behind TKT-foundation-069 (shared `src/Ingestion/Disclosures/` files).

## 26. The 065 contract half — `paidInCapital` under its own name, the price-derived `sharesOutstanding`, and the DCF per-share divisor (TKT-foundation-086, architecture v1.5.60 §10.9.11)

**Lane posture.** Both cases are **L2** and **doubles-only**. The doubles are the **committed** İş Yatırım
price captures (`RealSourceCatalog.PricesAsels` / `PricesThyao` / `PricesGaran` / `PricesFroto` /
`PricesEbebk`, the 2026-10-16 capture window 2026-10-06..08) and the committed fixture universe — a
capture that is missing is a **blocker report, never a fetch**. Every negative case is derived by
**mutating the decoded capture in memory** (`with { MarketCap = null }`, a zeroed close, an older date),
never by inventing a body, and **no test issues a live request to kap.org.tr or isYatirim**.

**USER DECISION D5 (2026-10-26) is the source rule this section implements:** the honest count is
**market cap ÷ close** from the daily payload the prices lane already fetches — both operands arrive in
the same row (`PD` and `HGDG_KAPANIS`), both are recorded, and a quotient that is not integral is
**withheld and counted, never rounded**. The alternatives were pinned and rejected for this slice: the
KAP-report count rows (no committed capture evidences one) and the nominal divisor (065 Option B — no
evidenced nominal). **Precedence, pinned:** a statement-reported `SHARES_DILUTED` count supersedes the
derived one when evidenced; that read path exists today and stays first — the derived lane supplies
only where the statement is silent. **The rejected divisor path stays rejected:** no `/memberSecurities`
`currentCapital` division, no assumed 1 TL nominal. Per-share metrics and market P/E consumption stay
excluded (BR-MOV-010); `fin_line_items` is never written from the price lane (UPSTREAM-1 stays owed).

**TC-MDF-114 — The price-lane derivation: `PD ÷ close` integral-or-withheld, both operands + provenance + as-of stored, input-aware and monotonic** (new v1.30)
- Traces: FR-MDF-001 (the prices lane the derivation rides), FR-MDF-008 (provenance — both operands, source, as-of on every count), FR-MDF-011 (honest public surface), `01` v1.5.60/§10.9.11 (the `shares` lane, corrected) · Level: L2 · Design: decoded-capture arithmetic + store state transition over `inserted` / `updated` / `unchanged` / `withheld` / `stale`
- Preconditions: a migrated container; the committed price captures; the FU instruments. **The exact-capture evidence the case pins:** ASELS `PD 1,714,560,000,000 ÷ HGDG_KAPANIS 376 = 4,560,000,000` (and the 07-10/08-10 rows `1,590,300,000,000 ÷ 348.75` = the same 4,560,000,000); THYAO `400,890,000,000 ÷ 290.5 = 1,380,000,000`; GARAN `539,700,000,000 ÷ 128.5 = 4,200,000,000` (its 06-10/07-10 rows are **non-integral**: 4,199,999,902.306534 / 4,199,999,802.657694 — a ~100-share disagreement on a 4.2B count, and the rule withholds it anyway); FROTO is non-integral on all three dates; EBEBK is non-integral on every date through the decoder's own `HGDG_KAPANIS` (the integral `160,000,000` only appears by dividing the `HG_KAPANIS` fallback the decoder does **not** prefer for close) — so the captures themselves exercise both outcomes.
- Steps: (i) decode `PricesAsels` and assert the dated fact carries `PD` as its market cap (additive, nullable) alongside the pinned close — the same row, one operand pair; (ii) run the derivation over the decoded facts and read `price_derived_share_counts` back; (iii) re-run unchanged, then re-run with a mutated market cap; (iv) feed an **older** dated fact after a newer one is stored; (v) derive from a row with no `PD` (the `HG_*`-fallback variant and the canonical double both carry none), from a row with `close = 0`, and from the non-integral GARAN/FROTO/EBEBK rows; (vi) run the production `prices` job end-to-end over a single-member bound serving the ASELS capture, and assert the row the derivation wrote through the real decoder/adapter/job path.
- Expected: **(i)** the decoded fact carries market cap `1,714,560,000,000` with close `376` for 2026-10-06; **(ii)** exactly one row per instrument, `shares_outstanding = 4,560,000,000`, **both operands recorded** (`market_cap_raw = 1,714,560,000,000`, `close_raw = 376`), `price_date = 2026-10-06` (the as-of), `source_ref = "isyatirim"`, `recorded_at` set — the arithmetic is auditable from the row itself; **(iii)** an identical re-run reports `unchanged` and writes nothing (`recorded_at` unmoved), a changed market cap repairs the row in place and is counted; **(iv)** the older fact never regresses the row — the stored count and as-of keep the newer fact, and the refusal is named and counted; **(v)** a missing `PD` is a named, counted skip (`PD_MISSING`), `close ≤ 0` a named, counted skip (`CLOSE_INVALID`), a non-integral quotient **withheld and counted** (`NON_INTEGRAL_QUOTIENT`) with **no row written and no rounded guess** — GARAN serves its 08-10 count or nothing, never 4,199,999,902; no zero row is ever produced (the 031/052 doctrine), and a withheld instrument keeps any previously stored row (the as-of on the row is what makes a stale count honest) rather than being deleted or rewritten; **(vi)** the job-written row equals the store-level row for ASELS, and the run is `succeeded` — the derivation adds **no fetch, no source and no request**.

**TC-MDF-115 — The two sides of the contract and the consumer: `paidInCapital` (TRY) under its own name, the derived count as `sharesOutstanding`, the count-semantics web row, and the DCF `share_count` fact NULL-gated with statement precedence** (new v1.30)
- Traces: FR-RES-011/027 (balance-sheet supply), FR-MDF-011 (honest public surface — no money under a count name), FR-VAL-001/002 (baseline supply), UXR-VAL-009 (missing-inputs explanation), UXR-VAL-010 (fact provenance/as-of), UXR-RES-0xx (line labels), BR-MOV-010 (P/E exclusion — cited, unchanged), `01` v1.5.60/§10.9.11, `02` §3.1 + §6.1, `03` §3 · Level: L2 · Design: served-contract assertions over the production endpoint + the production baseline job + the SCR-005 component suite
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the `share_counts` store renamed to paid-in capital (forward-only migration); the derived store of TC-MDF-114; the web `StockPage` suite harness.
- Steps: (i) seed a `share_counts` row and a derived row for one instrument and read `GET /api/v1/stocks/{symbol}/balance-sheet`; (ii) read an instrument with neither; (iii) run `dcf-baselines` for a stock whose FY balance sheet carries **no** `SHARES_DILUTED` but which has a derived row, then serve `GET /api/v1/stocks/{symbol}/dcf` and `POST …/dcf/compute` with the served parameters; (iv) seed both a statement `SHARES_DILUTED` and a derived row and re-run; (v) render the SCR-005 balance-sheet section over the served `lines.sharesOutstanding`.
- Expected: **(i)** the `shareCount` block serves **`paidInCapital`** (TRY, the renamed store's figure) with its own as-of, **and** `sharesOutstanding` from the derived store with **its own** as-of — two named facts, no money under a count name and no count named as capital; the statement's `lines.sharesDiluted`/`lines.sharesOutstanding` figures and BVPS are untouched (golden stability); **(ii)** both fields are `null` with null as-ofs — honest absence, never `0`; **(iii)** the baseline's `share_count` parameter equals the derived count, its `canonicalFactRefs.share_count` records **which source supplied it** (`price-derived`) and the **row's as-of** (not the build date), the state is not `missing_inputs`, and the compute endpoint returns `fairValuePerShare` = the DCF model's equity value ÷ that count; **(iv)** the statement-reported count wins (the pinned precedence) and the ref names the statement source; **(v)** the SCR-005 row carries **count semantics** — a share-count label and a count unit, never the paid-in-capital label or a TRY unit — and stays an **honest dash** when the statement emits no `SHARES_OUT` line (none does today); and when both sources are absent `share_count` is in `missingInputs`, `params` carries no `share_count`, and **no fair value per share is computed** (UXR-VAL-009 — the compute path's existing `share_count ≤ 0` rejection is unchanged; nothing is divided by a guess).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-114; TC-MDF-092/093 (the store being renamed and the seam being extended).

## 27. The public-site identity-population trigger — one registered job, admin-triggerable, never a chain step (TKT-foundation-087, architecture v1.5.61 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** The case is **L2** and **doubles-only**. The identity answer is the **committed**
`kap-public-identity-ASELS` capture (2026-10-25) served by the WireMock double; the bound is the
fixture store's own index membership. **No test calls kap.org.tr**, and every request the pass makes is
**079's** client's — the job adds no `HttpClient`, no pacer and no parallelism, so the pacing,
single-flight, User-Agent and posture assertions stay 079's (§20) and are not re-proven here.

**Why this section exists.** 080's table and 082's mode were merged, but `KapPublicCompanyIdentityResolver.ResolveAsync`
had **no production caller**: `kap_public_company_map` never populated, and a live `Source = kap-public`
statements walk isolated every bound company as `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` and wrote nothing (the
blocker recorded on 080 and 082). The missing piece is the invocation surface — a registered
`IIngestJob`, which is exactly what the runner, the run ledger, the admin summary's
every-registered-job list (UXR-MDF-003) and the admin trigger (UC-MDF-002) enumerate. This case pins
that surface and the honest-status mapping it owes, **not** a new request path and **not** a
re-implementation of 080's resolver or store (idempotency and space isolation remain TC-MDF-105's).

**TC-MDF-116 — The identity-population trigger: a registered `kap-public-identity` job binds the statements lane's universe, runs the merged resolver unchanged through the production runner, and maps its honest statuses onto the ledger — admin-triggerable, never a chain step** (new v1.35)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (the company-scoped fetch's identity precondition), FR-MDF-008 (the stored provenance the pass writes), FR-MDF-012 (isolation — named, counted skips and a `partial` verdict), FR-MDF-016 (the run-ledger row), UC-MDF-002 (admin trigger), UC-MDF-004 (ops visibility via the admin summary), `01` v1.5.61/§10.9.19 (the invocation subsection) + §10.9.11 (the shares-lane scheduling precedent), `03` §8 (the job-code catalog) · Level: L2 · Design: registration/decision table over {registered in `IIngestionJobRunner.JobCodes`, a step of `EodIngestChain`/`MorningIngestChain`} × {bound universe: all resolvable, partly unresolvable, empty} × {identity source: answers, fails} × {re-run}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the production composition (`AddMarketDataIngestion`); the public-site double with `RequestDelayMs` = 0 and the ratified `AutomatedAccess = Allowed` (079's harness convention — the posture is asserted by TC-MDF-103(i), not assumed here); the statements lane's own `Ingestion:Statements:UniverseIndex` bound, with test-local index membership created by the case (XU030, a wider XU050, and an empty XU060) so no symbol list is hardcoded in the job or in an assertion.
- Steps: (i) read `IIngestionJobRunner.JobCodes` and both shipped chains' code lists from the production composition. (ii) Run the job through the production job runner over a one-company bound; read `kap_public_company_map`, the run ledger (`ingest_runs`) and the WireMock request journal. (iii) Repeat over a bound carrying one resolvable and one unmappable symbol. (iv) Repeat with the identity route answering HTTP 500. (v) Repeat with the configured bound empty. (vi) Run twice over the same bound (idempotency), then re-compose with a wider configured index and run again.
- Expected: **(i)** the job code `kap-public-identity` is registered **exactly once** in `IIngestionJobRunner.JobCodes` — the registry the admin summary enumerates and the admin trigger resolves a `{job}` through — and is **absent** from `EodIngestChain.JobCodes` **and** `MorningIngestChain.JobCodes` (admin-triggerable only, the shares-lane precedent; the chains byte-unchanged). **(ii)** the pass resolves the bound company through the committed capture and **stores** the mapping with its public oid, provenance, as-of and `recorded_at`; the run is `succeeded` with `written = 1` (inserted), `rewritten = 0`, `unchanged = 0`, `skipped = 0`; the ledger row carries the job code, that status, no `error`, the counts and the admin trigger value; and the pass issued exactly **one** identity request per bound symbol and **zero** byCriteria requests (no request path of its own, no new fetch). **(iii)** the unmappable symbol is **named** in the run's per-symbol stats under `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` (the one 044 spelling the VYK directory and the statements lane also record), **counted** in `skipped`, logged with the symbol and the reason, **never stored**, and never fetched beyond its own identity search;
