the resolvable company is still mapped and the run is **`partial`** — never `failed` (the source answered) and never `succeeded` (a green run must not hide a company with no identity). **(iv)** a 500 identity answer is a **`failed`** run whose result and ledger `error` name `SOURCE_FETCH_FAILED` and the symbol the pass stopped at, with **nothing stored** (no partial mapping, no fabricated oid, no empty success) and exactly one request. **(v)** an empty bound issues **zero** requests, stores nothing, resolves nothing, and is named in the log under `EMPTY_BOUND_UNIVERSE` with the index code — the honest named empty (a cold-start/misconfiguration gap), **not** an error status (no source failed; the cold start belongs to the EOD/morning chain and the statements lane names the same gap on its walk) and **never** a fabricated resolution zero. **(vi)** the identical re-run is **`unchanged`** with `recorded_at` unmoved and still one row per instrument (080's contract, exercised through this surface), and switching `Ingestion:Statements:UniverseIndex` to a wider index binds one more company with no code change — the bound is the statements lane's configured universe, never a job-local symbol list.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-105 (the mapping this pass writes); TC-MDF-109 (the statements walk whose precondition it satisfies). The live criteria — one paced run over the bound universe on the dev stack with resolved/unresolved counts verbatim and unresolved symbols named on the ticket (discharging 080's), then 082's open first-walk criterion — are the orchestrator's post-merge steps, never CI.

## 28. The public-site FR decoder's IS/BS coverage — the debt/cash core and the income ladder, capture-first (TKT-foundation-088, architecture v1.5.62 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, and the double is the **whole** input: four committed
excel-HTML bodies (bounded trims of paced captures — `kap-public-report-1643141` from §22 since 081,
plus `-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık, `-1561039` ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık and `-1648240` MİGROS 2026-06 from
this ticket). **No test calls kap.org.tr**; every negative case is a mutation of one of those bodies,
never an invented one. The map entries this section governs stand **only** on a committed body, and
each names the body that carries its spelling (`KapPublicLabelRule.Provenance`).

**Why this section exists.** The first live `kap-public` walk (2026-10-26/27) wrote 62 statements
across 7 companies: **60 CF / 2 IS / 0 BS** — and `GET /stocks/{symbol}/dcf` returned
`state: "missing_inputs"` naming `['debt','cash']`, revenue was null on the public-site financials
periods, and `metrics-recompute` wrote 0 rows. The decoder's diagnosis (recorded verbatim on the
ticket) found the reports **decoding in full** (146–174 BS lines, 66–94 IS lines per capture) with
only 2–6 canonical codes, and the statement rows being **quarantined whole** by
`DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` on `OTHER_*` passthrough codes — the balance sheet legitimately repeats
`Ticari Alacaklar`, `Peşin Ödenmiş Giderler`, `Finansal Yatırımlar` and `ARA TOPLAM` in its current
and non-current blocks. The fix therefore carries two halves, and this case pins both.

**TC-MDF-117 — The live captures' IS/BS rows store: the debt/cash core and the income ladder decode onto canonical codes from committed bodies, repeated passthrough labels are kept side by side instead of quarantining the statement, and canonical conflicts still quarantine** (new v1.36)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002/003 (statement ingest), FR-MDF-008 (provenance `kap-public`), FR-MDF-012 (quarantine/honest gaps), FR-VAL-001 (DCF baseline — the `debt`/`cash` facts), FR-RES-005..018 (served financials supply), `02` §6.1 (the concept-map doctrine over the HTML label space), `01` v1.5.62 §10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: a decode/store matrix over {committed body: TÜPRAŞ 2025, ASELSAN 2025, MİGROS 2026-06} × {statement: BS, IS, CF} × {duplicate class: repeated `OTHER_*` label, repeated canonical label} × {store outcome: all six rows written, row quarantined}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the production `FinancialStatementStore`; the four committed report bodies reachable through `RealSourceCatalog`; the label map after this ticket's extension.
- Steps: (i) decode each of the three new bodies and store each payload; read the stored `financial_statements`/`fin_line_items`. (ii) read TÜPRAŞ's FY2025 balance sheet from the store. (iii) read ASELSAN's FY2025 income statement from the store. (iv) inspect the TÜPRAŞ balance sheet's repeated `ARA TOPLAM`/`Türev Araçlar` labels in the decoded payload and the findings ledger. (v) mutate one real ASELSAN income-statement row's label onto the bottom-line label and store the result. (vi) read every `LabelMap` entry against the catalog body its provenance names.
- Expected: **(i)** each body decodes to six payload rows (BS/IS/CF × current + comparative), the report's own `Yıl`/`Periyot`/period type and `1.000 TL` unit agree with the capture, **no row carries `ConflictingItemCodes`**, `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` is **0**, every row carries canonical coverage, and the store writes **all six** (`Inserted = 6`, no rejection) — the live walk's 0-BS/2-IS symptom asserted away on the very bodies it produced; the equity matrix still drops whole (`NO_COLUMN_HEADERS`) and contributes no row. **(ii)** `ST_DEBT`/`LT_DEBT`/`CASH` store with the balance sheet's own values (thousands → single TL through the store's scale), so the DCF's `debt = ST_DEBT + LT_DEBT` and `cash = CASH` exist as stored facts with `kap-public` provenance; the balance sheet's canonical surface is **exactly** the six mapped codes — trade payables and every operating liability stay `OTHER_BS_*` (never debt) — and the current portion of long-term borrowings stays a labelled companion, never merged into `ST_DEBT` (a combined/aggregated debt figure is a modelling decision: recorded and routed, never guessed). **(iii)** the income statement's canonical ladder stores: `REV`/`COGS`/`GROSS_PROFIT`/`SGA`/`EBIT`/`FIN_EXP`/`PRETAX_INC`/`TAX_EXP`/`NI` with the capture's own values, and the filer's differently-spelled sub-total companion stays a separate line (no second `NI`, no lost presentation). **(iv)** a repeated `OTHER_*` label keeps **both** values under a counted, deterministic `{code}_n` suffix (`OTHER_BS_ARA_TOPLAM` beside `OTHER_BS_ARA_TOPLAM_2`, `…Türev_Araçlar` up to `_4`), one counted `DUPLICATE_LABEL_DISAMBIGUATED` finding per kept line naming the label and the code verbatim, **no** conflict and **no** quarantine, the codes are never canonical, and a re-decode is identical. **(v)** the same unequal-duplicate condition on a **mapped** code is unchanged: a counted `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` per column retaining both values, the first value kept in the line, `ConflictingItemCodes = ["NI"]`, and the store quarantines **that row only** (`CONFLICTING_VALUE`) while the other four rows land — the TC-MDF-107 (v) doctrine is not weakened. **(vi)** every map entry names a committed capture (`CaptureNames`), and the spelling it claims is present in **that** body; the debt/cash core cites the TÜPRAŞ annual body, the income ladder cites the ASELSAN annual body, no borrowings entry is a trade payable/lease/current-portion spelling, and the map is still a function of (statement, label).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-106/107/108 (the decoder contract this section extends, unrelaxed — ASELS 1643141 goldens, the `SUB_PERIOD_COLUMN` skip, the honest-null drops and the equity decode all stand); TC-MDF-110/111 (the lanes that feed this decoder, unchanged). The live criteria (one paced `Source = kap-public` pass writing IS **and** BS rows; the ASELSAN `1561039` annual decode; TUPRS FY2025 `fin_line_items` gaining `ST_DEBT`/`LT_DEBT`/`CASH`) are the orchestrator's post-merge steps, never CI.

## 29. The per-share DCF completes on public-site statements — `debt`/`cash` into the baseline, the financials terrace, and the derived lanes' stored-session day (TKT-foundation-089, architecture v1.5.63 §10.9.19/§10.9.13)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, and the double is again the **whole** input: the
committed `kap-public-report-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık body (088) is decoded by the production
decoder and written by the production store, then the production `dcf-baselines` job builds the
baseline it serves. **No test calls kap.org.tr or isYatırım**; the dividend/price side is the L2
fixture's own, and the price-derived count is a store row the case writes.

**Why this section exists.** §28 closed the *decode* half (IS/BS rows store). This section pins the
*consumer* half — the user's headline: with FCF (081/082), the price-derived counts (086) and the
balance sheet (088) all landed, the per-share DCF must stop answering
`state: "missing_inputs"`. The section is deliberately a **differential**: the same committed
capture, the same store and the same job, once with its IS/BS rows and once with only its CF rows
(the live pre-088 shape, 60 CF / 2 IS / 0 BS) — so "the state leaves `missing_inputs`" is a
falsifiable claim rather than a tautology. It also carries the one repair this ticket's
investigation found: the derived lanes were asked for a date that owned no stored prices and
reported a green `written: 0, unchanged: 0` — the 2026-10-26 live run's own reading — instead of
describing the latest real session.

**TC-MDF-118 — The per-share DCF completes on the decoded public-site statements: `debt`/`cash` enter the baseline with statement refs, `fairValuePerShare` is served, the CF-only world still leaves both missing, and the derived lanes describe the latest stored session rather than a priceless date** (new v1.37)
- Traces: FR-VAL-001/002/003 (baseline supply + the per-share value), UC-VAL-003 (baseline generation), FR-MDF-008 (per-fact provenance: source, as-of, restated), FR-RES-005..018 (served financials for `kap-public` periods), FR-MDF-013/014 (recompute over the mixed world), `01` §10.9.19 (public-site consumer note) + §10.9.13 (recompute doctrine, cited) · Level: L2 · Design: a completion matrix over {statement world: IS+BS+CF, CF-only} × {surface: `GET …/dcf` baseline state, `POST …/dcf/compute`, `GET …/financials`} × {derived lane: `metrics-recompute`, `medians`} × {requested day: a stored session, a priceless date}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; an active XU100 constituent carrying the committed TÜPRAŞ `1554106` capture decoded and stored through the production `KapPublicReportDecoder` + `FinancialStatementStore` as `kap-public`; a stored price at the build date and a `price_derived_share_counts` row (086's fallback divisor); the production `dcf-baselines` job run through the registered runner.
- Steps: (i) with the full IS+BS+CF world, run the job at the build date, read `GET /stocks/TUPRS/dcf`, then `POST …/dcf/compute` over the served parameters. (ii) read the stored balance sheet's own `ST_DEBT`/`LT_DEBT`/`CASH` and the CF statement's `OCF`/`CAPEX` beside the served values. (iii) with the same capture's IS/BS rows withheld (CF only), repeat the baseline read, the compute call and `GET …/financials`. (iv) on the plain fixture world, request `metrics-recompute` and then `medians` for a Saturday after the last stored price; read `derived_metrics`/`sector_metric_medians` by `as_of_date`.
- Expected: **(i)** the baseline's `state` is **not** `missing_inputs`, `missing_inputs` is empty, `params` carries the complete eight-parameter set (OQ-UX-001) with `debt = 37,385,875,000`, `cash = 107,237,211,000` and `share_count = 2,500,000,000` (thousand TL × the store's scale, the capture's own cells), each of `base_fcf`/`debt`/`cash` carrying a `canonicalFactRefs` entry with `source: "statement"`, `restated: false` and the baseline's own as-of, and `share_count`'s ref naming `source: "price-derived"` with its `asOf` set to the row's `price_date`; `POST …/dcf/compute` returns **200** with a positive `fairValuePerShare`. **(ii)** `debt` equals the **stored** balance sheet's `ST_DEBT + LT_DEBT` and `cash` its `CASH` — the served facts are that filing's rows, not a re-derivation from the response — and `base_fcf` equals the stored CF statement's `OCF − |CAPEX|`; every stored statement's `source_ref` is `kap-public`; the current portion of long-term borrowings is **not** in `debt` (088's routed DR-2 stays open: no guessed split, no widened debt definition). **(iii)** on the CF-only world the same job answers `state: "missing_inputs"` with exactly `['cash','debt']`, `debt`/`cash` absent from `params` while `base_fcf` is present, no `fairValuePerShare` served, and `POST …/dcf/compute` refused **400** naming `debt`; `GET …/financials` serves the public-site period with `revenue` and `netIncome` honestly **null** — the recorded red, reproduced. **(iv)** the requested priceless date owns **no** rows in either lane, while the latest stored session owns the full fixture contract (**416** metric rows, each stamped `CanonicalMetrics.Version`) and the medians' rows — a resolved run, never a silent zero (`Expected 416, Actual 0` is the pre-fix reading).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-117 (the decode this consumes, unrelaxed); TC-MDF-115 (the divisor precedence — statement `SHARES_DILUTED` wins, the price-derived count is the fallback); TC-MDF-111 (the mixed-world recompute lane this extends — its six cases stand unchanged); TC-VAL-005..008 (the DCF baseline/serve contract over the fixture world, unchanged). §11's TC-MDF-088 pins the same-day *write* honesty on a stored day; (iv) pins the *day resolution* that precedes it. The live criteria (a paced `GET /stocks/TUPRS/dcf` ≠ `missing_inputs` with `debt`/`cash` in `params` and their kap-public refs; the financials payload's revenue/NI for `kap-public` periods; `metrics-recompute`/`medians`/`market-snapshot` writing current-period rows with `formula_version` — 083's open criterion) are the orchestrator's post-merge steps, never CI.
## 30. Cumulative-vs-period (YTD) column semantics — one real interim report determines what the decoder stores, and the honest fail-safe when no column attests a period (TKT-foundation-090, architecture v1.5.64 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, and — as in §28/§29 — the double is the **whole** input:
the three captures 088 committed (`kap-public-report-1648240` MİGROS 2026-06 interim,
`kap-public-report-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık, `kap-public-report-asels-1561039` ASELSAN 2025
Yıllık) decoded by the production `KapPublicReportDecoder`, and for the store half written by the
production `FinancialStatementStore`. **No test calls kap.org.tr**; every negative case is a
mutation of a committed body (the declared-range text stripped, the `Periyot` line rewritten) —
never an invented report.

**Why this section exists.** The 2026-10-26/27 live run recorded four suspect FCF numbers
(TUPRS Q3-2025 ₺36.9bn > FY2025 ₺30.3bn, TUPRS Q2-2026 ₺120.5bn, KCHOL FY2025 −₺204.9bn) and one
hypothesis: Turkish interim FR reports carry **cumulative (year-to-date) columns**, so a "Q2"
filing's main column spans 01.01–30.06 and the decoder might be storing that YTD figure under the
interim's `periodEndDate` as if it were the discrete quarter. This section pins the **verified
answer** — the decoder is *not* doing that (it decodes the column whose declared duration is the
report's own, and names + skips the discrete-quarter column where a table has one) — and the **one
real defect** the investigation found: a report whose declaration is the *only* period evidence,
with no column attesting any duration, silently typed its instant (balance-sheet) rows `FY`, the
exact shape that could put a half-year `debt`/`cash` into the DCF's annual basis. The defect's
incidence is unmeasured (no captured report lacks declared ranges; the case is a mutation), and in
such a report the annual comparative balance sheet also types `Q` and drops out of the FY-only read.

**TC-MDF-119 — Cumulative-vs-period semantics: an interim's main column is its declared cumulative range (never a quarter), annual columns are twelve months and their FCF is the year, a cumulative interim is stored `Q` and unreachable by the FY filter, and a period no column attests is named `PERIOD_SEMANTICS_UNDETERMINED` and fails safe to `Q`** (new v1.38)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002/003 (statement ingest), FR-MDF-008 (per-fact provenance), FR-MDF-015/NFR-MDF-002 (historical immutability — no silent rewrite), FR-VAL-001 (the baselines this protects), `02` §3.1 (period semantics) · Level: L2 · Design: a decode matrix over {capture: MİGROS 2026-06 interim, TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık, ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık, MİGROS with its declared ranges stripped} × {surface: column declarations, decoded payload, stored facts + the consumer's FY filter} × {declaration: `6 Aylık`, `Yıllık`, unattested}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the four committed bodies served from `RealSourceCatalog`; for the store half an instrument row for the decoded symbol and the production `FinancialStatementStore`.
- Steps: (i) decode the MİGROS interim and read its IS/CF column headers, their declared ranges and the discrete-quarter columns beside them; compare the decoded `REV`/`NI`/`OCF` cells with the body's own raw cumulative and three-month cells. (ii) decode both annual bodies; read the CF column's declared span and its `OCF`/`CAPEX` cells; compute FCF through the locked formula. (iii) write the interim's payload through the store; read the stored `period_type`s and the CF line items, then apply the DCF baseline's own FY selection. (iv) decode the same interim body with every declared date range removed — once under its own `6 Aylık` declaration and once with `Periyot` rewritten to `4` (Yıllık) — and read the findings ledger and every statement's `period_type`.
- Expected: **(i)** the header reads `Periyot 2` / `6 Aylık` / 6 months; the CF and IS main columns are verbatim `Cari Dönem 01.01.2026 - 30.06.2026` with `PeriodStart` 2026-01-01, `PeriodEnd` 2026-06-30, `DurationMonths` 6 and no skip reason; **four** three-month columns (`Cari Dönem 3 Aylık 01.04.2026 - 30.06.2026` and its comparative, in the two IS tables) are present, none decoded, each named `SUB_PERIOD_COLUMN`; the **cash-flow table has exactly two columns** (current and prior, both six-month ranges) and **no** `3 Aylık` sibling, so the CF span rests on its own declaration; the decoded figures are the **cumulative** cells — `REV` 241.298.039 (not the three-month 124.399.203), `NI` 1.078.256 (not the three-month −644.936), `OCF` 17.837.860, `CAPEX` −6.297.863, FCF 11.539.997 — and **every** decoded line carries `PeriodStartDate` 2026-01-01 and `DurationMonths` 6, preserving the filing's own span on the decode result for the decode's own consumers and tests (the store and the served payload carry no span; the served label stays the `FY`/`Q` vocabulary). **(ii)** both annual bodies declare `Yıllık` / `Periyot 4`, every CF column is 12 months (2025-01-01 → 2025-12-31), the statements are typed `FY`, and their FCF is the year: TÜPRAŞ 49.464.734 − |19.118.339| = **30.346.395** thousand TL (₺30,35bn — the recorded FY2025 figure, *matching* the live number), ASELSAN 48.901.969 − |38.168.467| = **10.733.502**; every line again carries the twelve-month span. **(iii)** all **six** stored statements are `Q` (the interim's own `periodEndDate`s, no `FY` among them), the store's thousands→TL scaling applied (`OCF` 17.837.860.000, `CAPEX` −6.297.863.000), and the consumer's `s.PeriodType == "FY"` predicate — re-applied by the test over the stored rows, not by running the job — matches **empty**, so a half-year can never enter the annual basis (the served label is 057's surface: `Q` + `2026-06-30` still renders "2026 Q2", a routed gap, not a claim of this case). **(iv)** with no declared range the report is named exactly once per report (`PERIOD_SEMANTICS_UNDETERMINED`, count 1, detail saying no column attests a span) in **both** declaration variants — an interim's `Q` must rest on evidence too, not on the absence of a danger; **no** statement is typed `FY` (the pre-ticket decoder typed the half-year balance sheet `FY` here — the recorded red), the half-year **and** the 2025-12-31 comparative balance sheets are still stored as `Q`, and no IS/CF fact is invented from columns that cannot be attributed (`COLUMN_UNATTRIBUTED` findings instead).
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-117 (the IS/BS decode these captures come from, unrelaxed); TC-MDF-108 (the declared-`Periyot`-vs-column-duration cross-check — this section's (iv) is its *unattested* sibling: there the declaration contradicts attested columns, here nothing attests anything); TC-MDF-118 (the DCF consumer whose FY filter (iii) drives, unchanged); the annual goldens of §28/§29 hold byte-identically — cumulative semantics do not touch a twelve-month column. The four suspect numbers' accounting and the data-repair route (an explicit cursor reset + re-walk, never a silent rewrite; FR-MDF-015) are recorded on the ticket, not asserted here — (ii) proves FY2025 is the body's own arithmetic, and (i) disposes of YTD-vs-quarter column confusion in the decoder on the interim path; the TUPRS Q3-2025/Q2-2026 figures stay unattributed (no TUPRS interim body is committed) and KCHOL's FY-typed anomaly keeps a mapping/label defect as a live alternative to genuine magnitude.

## 31. Comma-coded identity matching — `cmpOrFundCode` as a token list, and the honest ambiguity when candidates share a token (TKT-foundation-091, architecture v1.5.65 §10.9.19)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, **zero live kap.org.tr requests**. The identity answers are the committed catalog bodies served by the WireMock double — the captured
`kap-public-identity-ASELS` shape and the two **constructed** comma-coded answers this ticket
commits (`kap-public-identity-garan-comma-coded`, `-isctr-comma-coded`; provenance `Constructed`,
each label stating which facts come from the run-301 observation and which are derived, because no
verbatim answer for these symbols exists in the repository and this ticket issued no live request).
Every negative case is an **in-memory mutation** of one of those bodies (a token renamed to make the
symbol a substring, padding and case changed, a code collapsed to a single token) — never an invented
body. The bound instruments are created by the case (the real companies run 301 named do not exist in
the synthetic fixture universe), so a case is always about a **bound** symbol.

**Why this section exists.** Run 301 mapped 22 of 30 XU030 companies and isolated 8 (BIMAS, ENKAI,
GARAN, ISCTR, KRDMD, SISE, VAKBN, YKBNK) under `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED`: the site answers those
companies with a **comma-joined** `cmpOrFundCode` (GARAN → `"garan,tgb"`, several candidates), which
the shipped exact-equality candidacy rule can never match. The rule becomes the **token** rule
TKT-foundation-046 established for the VYK directory — with one deliberate divergence: an answer
whose candidates **share the token** is `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` (named, counted, nothing
stored), **never** 046's last-wins, because this source's answer is a search result list in which
several real companies legitimately carry the token.

**TC-MDF-120 — Comma-coded identity matching: a token of a comma-joined `cmpOrFundCode` binds, several distinct candidates sharing the token are a named ambiguity that stores nothing, the rule is token equality (not substring, trimmed, case-folded) whose single-token case is unchanged, a mixed bound continues past the ambiguous symbol, and the production job reports the two outcomes under two different ledger words.**
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (the identity precondition of the company-scoped fetch), FR-MDF-008 (the stored mapping's provenance), FR-MDF-012 (named/counted skips — ambiguity as its own named outcome, never a guess), FR-MDF-016 (the ledger row); architecture v1.5.65 §10.9.19 (the comma-token subsection), §10.9.15/§10.9.11 (046's rule *class*, its last-wins posture deliberately not carried over) · Level: L2 · Design: the resolver's classification rule plus the production `kap-public-identity` job over it; the doubles are the committed catalog bodies.
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the bound symbols (`ASELS` from the fixture world, `ISCTR`/`GARAN` created by the case) as current constituents; the identity route stubbed per symbol on its exact request body (`{"keyword":"<symbol>"}`); pacing 0 and the ratified posture selected explicitly by the harness.
- Steps: (a) resolve a bound comma-coded single-company answer (`"isctr,isatr"` beside a fund hit spelling `isctr`); (b) resolve a bound answer carrying two distinct company candidates that both carry the token (`"garan,tgb"`, `"garan,gryo"`), then re-run it; (c) mutate the committed comma-coded double three ways — the symbol inside a longer token (`"xisctr,isatr"`), padded mixed case (`" isctr , ISATR "`), and a single token (`"isctr"`) — and resolve each; (d) resolve a mixed bound (exact single-token + comma-coded single candidate + ambiguous) in one pass; (e) resolve the committed single-token ASELS capture; (f) run the production `kap-public-identity` job over a bound of {ISCTR, GARAN} and read the run result, the ledger's stats and the store.
- Expected: **(a)** `Ok`, bound 1, resolved 1, inserted 1, no unresolved; the stored row is the **company's** oid (read out of the committed body), never the fund's; one identity request, zero byCriteria. **(b)** `Ok` with resolved 0 and one unresolved entry for the symbol under `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`, the refusal naming **both** joined codes; the map stays **empty** on both passes (no pick, no drift on re-run); the log names the symbol with that reason; two identity requests, zero byCriteria. **(c)** the substring mutation is `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` with nothing stored; the padded mixed-case mutation resolves to the same committed oid; the single-token mutation resolves and stores the same oid (the degenerate exact case). **(d)** bound 3, resolved 2, inserted 2, one unresolved (`COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`); exactly the two resolvable rows stored and the ambiguous instrument absent; three identity requests, zero byCriteria. **(e)** resolved 1, inserted 1, the committed ASELS oid and as-of — the 080 golden, unamended. **(f)** run `partial`, `written` 1, `skipped` 1; the per-symbol stat names the ambiguous symbol under `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` (never `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` — the two ledger words must be distinguishable) with `written` 0 and `failed` false; the stored mapping is the comma-coded company's; the ledger row carries the same counts and the same per-symbol reason; one identity search per bound symbol.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-105 (080's identity cases — (e) re-pins their degenerate case against the new rule, unrelaxed); TC-MDF-116 (087's job cases — (f) rides the same runner surface, unrelaxed); TC-MDF-082 (046's comma-split precedent, the rule class this applies). **Not** this section's business: the decoder (088/090), quarantine recovery (092). **The WAF/User-Agent default correction is this ticket's other half and is pinned where it already lived** — `TC-MDF-103(a)`/`(d)` in §20 now assert the **corrected** default (`…Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36 Degerli/0.1`, no parenthetical), together with the blocked-**marker** pins (`DoesNotContain("DegerliBot")` + `DoesNotContain("public-site reader")`) so the HTTP-666 spelling cannot return — not a punctuation pin, because the UA's browser tokens legitimately contain `(`; see the v1.40 entry and its v1.41 correction.

## 32. Quarantined-cursor recovery — a transport-failure detail quarantine is replayed once, bounded and persisted (TKT-foundation-092, architecture v1.5.67/v1.5.68 §10.9.19 — v1.5.67 landed the subsection, v1.5.68 amended it)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, **zero live kap.org.tr requests**. The list is the committed
`kap-public-bycriteria-asels-fr` capture and the recovered report the committed `kap-public-report-1643141`
body; the **transport failure** is a real connection that never delivers — WireMock answers the report
route after **30 s** while the lane's per-item bound is **150 ms**, so the client times out with **no body
ever read**, which is the live runs' own signature (`failed in transport`, `bodyReceived: false`). Nothing
is fabricated and nothing is called live. Every case keeps the deterministic fake clock **except** this
class: a per-item timeout is a real timer, so the transport-failure cases inject `TimeProvider.System` (a
fake clock that never advances would hang instead of failing) — recorded here so a later reader does not
mistake it for nondeterminism.

**Why this section exists.** Runs 302/303 (2026-10-26, recorded on 082) left ~180
`DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE` quarantines after transport bursts, AKBNK alone 102 in pass 1. The walk
marks a failed detail **completed**, so its window closes and the date watermark advances past it: under
the 063/046 terminal-quarantine rule those windows can never be re-walked, and three companies'
Q2-2023/H1-2024 history is silently bounded by a transient outage. TKT-foundation-092 applies the 063
stale-cursor-reset doctrine one level down: recovery is bounded, persisted and recorded, never infinite.

**TC-MDF-121 — Quarantined-cursor recovery: a transport-failure detail quarantine is replayed exactly once by a later pass (persisted across processes), a shape verdict and a status answer that carried a body stay terminal, the run's own completion log line names `recovered` and `still-quarantined` (a log line, **not** a stored ledger/stats field — `IngestResult`/`SymbolRunStat` are unchanged by this ticket, see v1.44), the replay is admitted by the pass's detail ceiling like any fetch, and a spent replay is never attempted a third time.**
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (statements ingest — the repair path), FR-MDF-010/011 (achieved-depth honesty — a recovered window updates against the same configured request), FR-MDF-012 (isolation — recovery and its terminal residue are named and counted), FR-MDF-015/NFR-MDF-002 (immutability — recovery writes through the ordinary store), FR-MDF-016/NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole in either direction); architecture v1.5.67/v1.5.68 §10.9.19 (v1.5.67 landed the quarantine-recovery subsection, v1.5.68 amended it), §10.9.15/§10.9.17 (the budget and deadline bounds, cited unchanged) · Level: L2 · Design: the production walker + `statements` job over the production paced client; the doubles are the committed capture plus WireMock's delayed-answer transport failure.
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; `ASELS` bound with its stored public oid; `Source = kap-public`; pacing bound to `0` in the harness (the ≥3 s production pacing is 079's own pinned contract, untouched here); the detail bound set to 150 ms and a real clock, because the failure class *is* a timeout.
- Steps: (i) pass 1 with every report route answering far too late (three transport failures), then pass 2 with the interim healthy and the other two still failing, then pass 3 with every route healthy; (ii) pass 1 with one report answering a 200 that is not a report (a shape verdict) and one dying in transport, then replay, then a healthy pass; (iii) pass 1 with one report answering a 500 carrying the gateway's own error body and one dying in transport, then replay; (iv) seed three transport-failure quarantines, then a pass with `MaxDetailFetches = 1`, then a default-ceiling pass.
- Expected: **(i)** pass 1 is `partial`, written 0, quarantined 3; the cursor's `quarantined` array holds all three, `retried` is empty, and all three are in the seen set with the watermark moved past them (that is exactly why the replay must be remembered). Pass 2 re-fetches each index **once**: the interim **recovers** — its facts stored under `kap-public` at its own period end, one report request in the journal — while the other two re-quarantine **terminally** (2 quarantines counted, 5 unfetchable rows in total, each carrying `"bodyReceived": false`) — and those five rows are **five**, not three: a failed replay appends a **second** `DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE` row for the same index, one row per attempt, neither deduped nor superseding the first (a deliberate recorded rule, 01 v1.5.68 §10.9.19, not a double-write defect); the run is `partial` (never `failed`, never green) and its log names `quarantine-replays 3 (recovered 1, still-quarantined 2)`; afterwards `retried` holds all three and nothing is pending. Pass 3 over a **now-healthy** source issues **zero** report requests for any of the three — the journal is written the moment a healthy route is asked, so `0` is evidence of "never asked", not of blindness. **(ii)** the shape verdict is `UNPARSEABLE_PAYLOAD` and the transport failure `DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE`; only the latter enters the cursor's backlog (the shape index is recorded neither as pending nor as spent), the replay pass reports `quarantine-replays 1 (recovered 0, still-quarantined 1)`, the shape index is fetched **exactly once in its lifetime** and the spent transport index never again. **(iii)** the 500-with-body is quarantined under the same word but is **terminal** — it is absent from the backlog, is never fetched again (`0` requests on a route that answers at once), while the transport failure beside it is the only replayable one and is replayed once. **(iv)** the one-detail pass spends exactly **one** replay (the backlog's oldest index, which fails again), reports `WALK_DETAIL_CAP`, journals **no** request for the recoverable interim (the ceiling stopped before it), leaves `retried` = 1 with 2 still pending and the position unmoved; the next default-ceiling pass drains the backlog — the recoverable report is written, the last tombstone is spent (`retried` = 3), nothing is pending.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-109/110 (082's walk/resume and coexistence cases — unchanged, re-run green); the 063 precedent (TC-MDF-096/097) whose "reset once, persist the outcome, never retried forever" shape this applies one level down. **Not** this section's business: the decoder (088/090), the identity resolver and its comma-token rule (091), the quarantine evidence format, and the root-causing of the transport bursts (a separate ops finding) — nor the live recovery run itself, which is the orchestrator's post-merge criterion, recorded on the ticket.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the four sub-cases are **(i)**–**(iv)** here, in v1.43, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.68, and that is the lettering the steps use. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator, not a second lettering: `TC_MDF_121_a_transport_failure_quarantine_is_replayed_once_and_the_recovered_report_is_written` = (i), `TC_MDF_121_a_shape_verdict_is_terminal_and_only_the_transport_failure_is_replayed` = (ii), `TC_MDF_121_a_non_success_status_with_a_body_is_terminal_while_the_transport_failure_replays` = (iii), `TC_MDF_121_the_replay_counts_against_the_pass_detail_ceiling_and_the_backlog_resumes` = (iv). No TC id is renamed.
- **Recorded harness limit (not a relaxation):** an aborted response is entered into WireMock's request journal only when it eventually completes, so a per-index request count is used as evidence **only** for doubles that answer at once. The still-failing replays are witnessed by the run's completion log line (attempts/recovered/still-quarantined) and by the persisted tombstones; and the abort delay is set far beyond any case's runtime, so no late journal entry can leak into a later pass's counts.

## 33. The statements-view label map — the industrial IS/BS/CF row set, the row-scope dimension and the Konsolide/Solo filing flag (TKT-foundation-093, architecture v1.5.69 §10.9.19 / `02` §6.1)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, and the doubles are the **whole** input: five committed
excel-HTML bodies (bounded trims of paced captures — `kap-public-report-1643141` ASELS 2026-06 from
§22, `-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık / `-1561039` ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık / `-1648240` MİGROS 2026-06 from
§28, and **`-1565996` THYAO 2025 Yıllık**, committed as the first step of this ticket's lane).
**No test calls kap.org.tr**; every negative case is an in-memory mutation of a committed body,
never an invented one. The map entries this section governs stand **only** on a committed body, and
each names the body that carries its spelling (`KapPublicLabelRule.Provenance`, the closed set
`KapPublicReportDecoder.CaptureNames`).

**Why this section exists.** §28 closed the decoder's canonical gap down to the debt/cash core and
the income ladder (9 IS / 6 BS codes). The statements view the user specified (recorded verbatim on
TKT-foundation-095) needs the **rest** of the taxonomy's row families — trade receivables/payables,
inventories, net PP&E, right-of-use assets, goodwill+intangibles, lease liabilities, deferred
revenue, retained earnings, the NCI and parent-equity block, paid-in capital, total assets/liabilities,
the other current/non-current families, R&D and the other-operating/investing/financing pairs, the
TMS 29 net monetary position, parent net income, and the cash-flow detail lines (impairments,
provisions, the monetary adjustment, asset-sale proceeds, acquisitions, equity issuance, buybacks,
dividends, cash interest, debt issued/repaid). Every one of them already decodes as a valued
`OTHER_*` passthrough; what is missing is the **canonical mapping**. Two structural facts make the
mapping non-trivial and are what this section pins:

1. **The balance sheet legitimately repeats captions across its current/non-current blocks**
   (`Ticari Alacaklar` ×2, `Ticari Borçlar` ×2, `Kiralama İşlemlerinden Borçlar` ×2 per column pair,
   `Ertelenmiş Gelirler (…)` ×2), and the income statement repeats `Ana Ortaklık Payları` across its
   profit-or-loss and other-comprehensive-income parts. A plain 1:1 `(statement, label)` map would
   either overwrite one value with the other or raise `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` and quarantine the
   whole statement row — the §28 failure mode coming back through the front door.
2. **The declarations the view needs sit in the filing, not in an inference:** the consolidation
   flag is the header-table field `Finansal Tablo Niteliği` (`Konsolide`/`Solo`), never the company
   heading and never inferred from minority interests.

The design is **additive** and is recorded in `01` v1.5.69 §10.9.19 and `02` §6.1: the label-rule
key gains a **row-scope** dimension resolved **before** label mapping exactly as the cash-flow
section already is (the balance sheet's current/non-current block from the capture's own
`DÖNEN VARLIKLAR`/`DURAN VARLIKLAR`/`KISA VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER`/`UZUN VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER` anchors;
the income statement's profit-or-loss/other-comprehensive-income part from its own
`Kar veya Zararda Yeniden Sınıflandırılmayacaklar` anchor; the cash-flow block the section already
carries). A rule **without** the scope qualifier matches in every scope, so every pre-093 entry is
unchanged and the 088/090/091/092 goldens hold; a row whose block cannot be determined matches **no**
scope-qualified entry and is a named, counted `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` finding instead of a guess.

**TC-MDF-122 — The statements-view row set: the industrial IS/BS/CF families map onto new canonical codes from committed bodies, a caption repeated across a statement's row scopes maps to distinct codes per scope (with an honest fail-safe when the scope cannot be determined), a qualifier-free rule still matches every scope, and the `Finansal Tablo Niteliği` declaration decodes Konsolide/Solo/absent honestly** (new v1.45)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002/003 (statement ingest — the row families the served financials need), FR-MDF-008 (provenance `kap-public`, per-entry capture provenance), FR-MDF-012 (honest gaps: an unmapped family stays a labelled passthrough, an undetermined block is named and counted), FR-RES-005..018 (the financials terrace this supplies), FR-VAL-001 (the DCF's `debt`/`cash` consumed as stored — fenced, not widened), `02` §6.1 (the chart extension and the block dimension), `01` v1.5.69 §10.9.19 · Level: L2 · Design: a decode/store matrix over {committed body: THYAO 2025, ASELSAN 2025, TÜPRAŞ 2025, ASELS 2026-06, MİGROS 2026-06} × {statement: BS, IS, CF} × {row scope: current, non-current, profit-or-loss, OCI, undetermined} × {map projection: unqualified, scope-qualified} × {flag: Konsolide, Solo, absent, other}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the production `FinancialStatementStore`; the five committed report bodies reachable through `RealSourceCatalog`; the label map **and** the scope-qualified projection after this ticket's extension.
- Steps: (a) read **both** map projections and every entry's provenance against the catalog body it names; (b) decode THYAO 2025 Yıllık and read its current-period balance sheet; (c) decode ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık and read the balance sheet's two blocks of trade receivables and payables; (d) decode the committed ASELS 2026-06 body with its `DÖNEN VARLIKLAR` anchor renamed, and read the current balance sheet plus the findings ledger; (e) re-decode the committed ASELS 2026-06, TÜPRAŞ 2025 and MİGROS 2026-06 bodies unchanged and read their line counts and the 088 debt/cash/core values; (f) decode THYAO 2025 and read its income statement (both `Ana Ortaklık Payları` occurrences) and the ASELSAN annual R&D row; (g) decode THYAO 2025 and read its cash-flow statement's detail lines and TÜPRAŞ's monetary-adjustment row; (h) decode the committed ASELS 2026-06 body, then the same body with `Konsolide`→`Solo`, with `Konsolide`→`Bireysel`, and with the field renamed away, and read `Header.ConsolidationType`/`Consolidated`; (i) decode THYAO 2025, map its balance sheet through `StatementMapper.Map`, and store the payload through the production store; read `financial_statements`/`fin_line_items`.
- Expected: **(a)** the unqualified projection is still a function on `(statement, label)` and every entry's `Scope` is null; the scope-qualified projection is a function on `(statement, label, scope)`, every `Scope` is in the closed vocabulary (`CURRENT`/`NONCURRENT`/`PL`/`OCI` and the four CF blocks), and **every** entry of **both** projections names a provenance in `CaptureNames`, whose committed body carries the exact spelling, and targets a code in `StatementMapper.CanonicalCodes`; the union has no duplicate; every row family the view needs is present in the map (`INVENTORY`, `PPE_NET`, `ROU_ASSET`, `INTANGIBLES_NET`, `GOODWILL`, `PAID_IN_CAPITAL`, `RETAINED_EARNINGS`, `EQUITY_PARENT`, `TOTAL_ASSETS`, `TOTAL_LIAB`, `CUR_ASSETS_OTHER`, `CUR_LIAB_OTHER`, The live criteria (one paced `Source = kap-public` pass writing the expanded canonical row set with per-family counts verbatim, honest-absent families named) are the orchestrator's post-merge steps, never CI. **Not** this section's business: the served payload and the statements surface (094/095), metric consumption (096), the DR-1/DR-2 fences (`ST_DEBT`/`LT_DEBT` stay exactly two lines, `TOTAL_EQUITY` stays NCI-inclusive as stored — the new lease/`MINORITY_INT`/`EQUITY_PARENT` codes are decoded lines, never a widening of `debt` or of equity), share counts (065/086), and any VYK/XBRL decode change.
