No TC id is renamed.

## 34. The statements-view serving surface — the full line set, the [C] derived rows, honest cumulative period labels, the filing badges and the financial-sector layout notice (TKT-foundation-094, architecture v1.5.70 §10.9.19 / `02` §3.1 / `03` §3)

**Lane posture.** **L2**, **doubles-only**, and the doubles are the **whole** input: the five committed
excel-HTML bodies of §33 (`kap-public-report-1643141` ASELS 2026-06, `-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık,
`-1561039` ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık, `-1648240` MİGROS 2026-06, `-1565996` THYAO 2025 Yıllık) decoded by
the **production** `KapPublicReportDecoder`, written by the **production** `FinancialStatementStore`,
and read back through the **production** endpoint against a real migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL.
**No test calls kap.org.tr**, and this lane issued none; every negative case is an in-memory
mutation of a committed body *or* of the seeded world (a hand-built pre-migration-shaped fact, an
`accounting_profile` flip, a stored line added, a stored share count added), never a live read and
never an invented body.

**Why this section exists.** §22–§33 built the decode side: the canonical row set is now stored
(64 codes, `02` §6.1) with per-block separation, the filing's declared cumulative span rides the
decode result (090), and the `Konsolide`/`Solo` flag is decoded (093). The statements view the user
specified (recorded verbatim on TKT-foundation-095) needs the **serving** side: the full line set
reachable per period, the [C] row families computed server-side, the badges derived from served
facts, and an honest statement of what the surface cannot show. This section pins that surface.

**The serving decision, recorded so a later reader need not infer it (and stated here because
`03` §3 is the contract it amends).** The statements view's [C] rows are **period facts** and
therefore compute in the endpoint from the period's own stored lines — the same seam
`Line(EBITDA) ?? Sum(EBIT, DEPR_AMORT)` and `CanonicalMetrics.FreeCashFlow` already use — and are
**not** written to `derived_metrics`: point-in-time canonical metrics (ROIC, ROA, medians, CAGRs)
stay the Metrics Engine's (TKT-foundation-096), and nothing is duplicated across the two. The
payload extension is **additive** (`03` §3's in-V1 field rule): the five compat figures stay
byte-compatible, and every new block is a new key.

**TC-MDF-123 — The statements-view serving surface: `GET /api/v1/stocks/{symbol}/financials` serves the full canonical line set per period (with the `OTHER_*` passthrough beside it), the server-computed [C] derived rows honest-null on any missing operand, the filing's own declared span and consolidation flag with badges derived from served evidence, and the financial-sector layout notice instead of a half-filled industrial row set.**
- Traces: FR-RES-008 (financials section supply), FR-RES-014/015/016 (restated marking, as-of, no-data with the coverage boundary), FR-RES-027 (BVPS), FR-MDF-002/008/015 (ingest/provenance/immutability — the span and flag columns are additive, no row rewritten), BR-RES-004 (the client computes nothing), BR-RES-008 / M-1 (`03` §1.2: missing data is an explicit state, never blank-as-zero), `02` §3.1 (`financial_statements`'s three additive columns), `02` §6.2 (the TMS 29 transition, the financial-sector profile posture), `03` §3 (the `financials` row, amended additively).
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container (`FixtureUniverse.Build(FixtureAnchor.L2)` via `FixtureSeeder`, `derived_metrics` cleared so a stale metric cannot mask a served figure); the production decoder + store; the five committed bodies reachable through `RealSourceCatalog`; the L2 fixture's `daily_prices` row and the seeded `price_derived_share_counts` row (2.5bn at `T`); a test company created per case and joined to `XU100` (the covered universe the endpoint resolves through).
- Steps: (a) decode TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık, store it, `GET /financials`, and read the FY2025 period's `lines`, `otherLines`, the compat five, `derived`, and the envelope's `evHeader`; (b) decode the same body and read the served `span`/`consolidated` per period plus the `filing` envelope, then the same envelope for a pre-transition hand-built fact; (c) decode MİGROS 2026-06, store it, `GET /financials`, and read the `Q` 2026-06-30 and its comparative periods; (d) decode ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık, store it, and read its `derived` block for the absent-operand rows and the FY2024 growth row; (e) decode ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık with **no** price-derived count, read the per-share rows; then add a `SHARES_DILUTED` stored line (100m) beside a price count (2.5bn) and read them again; (f) decode TÜPRAŞ 2025, read the industrial payload's `layoutNotice` and its `evHeader`, then flip the instrument's `accounting_profile` to `bank` and `insurance` and read them again (plus the period's `span`, `consolidated` and `evHeader`), then to `reit`; (g) store a hand-built pre-migration-shaped fact (no span, no flag, `sourceRef = manual-test`, period 2022-06-30) and read its period and the `filing` envelope; (h) decode THYAO 2025 Yıllık (the body carrying lease liabilities beside the borrowings pair), store it, and read `grossDebt`/`netDebt`/`tangibleBookValue` beside the served lease lines.
- Expected: **(a)** FY2025's `lines` is a flat canonical-code → `{value, restated}` map per statement type and carries the capture's own stored figures (single TL: `IS.REV` 830.356.131.000, `CF.OCF` 49.464.734.000, `CF.CAPEX` −19.118.339.000, `BS.ST_DEBT` 8.957.100.000, `BS.LT_DEBT` 28.428.775.000, `BS.CASH` 107.237.211.000, `BS.CUR_ASSETS` 236.931.136.000, `BS.CUR_LIAB` 169.748.201.000, `BS.TOTAL_EQUITY` 369.827.994.000, `BS.GOODWILL` 4.064.000, `BS.INTANGIBLES_NET` 10.129.896.000, `BS.MINORITY_INT` 5.728.736.000), every entry carrying its statement's `restated` flag (false, as-reported); the `OTHER_*` passthrough stays **reachable** in `otherLines` under the same shape (`OTHER_BS_ARA_TOPLAM` 236.931.136.000 present) with **every** key prefixed `OTHER_` and **no** `OTHER_*` key inside `lines`; the compat five are byte-compatible beside the new blocks (`revenue` 830.356.131.000, `ebit` 41.646.897.000, `netIncome` 29.872.672.000); `derived` carries the pinned goldens — `grossDebt` 37.385.875.000 (**exactly** `ST_DEBT + LT_DEBT`), `netDebt` −69.851.336.000, `netWorkingCapital` 67.182.935.000, `tangibleBookValue` 359.694.034.000 — and **every ratio equals the served lines' own quotient** (`grossMargin` = `GROSS_PROFIT/REV`, `operatingMargin` = `EBIT/REV`, `effectiveTaxRate` = `TAX_EXP/PRETAX_INC`, `netMarginParent` = `NI_PARENT/REV`, `netWorkingCapital` = `CUR_ASSETS − CUR_LIAB`, `netDebt` = `ST+LT − CASH`), with `revenueGrowthYoy` = `REV(FY2025)/REV(FY2024) − 1` against the **served** FY2024 lines; the per-share rows use the price lane's count (the statement declares no `SHARES_DILUTED`) — `bvps` = equity/2.5bn, `tbvPerShare` = (equity − goodwill − intangibles)/2.5bn, `fcfPerShare` = (OCF − |CAPEX|)/2.5bn; `evHeader` serves each input as its own fact with its own as-of (`sharePrice` 150 at `T`, `marketCap` 375.000.000.000 at `T`, `grossDebt` 37.385.875.000 at 2025-12-31, `cash` 107.237.211.000 (the stored `CASH` line exactly — the chart has no short-term-investments code), `minorityInterest` 5.728.736.000) and the DCF's own inputs are untouched. **(b)** the FY2025 period's `span` is its own declared twelve-month range (`periodStartDate` 2025-01-01, `durationMonths` 12) and `consolidated` is `true` (`Konsolide`) on both FY2025 and FY2024; the `filing` envelope describes the latest served period (`periodEndDate` 2025-12-31, `sourceRef` `kap-public`, `consolidated` true, `tms29` true, `kapSource` true), while the pre-transition fact's envelope reads `tms29` **false** — the derivation from `period_end_date >= 2023-12-31` (`02` §6.2), never a stored claim. **(c)** the 2026 interim is `Q`-typed **and** honestly cumulative: its `span` is 2026-01-01/6 (090's R-1 closed on the surface), the prior interim carries its own 2025-01-01/6 span, `revenueGrowthYoy` is anchored to the same period type one year earlier from served lines (`REV` 241.298.039.000 over the 2025-06-30 `REV`), and its `grossDebt` is the fenced pair (4.974.529.000 + 34.053.178.000); the balance-sheet-only comparative period (2025-12-31) serves its `BS` rows with an **empty** `IS` map, a **null** `span` (a balance sheet is an instant, never a range) and a null `revenueGrowthYoy`. **(d)** with no `GOODWILL` line on the sheet, `tangibleBookValue` and `tbvPerShare` are **null** with the explicit `no-data` state — never `equity − intangibles`; with no `EBITDA` line and no `DEPR_AMORT`, `ebitdaMargin` is null while `grossMargin` (its operands present) is served and equals `GROSS_PROFIT/REV`; the earliest served period (FY2024, no prior same-type period) has a null `revenueGrowthYoy` — null, never 0. **(e)** with neither a statement count nor a price count every per-share row is null with the `no-data` state (the user's exclusion #4 — no paid-in-capital-derived guess), never zero; once both exist the **statement's** `SHARES_DILUTED` (100m) wins over the price count (2.5bn) — the served `bvps`/`fcfPerShare` are equity/100m and (OCF − |CAPEX|)/100m and provably **not** the 2.5bn quotient. **(f)** the industrial filing serves `layoutNotice` **null**, a non-empty `BS` row set and a non-null `evHeader`; `bank` and `insurance` serve `200` with a notice carrying `accountingProfile` = the profile, `applies` true, `state` `not-applicable` and a message naming the industrial layout — explicitly not applicable, never an error and never a no-data claim — **instead of a row set**: every period's `lines` and `otherLines` keep the `IS`/`BS`/`CF` shape with every map **empty**, `derived` is **empty** and the envelope's `evHeader` is **null** (its inputs are the same industrial-mapped stored lines), while the compat five figures (e.g. `revenue` 830.356.131.000) and the period's own `span` (`2025-01-01`, 12 months) and `consolidated` (`true`) still serve — period facts about the filing, not industrial rows; `reit` serves a null notice, a non-null `evHeader` and keeps the natural row set (a non-empty `BS` map and a non-empty `derived`). **(g)** the pre-migration-shaped `Q` period keeps `periodType` `Q` with `span` **null** and `consolidated` **null** — today's label, never a fabricated range — while its [C] rows still compute from the stored lines (`grossDebt` 300, `netWorkingCapital` 250, `netDebt` 250); its `filing` envelope suppresses every badge rather than guessing (`consolidated` null, `tms29` false, `kapSource` false for `manual-test`). **(h)** THYAO's served lease lines are visible beside the borrowings pair (`ST_DEBT` 70.069.000.000, `LT_DEBT` 601.817.000.000, `ST_LEASE_LIAB` 86.152.000.000, `LT_LEASE_LIAB` 552.824.000.000) and `grossDebt` is **671.886.000.000** — exactly `ST_DEBT + LT_DEBT`, provably **not** the lease-inclusive sum (DR-2's fence consumed as stored, never widened); `netDebt` = `grossDebt − CASH` and `tangibleBookValue` = `TOTAL_EQUITY − GOODWILL − INTANGIBLES_NET`, with all four components read from the served `lines` (`CASH` 86.035.000.000, `TOTAL_EQUITY` 911.256.000.000, `GOODWILL` 1.145.000.000, `INTANGIBLES_NET` 8.867.000.000) and the derived rows' goldens pinned (`netDebt` 585.851.000.000, `tangibleBookValue` 901.244.000.000) — the payload cross-checked against itself, never against hand-written constants.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-117/119/122 (the decode-side contracts this section consumes — the served `lines` are those codes as stored, byte-identical to the goldens those sections pin); TC-MDF-118 (the DCF inputs — **untouched**: `evHeader` is display-only and the DCF's own `debt`/`cash`/share count are read from the same stored rows, never from this payload); TC-MDF-111 (the source-preference `Pick` this endpoint still uses to choose the served statement version); TC-MDF-005/006 (the honest-data envelope and the covered-universe 404). **Not** this section's business: the rendering (TKT-foundation-095), the point-in-time canonical metrics and medians (TKT-foundation-096, never duplicated here), the USD/Reel-TL basis, standalone 3-month quarters or TTM, 10Y CAGR and any DR-1/DR-2 resolution, and the `/profitability`, `/growth`, `/balance-sheet`, `/dividends` endpoints.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the eight sub-cases are **(a)**–**(h)** here, in v1.46, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.70. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_123_a_financials_serves_the_full_line_set_and_the_derived_rows_from_the_stored_lines` = (a), `…_b_served_span_and_badges_come_from_the_stored_filing_evidence` = (b), `…_c_a_quarter_typed_interim_serves_its_declared_cumulative_span` = (c), `…_d_a_missing_operand_makes_its_derived_row_null_never_a_guess` = (d), `…_e_per_share_rows_use_the_statement_count_then_the_price_count_then_null` = (e), `…_f_bank_and_insurance_profiles_serve_the_layout_notice_instead_of_a_row_set` = (f), `…_g_a_pre_migration_row_serves_no_span_and_suppresses_the_badges` = (g), `…_h_gross_debt_is_the_fenced_st_plus_lt_pair_never_widened_to_leases` = (h). No TC id is renamed.

## 35. The key-metrics & capital-tracker backend — seventeen canonical codes through the versioned engine, the depth-honest 5Y company median, the extended sector medians and one serving endpoint (TKT-foundation-096, architecture v1.5.78 `02` §6.2/§6.3 / `03` §3)

**Lane posture.** **L2, doubles-only, zero live requests.** The world is the L2 fixture universe
(16 instruments, 3 sectors) seeded through the production `FixtureSeeder`, the committed
`corporate_actions` rows the fixture carries (EPSL's split and bonus issue), and the production
`metrics-recompute` / `medians` jobs and `MetricsEngine` / `SectorMedianEngine`. No capture is
re-fetched, no price is invented, and the only synthetic inputs are statement lines **injected onto
ALFA's own FY periods** to exercise formulas the L2 world has no line for — the DR-1 falsifiability
pattern, removed again in a `finally`.

**Why this section exists.** The stock page's KEY VALUE METRICS & MOAT INDICATORS panel and its
CAPITAL & DILUTION TRACKER (the user's spec, verbatim on TKT-foundation-095; rendering is
TKT-foundation-097's) ask for seventeen metric concepts v1's catalog does not carry, four of them
per-share growth families, plus a company-vs-own-history median and a sector median for every code
the panel shows. **BR-RES-004/UXR-G-011 pin the placement:** the panel's canonical values must be
the numbers the screener serves, and `derived_metrics` is the formula-versioned single source (`02`
§1 principle 3) — so the new codes go **through the Metrics Engine like the existing 26** (AD-06,
§5.1), and only the medians/aggregations are computed at serve time, from stored rows. The section
therefore has four load-bearing halves, and every one carries a sub-case: the catalog + formula
implementation with its version bump (a)/(b), the depth-honest 5Y company median (c), the extended
sector-median set with its financial-peer exclusion disclosed by count (d)/(e), and the serving
endpoint's capital-tracker block (f)/(g) with its universe boundary (h).

**The exclusions and fences this section enforces rather than plans.** The user's five pinned
exclusions are mechanical here: no USD/real basis (every served value is the stored nominal TRY
figure, asserted as equality with the row); no 3M+TTM improvisation (no code in the new set mixes a
quarter with a TTM span); no per-share fabrication (the per-share CAGRs and the Y/Y share-count
change are honestly N/M where the share count or the count history does not exist — (c) and (f)/g));
no 10Y window for the panel families (`ebit_cagr_*`, `bvps_cagr_*`, `fcf_ps_cagr_*` exist only as
3Y/5Y, and the panel's served set is asserted exhaustively); no widened gross debt (`croic`,
`ev_ebit` and `net_debt_fcf` consume `ST_DEBT` + `LT_DEBT` exactly as stored — DR-1/DR-2, `02` §6.1
— and `net_debt_fcf` is N/M when FCF ≤ 0).

**TC-MDF-124 — The key-metrics backend: seventeen new canonical codes are computed through the versioned Metrics Engine and stamped with the bumped formula version, the served current values are those stored rows cross-checked against their own statement arithmetic, the 5Y company median is withheld below its pinned depth floor and exact above it, the sector medians cover the 32-code set (the 31 panel codes plus `ev_fcf`) with peer and exclusion counts and exclude financial peers only from gated codes, a bank computes the financial-meaningful set and never the industrial-shaped one, the capital tracker serves the corporate-action history and the honest share-count N/M, and an uncovered symbol is 404** (new v1.47 reservation, authored here)
- Traces: FR-MDF-013 (canonical metrics — the panel's supply through the one engine), FR-MDF-014 (window honesty — the company median's `windowYears`/`depthYears` disclosure), FR-MDF-018 (basis markers — the per-share growth families' `isAdjusted`), FR-MDF-004 (corporate actions — the capital tracker's bedelli/bedelsiz source), FR-RES-006/007 (valuation metrics and vs-sector supply), FR-RES-009 (profitability supply), FR-RES-010 (growth CAGRs), FR-RES-027 (per-share book values), BR-RES-004 (one canonical definition per number), UXR-RES-024/025 (peer-count and N/M disclosure analogs), SCR-005 §2/§4/§5 (the panel this supplies; rendering is 097's), `02` §6.2 (the code table, the five exclusions, the extended gate, the company-median definition), `02` §6.3 (the covered median set and the financial-peer exclusion), `03` §3 (the endpoint), `01` v1.5.78 · Level: L2 · Design: a compute/serve matrix over {instrument profile: industrial, bank} × {code family: valuation, quality, financial-health, growth, per-share-growth} × {history depth: 1 observation, 12 observations, 11 observations} × {store surface: `derived_metrics`, `sector_metric_medians`, `corporate_actions`} × {serving state: value, honest N/M, 404}
- Preconditions: a migrated, fixture-seeded container; the production `MetricsEngine` and `SectorMedianEngine` behind the registered `metrics-recompute` and `medians` jobs; the fixture's `corporate_actions` rows for EPSL; `CanonicalMetrics.Version` at the bumped value with `MetricCodes.All` at 43; the new `GET /api/v1/stocks/{symbol}/key-metrics` route registered.
- Steps: (a) seed the L2 universe, run `metrics-recompute` at T, and read every stored `derived_metrics` row of the day (definitions, stamps, per-instrument code sets, ALFA's negotiated set); (b) `GET /stocks/ALFA/key-metrics` and read the envelope, the served metric map, and every panel code's value/window/adjustment/gate flag against the stored rows; (b cont.) inject the inputs the L2 statements do not carry onto ALFA's FY periods (`TOTAL_ASSETS`, `GOODWILL`, `INTANGIBLES_NET`, `NET_MONETARY_GL`, `INTEREST_PAID`, plus multi-year `EBIT`/`TOTAL_EQUITY` series — **not** `PRETAX_INC`, whose 250 is the fixture's own FY line and which stays untouched; the injected `NET_MONETARY_GL` is what makes `monetary_gain_pretax` computable), re-run the job, and read the newly computable codes; (b cont., basis marker) inject a panel-only per-share history onto **ZETA** (FY2020–FY2024 equity + share counts, none of which the world stores) plus one 2022-06-01 1:5 split, re-run the job, and read the four per-share CAGR families' `is_adjusted` markers — ZETA has no dividend rows and no EPS year before FY2024, so the action reaches the BVPS series alone; (c) read the 5Y company median on one stored observation, then with twelve stored observations inserted on earlier dates, then with one deleted (eleven); (d) run `medians` and read every stored sector median row plus the endpoint's per-code `sectorMedian`/`peerCount`/`excludedCount`; (e) create a `bank` instrument in a private sector carrying a **complete** industrial line set, run both jobs, and read its stored rows, its sector's medians, and its served payload; (f)/(g) `GET /stocks/EPSL/key-metrics` and read the capital-tracker block (actions, basis, share-count change, paid-in capital, payout ratio); (h) `GET /stocks/NOSUCH/key-metrics`.
- Expected: **(a)** `CanonicalMetrics.Version` is the bumped `2`, `MetricCodes.All` holds **43** distinct codes, the day's `derived_metrics` holds exactly **16 × 43 = 688** rows and **every** one carries `FormulaVersion == 2` (070's discipline: a row republished by a new definition carries the new stamp, and the fast-path still reports a matching row `unchanged` — the idempotency and recompute suites, re-run as non-regression below); each instrument's code set **is** `MetricCodes.All` — the seventeen new codes exist as rows of their own for every instrument, never an aggregate and never a missing row — with ALFA's `croic` and `fcf_ni_3y` non-null and its `roa` and `ebit_cagr_3y` honestly **null** (the L2 statements carry no `TOTAL_ASSETS` line and one FY of `EBIT`). **(b)** the payload carries the honest-data envelope (`asOf` = the compute day, `symbol`, `accountingProfile` = `industrial`, `financialSector` **null**) and serves **all 31** panel codes; the values the fixture's own lines determine are the hand-derived ones — `croic` 0.208333 (`OCF 250 / IC 1,200` where IC = debt 400 + equity 1,000 − cash 200), `ev_ebit` 7.333333 (`EV 2,200 / EBIT 300`), `p_fcf` 20 (mcap 2,000 / FCF 100), `p_tbv` 2 (mcap / equity, no goodwill or intangibles stored), `fcf_ni` 0.5, `cfo_ebit` 0.833333, `net_debt_fcf` 2 (net debt 200 / FCF 100), `fcf_ni_3y` = the mean of the three annual ratios (190/185, 180/190, 100/200), and the per-share FCF CAGRs −0.206300 (3Y) / −0.089718 (5Y) on the constant 100 M share count — while `roa`, `monetary_gain_pretax`, `cfo_interest`, `ebit_cagr_3y/5y` and `bvps_cagr_3y/5y` are served as **honest N/M** (`value` null, `notMeaningful` true), never `0` and never a two-year statistic in a five-year slot; and for **every** panel code the served `value`, `windowYears`, `isAdjusted` and `profileGated` equal the stored row (the endpoint computes no canonical value). **(b cont.)** with the injected lines the newly computable codes are exactly the formulas: `roa` 0.10 (200 / 2,000), `p_tbv` 2000/(1000−100−50), `monetary_gain_pretax` 0.20 (50 / 250), `cfo_interest` 5 (250 / |−50| — the KAP outflow sign is the denominator's absolute, not its sign), `ebit_cagr_3y` 0.077217 / `_5y` 0.063995 and `bvps_cagr_3y` 0.077217 / `_5y` 0.073941, with the TMS 29 `regimeBreak` marker set on the windows spanning 2023-12-31 and `isAdjusted` false on the EBIT (non-per-share) family. **(b cont., basis marker)** the per-family split is discriminating, not incidental: with ZETA's single 2022 split adjusting **only** the panel's per-share series, `bvps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` and `fcf_ps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` carry `is_adjusted` **true** (and the BVPS CAGRs carry a value, `bvps_cagr_5y`'s base being the adjusted FY2020 and `bvps_cagr_3y`'s the unadjusted FY2022, both asserted non-null — the marker is not a blanket swallow) while `eps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` and `div_cagr_3y`/`_5y` stay **false**: a panel-family adjustment never re-stamps a v1 EPS/DPS row (`02` §6.2's per-family marker, the v1 byte-identity condition). **(c)** the 5Y company median is a statistic over **stored** history: with one stored observation the block discloses `windowYears` 5, `minimumObservations` 12, `observations` 1, `depthYears` 0 and `notMeaningful` true with a null `value`; with twelve observations inside the window it is the standard median of the stored values (even count → 6.5) with `notMeaningful` false and a non-zero `depthYears`, and the **current** column is untouched by that history (the served `value` stays the stored T value); one observation below the floor (eleven) withholds it again — a short-window statistic never wears the 5Y label. **(d)** the medians job writes **3 sectors × 32 codes = 96** rows, every one stamped `FormulaVersion == 2`, covering exactly the `02` §6.3 covered set — **32 codes: the 31 panel codes plus `ev_fcf`**, the fifth standard valuation code the vs.-sector strip already carried (the panel is 31, so the median set is the panel ∪ `ev_fcf`, never "the panel's set"); ALFA's sector serves `pe` median 10 with **3** assessed peers and **2** loss-maker exclusions, and every panel code's served `sectorMedian`/`peerCount`/`excludedCount` equals its stored median row — load-bearing for a code v1 never covered, `croic`, whose sector median is assessed over **4** peers with **1** exclusion (DELTA's invested capital is negative), i.e. a code the panel renders is never a silently missing median. **(e)** a `bank` instrument with a complete industrial line set computes the financial-meaningful concepts — `pe` 30, `roe` 0.10 (300 / 3,000), `roa` 0.06 (300 / 5,000) — while **all 26** profile-gated codes are null with a null `windowYears` even though their inputs exist (the gate, not the absence, is what makes them null); its sector's `ev_ebit` median has **0** peers and **1** exclusion with a null median while the same instrument is a legitimate `pe` peer (**1** peer, **0** exclusions, median 30); and the endpoint serves the `financialSector` block as `state` `sector_not_applicable` / `reason` `financial_sector` / `profile` `bank` with `profileGated` true and `notMeaningful` true on the gated rows. **(f)/(g)** the tracker block serves the seeded `corporate_actions` **newest first** on the `corporate-action-adjusted` basis — the 2026-03-02 `bonus_issue` (`bedelsiz`, ratio 0.1 → 10%) then the 2025-06-02 `split` (ratio 5 → 500%) — the Y/Y share-count change as an honest N/M (`value` null, `notMeaningful`, `state` `no-count-history` — no count history is stored, and nothing is guessed from a split factor), paid-in capital / its as-of / the shares-outstanding basis as honest **nulls** where nothing is stored (money, never a fabricated count), and `payout_ratio` 0.4 read from `derived_metrics` with its `asOf`. **(h)** a symbol outside the covered XU100 universe answers **404** with the standard problem envelope's `NOT_FOUND` code (`03` §7), never an empty panel that looks like a data gap.
- Dependencies: TKT-foundation-093 (the line codes the formulas consume — `TOTAL_ASSETS`, `GOODWILL`, `INTANGIBLES_NET`, `NI_PARENT`, `NET_MONETARY_GL`, `PRETAX_INC`, `INTEREST_PAID` — consumed as stored, never extended here); TC-MDF-088/117/122 (the debt/equity fences and the canonical line contract this section relies on, unchanged); the idempotency/versioning suites below (070's fast-path). **Not** this section's business: the served statements and the financials surface (094/095), the panel's rendering (TKT-foundation-097), the screener's criteria/UI (the catalog rows exist with `is_screenable = false`; flipping that is a builder toggle, FR-SCR-017), any share-count or per-share fabrication (065/086's stores answer or the code is honestly N/M), and any USD/real basis or 10Y window (the user's exclusions).
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the sub-cases are **(a)**, **(b)**, **(b cont.)**, **(b cont., basis marker)**, **(c)**–**(e)**, **(f)/(g)** and **(h)** here, in v1.54, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.78 / `02` §6.2/§6.3 — nine shipped cases, because review round 1 added the per-family basis case (DR-4). The shipped method names carry the test file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_124_engine_writes_the_panel_codes_stamped_with_the_bumped_formula_version` = (a), `…_key_metrics_endpoint_serves_the_stored_canonical_values` = (b), `…_the_new_formulas_compute_from_the_stored_lines` = (b cont.), `…_the_basis_marker_is_per_family` = (b cont., basis marker), `…_company_median_is_withheld_on_thin_history_and_exact_when_deep_enough` = (c), `…_sector_medians_cover_the_panel_set_with_peer_and_exclusion_counts` = (d), `…_a_bank_serves_pe_and_roe_but_never_ev_ebit_and_is_excluded_from_gated_medians` = (e), `…_capital_tracker_serves_the_bedelli_bedelsiz_history_and_the_share_count_nm` = (f)/(g), `…_a_symbol_outside_the_covered_universe_is_not_found` = (h). No TC id is renamed.

## 36. Decoder fail-safe hardening — the balance-sheet block boundary, the income-statement part fail-safe, and the per-row scope finding (TKT-foundation-098, architecture v1.5.74 §10.9.19 / `02` §6.1)

**Lane posture.** **L2, doubles-only**, and the hardening fires **only on malformed input**: none of
the five committed bodies is malformed (each decodes with zero conflicts and zero
`BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`), so every load-bearing case is an **in-memory mutation of a committed body** —
the THYAO liabilities anchors renamed, the MİGROS OCI heading suffixed / replaced by a bare
`Diğer Gelir Tablosu`, the MİGROS and ASELSAN-annual OCI anchors re-worded, a scoped caption's raw
value attributes blanked out of the decoded columns. **No test calls kap.org.tr**, no body is
invented, and no new capture is committed: the five committed bodies are the entire evidence base.

**Why this section exists.** §33 (TKT-foundation-093) landed the row-scope dimension and its
fail-safe, and the review of that PR (round 1, `reviews/TKT-foundation-093-round-1.md`, DR-4/DR-5/
DR-8/DR-9) found the fail-safe guards `null` but never `stale`:

1. **The balance-sheet block is a forward-filled state with no boundary (DR-4).** The block is reset
   only at `ÖZKAYNAKLAR`, so a body that omits or renames `KISA VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER` /
   `UZUN VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜKLER` lets every liabilities row inherit the **assets** block's scope and take
   a wrong-block canonical code (`LT_TRADE_PAY` for a current trade payable, `LT_LEASE_LIAB` for a
   current lease) with **no finding at all** — a silent wrong value under a canonical code.
2. **The income-statement starting part rested on one exact string (DR-5).** The standalone OCI
   table was recognized only by `heading == "Diğer Kapsamlı Gelir Tablosu"` verbatim while the table
   was *classified* by `Contains`, so a suffixed or re-worded heading started in profit-or-loss — and
   with the capture's own OCI anchor also re-worded, the OCI `Ana Ortaklık Payları` collided with
   `NI_PARENT` and **quarantined the whole statement row** (the §28 failure mode through the front
   door), where the scope dimension already disambiguates an honest per-row outcome.
3. **The scope finding was emitted per decoded cell, not per row (DR-8).** The flag was set inside
   the cell loop *after* a cell decoded, so a scoped caption whose only values sit in a skipped
   column produced **no** finding — the row left the ledger silently, contradicting the reason's own
   doc.

The rules pinned here and in `01` v1.5.74 §10.9.19: **every balance-sheet total row (a label
beginning `TOPLAM`) closes the block state**, so the side that follows must re-declare its block and
a scoped caption in between resolves to `null` and is counted `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` (`ARA TOPLAM`
is *not* `TOPLAM`-prefixed, so those subtotals sit inside a block and are not boundary rows; any
`TOPLAM`-prefixed label *is* a boundary, not only a closing one — the five committed bodies carry
only block-closing totals, and a future intra-block `TOPLAM …` subtotal would close the block early
and demote the rest of that block's scoped captions to `OTHER_*` + `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`, named,
never a wrong code); **the income
statement's starting part is read from the heading's content** — a heading naming the profit-or-loss
part (a combined statement included) starts there, a heading carrying the comprehensive-income part
without it starts in OCI, and a heading attesting **neither** leaves the part undetermined, where a
part-qualified caption is a counted **`PART_UNDETERMINED`** passthrough (the new closed reason, the
IS analogue of `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`) — and where a part-qualified caption the statement already
holds with a **different value** has a row attesting the **other** part between the two occurrences
(the part moved, only the anchor's wording was unreadable) is the same named passthrough instead of
a whole-row quarantine, **without weakening the conflict quarantine itself** (two genuinely mapped
unequal duplicates still `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT`): an unequal repeat of a caption **inside its own
part**, with no such attesting row between the occurrences, still conflicts and quarantines the row
— the scope state alone cannot tell the two apart, so the discriminator is the capture's own row
evidence (TC-MDF-125 (h), added in the ticket's review round 1, DR-01). The scope finding is a
property of the **row**: a scoped caption valued on **any** column (decoded, skipped or unreadable)
is named once.

**TC-MDF-125 — Decoder fail-safe hardening: a body without its liabilities anchors fails safe to honest passthroughs with the boundary named (never a stale block), a re-worded or suffixed OCI heading never puts the OCI parent income under `NI_PARENT` (and an undetermined or stale part is named, not quarantined), an unequal repeat of a part-qualified caption *inside its own part* still conflicts and quarantines the row, a scoped caption valued only in skipped columns is still named, and all five committed bodies decode byte-identically** (new v1.48)
- Traces: FR-MDF-003 (statement decode correctness — the fail-safes guard the wrong-code paths), FR-MDF-012 (named/counted skips — a stale or undetermined scope is a distinct named outcome, never a guess), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole), `01` v1.5.74 §10.9.19 (the boundary and IS-scope rules), `02` §6.1 (the closed reason set: `PART_UNDETERMINED` added) · Level: L2 · Design: a decode matrix over {committed body: THYAO 2025, MİGROS 2026-06, ASELSAN 2025, ASELS 2026-06, TÜPRAŞ 2025} × {mutation: liability anchors renamed, OCI heading suffixed / re-worded / bare, OCI anchor re-worded, a part-qualified caption's same-part occurrence re-labelled, scoped caption's raw values blanked, no mutation} × {statement: BS, IS} × {outcome: passthrough + named finding, canonical code, quarantine (only for a same-part unequal duplicate)}
- Preconditions: the five committed report bodies reachable through `RealSourceCatalog`; the production `KapPublicReportDecoder`; the label map **and** the scope-qualified projection of §33 (unchanged by this ticket — no entry added, renamed or removed, no canonical code added, `StatementMapper.CanonicalCodes` stays 64).
- Steps: (a) decode the THYAO 2025 body with **both** liabilities anchors renamed (`KISA VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER`→`KISA VADELİ BORÇLAR`, `UZUN VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER`→`UZUN VADELİ BORÇLAR`) and read the current balance sheet, its conflicting codes and the findings ledger; (b) decode the MİGROS 2026-06 body with its OCI heading suffixed (`Diğer Kapsamlı Gelir Tablosu (Sürdürülen Faaliyetler)`) and read the current income statement; (c) the same body with the **same suffix and its OCI anchor re-worded** (`Kar veya Zararda Yeniden Sınıflandırılmayacaklar`→`…mayan Kalemler`) and read the statement and its conflicts; (d) the same body with the OCI heading replaced by a bare `Diğer Gelir Tablosu` and the anchor re-worded, and read the statement, the passthrough and the finding; (e) decode the ASELSAN 2025 body with its OCI anchor re-worded and read the income statement, the passthrough and the finding; (f) the MİGROS body of (d) with the OCI occurrence's first two value cells blanked — both the raw `title` attribute **and** the displayed text removed, so neither cell carries a value and the row's only valued cells are the two skipped sub-period columns — and read the line count, the passthrough's absence and the finding; (g) decode all five committed bodies **unmutated** and read their line counts, statement counts, conflicts and scope findings; (h) decode the ASELSAN 2025 body with its profit-or-loss `Kontrol Gücü Olmayan Paylar` row re-labelled `Ana Ortaklık Payları` (two unequal occurrences of the same part-qualified caption **inside** its own part, no other-part row between them) and read the income statement, its conflicting codes and the scope findings.
- Expected: **(a)** 497 lines and six statements (the hardening repairs nothing and drops nothing); **zero** `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` and every statement's `ConflictingItemCodes` null — never a quarantine for a scope the fail-safe disambiguates; **five** `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` findings (one per undetermined liabilities row: `Ticari Borçlar`, the two `Kiralama İşlemlerinden Borçlar` and the two `Ertelenmiş Gelirler (…)` occurrences), each `StatementType = BS` with `Count = 1`; the six wrong-block codes `ST_TRADE_PAY`/`LT_TRADE_PAY`/`ST_LEASE_LIAB`/`LT_LEASE_LIAB`/`ST_DEFERRED_REV`/`LT_DEFERRED_REV` **absent from the statement**, while every liabilities occurrence keeps the capture's own value (78.640 / 86.152 / 151.257 / 552.824 / 3.240) under an `OTHER_BS_*` passthrough; the unqualified totals still map (`CUR_LIAB` 442.076, `TOTAL_LIAB` 1.085.489) and the assets side is untouched (`ST_TRADE_RECV` 45.791, `INVENTORY` 41.472, `PPE_NET` 362.618, `TOTAL_ASSETS` 1.996.745, `EQUITY_PARENT` 911.222). **(b)** the statement starts in the OCI part: `NI_PARENT` 1.010.950 (profit-or-loss), the OCI occurrence 704.436 under an `OTHER_IS_*` passthrough and **never** under `NI_PARENT`, `NI` 1.078.256 = parent 1.010.950 + NCI 67.306, zero conflicts, zero `PART_UNDETERMINED`. **(c)** zero conflicts, no quarantine, `NI_PARENT` 1.010.950 and the OCI 704.436 as a passthrough. **(d)** exactly one `PART_UNDETERMINED` finding (`StatementType = IS`, `Label = 'Ana Ortaklık Payları'`, `Count = 1`, the *undetermined-heading* wording), `NI_PARENT` 1.010.950 and the OCI 704.436 as a passthrough, zero conflicts — never a defaulted part. **(e)** 383 lines, zero conflicts, the FY2025 income statement 47 lines with `NI_PARENT` 29.949.517 and the OCI 67.860.266 as a passthrough, and exactly one `PART_UNDETERMINED` carrying the **stale-part** wording (distinct from (d)'s). **(f)** 312 lines and 32 income-statement lines — the mutated row decodes **no** line, so 704.436 appears nowhere — yet exactly one `PART_UNDETERMINED` names the row (once per row, not per decoded cell), with `NI_PARENT` 1.010.950 and `NI` 1.078.256 unharmed and zero conflicts. **(g)** the hardening is invisible on well-formed input: ASELS 116, TÜPRAŞ 346, ASELSAN 383, MİGROS 314, THYAO 497 lines; six statements each; **zero** conflicts, **zero** `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`, **zero** `PART_UNDETERMINED`, every `ConflictingItemCodes` null. **(h)** the stale-part tolerance does not fire: **zero** `PART_UNDETERMINED` findings, both periods' FY2025 income statement carries `NI_PARENT` = −31.790 (the first occurrence retained) with `ConflictingItemCodes` containing `NI_PARENT`, and exactly two `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` findings (one per period) — a genuine same-part unequal duplicate still quarantines the statement row, which is the ticket's pinned non-goal.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-122 (§33 — the map/scope projection and the anchor vocabulary these mutations perturb; its (a)/(h) assertions are strengthened by DR-6/DR-7 in this ticket, nothing relaxed); TC-MDF-107/108/117/119 (the decoder contract and the per-body goldens — all green unrelaxed); `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests`, `RealSourceStatementsTests`, `StatementNormalizationTests`, `StatementVersioningTests` re-run green. **Not** this section's business: the served financials payload / persistence (094), metric consumption (096), the DR-1/DR-2 fences (`ST_DEBT`/`LT_DEBT`, `TOTAL_EQUITY` untouched), any map entry or canonical code, any new capture, and any live request.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the eight sub-cases are **(a)**–**(h)** here, in v1.48 (+ v1.49 for (h)), on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.74. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_125_a_balance_sheet_liabilities_without_their_anchors_never_inherit_the_assets_block` = (a), `…_b_a_suffixed_oci_heading_starts_the_statement_in_the_oci_part` = (b), `…_c_a_rewritten_oci_anchor_never_quarantines_the_income_statement` = (c), `…_d_an_undetermined_part_is_named_and_never_defaulted` = (d), `…_e_a_stale_part_is_named_not_quarantined` = (e), `…_f_a_scoped_caption_valued_only_in_skipped_columns_is_still_named` = (f), `…_g_the_hardening_is_invisible_on_all_five_committed_bodies` = (g), `…_h_an_unequal_same_part_duplicate_still_conflicts_and_quarantines` = (h). No TC id is renamed.

## 37. Turkish dotless-ı identity resolution — the ı→i fold in the token comparison, comparison-time only (TKT-foundation-099, architecture v1.5.75 §10.9.19 / `01` §10.9.19)

After TKT-foundation-091's comma-token rule mapped 26 of 30 XU030 companies, the live identity pass
(run 362, 2026-10-28) left **four** named `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` — BIMAS, ENKAI, ISCTR, SISE —
with the verified root cause being the **Turkish dotless `ı`** (U+0131): the site answers those
companies' `cmpOrFundCode` as `'bımas'`, `'enkaı'`, `'sıse'` and `'ısatr,ısbtr,ısctr,ıskur,tıb'`, and
`StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase` folds ASCII case but **not** `ı`↔`i` (distinct Unicode letters;
invariant casing maps neither onto the other). The matching rule now folds both sides of the token
comparison (`ı`→`i`, `İ`→`i`) **at comparison time only** — the stored mapping, the answer body and
our own symbol keep their original spelling. Comma splitting/trimming, the empty-token rule, the
no-substring rule and the **ambiguity refusal** are all unchanged: the fold widens the candidate set
and is explicitly **not** a tie-break.

**TC-MDF-126 — Turkish dotless-ı identity resolution: a committed answer whose code carries the dotless ı resolves its bound symbol (BIMAS, SISE, ENKAI and the five-token ISCTR list), the fold is comparison-only and rewrites neither the stored mapping nor the answer body, a dotless multi-candidate answer is still `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` and never a pick, the fold is exactly ı/İ→i and never a substring or accent rule, and every 080/091 golden holds unchanged** (new v1.50)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (the identity precondition of the company-scoped fetch — four companies are locked out), FR-MDF-012 (named/counted outcomes — the fold never turns an ambiguity into a pick), FR-MDF-016 (the ledger row the job writes); `01` v1.5.75 §10.9.19 (the dotless-ı subsection) and §10.9.11/§10.9.15 (046's rule class cited; its last-wins posture still not carried over); TKT-foundation-091 (the comma-token lane this extends, and the live residue it recorded) · Level: L2
- Design: an identity-resolution matrix over {committed dotless answers: BIMAS `'bımas'`, ENKAI `'enkaı'`, SISE `'sıse'`, ISCTR `'ısatr,ısbtr,ısctr,ıskur,tıb'`} × {committed 080/091 doubles: ASELS single token, comma-coded ISCTR, ambiguous GARAN} × {mutation: none, a longer token containing the symbol, a near-miss outside the fold, the uppercase dotted `İ`, a second candidate sharing the folded token} × {outcome: mapped oid stored, verbatim-data-only, `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED`, `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` with nothing stored}
- Preconditions: the four captured answers reachable through `RealSourceCatalog` (`kap-public-identity-BIMAS`/`-ENKAI`/`-SISE`/`-ISCTR`, provenance `Captured`, committed verbatim); the bound symbols bound into the fixture universe (instrument + current XU100 membership, the 091 harness helper); the production resolver composed through `AddMarketDataIngestion` over the WireMock identity double with pacing bound to 0; **no CI test calls kap.org.tr**.
- Steps: (a) bind BIMAS, stub its committed answer and run one resolution pass; read the counts, the stored row and the request journal. (a2) bind ENKAI and ISCTR together, stub their two committed answers and run one pass. (b) bind SISE, stub its committed answer and run one pass. (c) read the committed BIMAS catalog entry's provenance, method and raw bytes; then run the BIMAS pass and read the stored row, its provenance string and the instrument's own symbol. (d) stub a derived answer in which the committed `'bımas'` candidate is joined by a second company candidate carrying the ASCII `'bimas'` under a distinct oid; run one pass, then a second pass over the same answer. (e) run three mutations of the committed BIMAS answer in turn — the code replaced by `'bımasxyz'`, by `'bımaş'`, and by `'bİmas'` — reading the counts and the reason codes of each. (f) bind ASELS, ISCTR and GARAN, stub the committed ASELS capture, the constructed comma-coded ISCTR answer and the constructed GARAN answer, and run one pass.
- Expected: **(a)** `Ok`, bound 1, resolved 1, inserted 1, updated 0, unchanged 0, no unresolved; the stored row carries the instrument id, the `memberOrFundOid` of the `'bımas'` hit read out of the committed body, the lane/route provenance with `keyword=BIMAS`, and the run's as-of date; exactly one identity request and zero `byCriteria` requests. **(a2)** `Ok`, bound 2, resolved 2, inserted 2, no unresolved; ENKAI stores its `'enkaı'` hit's oid and ISCTR its five-token hit's oid — the fold composing with the comma-token rule. **(b)** `Ok`, resolved 1, inserted 1, no unresolved; the stored oid is the `'sıse'` hit's own; one identity request. **(c)** the catalog entry is `Captured`, source `kap-public`, method `POST`; its raw bytes are the captured 386-byte UTF-8 body, containing U+0131 and `"cmpOrFundCode":"bımas"` and **not** the ASCII `"bimas"` — the record itself shows the fold was never applied to the data; the stored row carries the source's oid and the provenance `kap-public:/tr/api/search/combined keyword=BIMAS` (never a folded code, because the code is not what is stored) and the bound instrument still reads `BIMAS`. **(d)** the pass is `Ok` with resolved 0, inserted 0 and exactly one unresolved entry: symbol `BIMAS`, reason `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`, the detail naming **both** codes (`bımas` and `bimas`); nothing is stored; the named event is logged; a second pass reports the same reason and still stores nothing; exactly two identity requests. **(e)** the longer token (`'bımasxyz'`) resolves 0 with `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` and stores nothing (the fold is never a substring rule); the near-miss (`'bımaş'`, one letter outside the fold) resolves 0 with `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` and stores nothing (the fold is not a transliteration/accent rule); the uppercase dotted `İ` (`'bİmas'`) resolves 1 and inserts 1, storing the committed body's own oid (the second half of the fold, which no capture happens to exercise). **(f)** `Ok`, bound 3, resolved 2, inserted 2, exactly one unresolved (`GARAN`, `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`); the stored oids are ASELS' committed oid and the ISCTR comma-coded company oid (never the fund hit's), and nothing is stored for GARAN — every 080/091 golden holds under the fold.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-120 (§31 — the comma-token rule and the ambiguity refusal this extends; its six cases and the 091 doubles are unchanged and re-run green); TC-MDF-105 (§21 — the stored-mapping contract the comparison-only claim is asserted against); `KapPublicIdentityTests`, `KapPublicIdentityJobTests`, `KapPublicSourceTests`, `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests` and `RealSourcePricesTests` (TC-MDF-063's catalog contract) re-run green. **Not** this section's business: any change to the comma-token rule, the ambiguity refusal, the resolver's classification reason set or the store; any walk/decode change (the statements lane picks the companies up automatically); any serving/UI work (094/095/096/097); any culture-wide Turkish text handling elsewhere in the codebase; the HttpClient/TLS-fingerprint hardening and the StatementsJob refusal path (100/101). The **live criterion** (one paced identity pass resolving all four symbols and their statements walk then landing data) is the orchestrator's post-merge step, never CI.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the sub-cases are **(a)**, **(a2)**, **(b)**–**(f)** here, in v1.50, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.75. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_126_a_the_committed_dotless_bimas_answer_resolves_bimas` = (a), `…_a2_the_other_captured_dotless_answers_resolve_through_the_fold` = (a2), `…_b_the_committed_dotless_sise_answer_resolves_sise` = (b), `…_c_the_fold_is_comparison_only_and_rewrites_nothing` = (c), `…_d_a_dotless_multi_candidate_answer_stays_ambiguous_and_is_never_picked` = (d), `…_e_the_fold_is_exactly_dotless_i_and_never_a_substring_or_accent_rule` = (e), `…_f_the_080_and_091_goldens_are_unchanged_by_the_fold` = (f). (a2) carries a letter suffix rather than a new letter because it is the same claim as (a) on the two remaining captures — the `TC-MDF_105_a2` naming precedent. No TC id is renamed.

## 38. The durable KAP public-site access fix — the browser-shaped TLS handler, 429-quota awareness, and the export pacing knob (TKT-foundation-100, architecture v1.5.77 §10.9.19)

The live findings of 2026-10-28/29 escalated the 087/091 WAF finding to the **transport** level: after a
burst the edge refused the .NET `HttpClient` with connection resets while curl and python from the same
container succeeded (a **TLS ClientHello fingerprint** discriminator), and the excel export endpoint
began answering **HTTP 429 "Request Limit Exceeded"** (empty body, **no `Retry-After`**) under a rolling
per-IP quota. The planner's option-(c) ruling makes the **adapter** the product fix — a browser-shaped
`SocketsHttpHandler`/`SslClientAuthenticationOptions` (TLS floor, browser-ordered cipher suites where the
platform allows one, ALPN `h2`/`http/1.1`) behind the lane's options, **an honest fingerprint, not a
disguise** — and treats the relay as documented ops posture committed under `deploy/`, never a hidden
dev hack. The quota is **authoritative**: a 429 is named and counted, `Retry-After` is honored verbatim
when present, the observed absent-header case takes a conservative fixed backoff, a wait longer than the
caller's per-item bound **truncates the walk honestly** (never sleeps past it), and the export path gets
its own pacing floor while list/identity keep the ≥ 3 s general pace. The 091 UA pin, the single-flight
gate and the quota itself are untouched: no IP rotation, no parallel clients, no header games.

**TC-MDF-127 — The durable access fix: a 429 is a named outcome with one bounded re-issue and never a ladder, a `Retry-After` is honored verbatim in both forms, a backoff past the per-item bound truncates the walk with its own reason instead of sleeping, the export floor delays detail fetches and only them, and the TLS shape is config-equivalence-pinned with the honest UA unchanged** (new v1.51)
- Traces: FR-MDF-002 (a lane that cannot reach the source fetches nothing — this is the reachability half), NFR-MDF-001 (the transport/quota failures were invisible ledger holes — now named and bounded), FR-MDF-012 (the 429 outcome is named and counted), FR-MDF-016; `01` v1.5.77 §10.9.19 (the access-posture amendment: the fingerprint/quota findings and the option-(c) ruling) and §10.9.19's 079 lane / 091 UA pin (frozen input); TKT-foundation-091 (the UA-level precedent this escalates from) · Level: L2
- Design: a client-behaviour matrix over {response: `200`, `429` then `200`, `429` persistently, `429` with `Retry-After` delta-seconds, `429` with `Retry-After` HTTP-date, `429` whose demanded wait exceeds the route's per-item bound} × {route: detail/export, list/probe} × {outcome: body read, one bounded re-issue after the recorded wait, `QUOTA_RATE_LIMITED` after the bounded attempts, `WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF` deferred with **no** sleep and **no** re-issue} plus a config-equivalence matrix over the shipped `appsettings.json` TLS/pace keys × the code defaults × the handler `KapPublicHttpHandlerFactory` builds
- Preconditions: the production `KapPublicHttpSourceClient` over a `RecordingHandler` double (no network) and a `FakeTimeProvider` with an injectable quota-delay delegate that **records** each demanded wait instead of sleeping; the production `KapPublicHttpRequestPacer` with the same clock and recorder; the committed `kap-public-report-1643141` body as the successful detail payload; the shipped `src/Api/appsettings.json` read from disk; **no CI test calls kap.org.tr**.
- Steps: (a) answer the first detail request `429` (empty body, no `Retry-After`) and the second `200`, read the body, the request journal and the recorded waits; then answer every request `429`, read the exception, its reason/deferral/required wait, the journal and the waits, and drive the same refusing client through the production `KapPublicSourceReader` and read the outcome; (b) answer the first request `429` with `Retry-After: 7` seconds (delta form) and the second `200`, then the same with an HTTP-date five seconds ahead, and read the journal and the single recorded wait of each; (c) answer `429` with a `Retry-After` of 120 s on a route whose per-item bound is 60 s — read the exception and the journal — then answer the general list route `429` with the **configured** 8 s backoff under a 3 s bound, and finally the same 8 s backoff under a 30 s bound: `429` (no `Retry-After`) on the first request and `200` on the second, read the journal, the single recorded wait and the recorded pace; (c2) drive the production `KapPublicStatementsWalker` over one pass whose detail read is deferred (a `429` asking 120 s on the 60 s detail bound), read the walk result, the persisted cursor and the depth row, then walk again over the same stored cursor with that report answered `200` and read the second result and the cursor; (d) with an export floor of 12,000 ms and a general pace of 3,000 ms, issue probe → detail → probe → detail and read the journal order and the recorded gaps; then re-run with the floor at 0, and again with the general pace disabled and the floor set; (e) read the shipped `appsettings.json` `Ingestion:KapPublic` keys, bind them onto `KapPublicSourceOptions`, compare them key-by-key with the code defaults, build the handler through the production factory and read its TLS floor, ALPN offer and cipher-suite policy, build it with the shaping stood down, and read the shipped UA.
- Expected: **(a)** the recovered read returns the committed report body, the journal holds **exactly two** requests, and the single recorded wait is `DefaultQuotaBackoffMs` (8,000 ms) — one bounded re-issue, never a ladder; the persistently refusing read throws `KapPublicQuotaException` with reason `QUOTA_RATE_LIMITED`, `Deferred = false`, required wait 8,000 ms, the journal holding exactly `MaxQuotaAttempts` (2) requests and exactly one recorded wait; the reader reports `IsSourceDown` true, `IsQuotaDeferred` false and reason `QUOTA_RATE_LIMITED` — the ledger never records it as a plain source-down. **(b)** both forms re-issue exactly once and the single recorded wait is **exactly** the source's own ask — 7 s for the delta form, 5 s for the date form — never the configured fixed backoff, never rounded or capped. **(c)** the 120 s ask against the 60 s bound throws with `Deferred = true`, reason `WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF`, required wait 120 s and a message naming the per-item bound; the journal holds **one** request and **no** wait was recorded (nothing was slept past the bound); the reader sees `IsQuotaDeferred` and `IsSourceDown` true and `IsOk` false with reason `WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF`; the 8 s configured backoff under the 3 s bound defers the same way (one request, no wait), while under the 30 s bound it is slept and re-issued **exactly once** — the journal holds exactly **two** requests, exactly one wait of 8,000 ms is recorded, the `200` body is read with no exception, and with a 3 s general pace configured the re-issue records exactly one 3 s pace wait so it is never issued back-to-back — a **bound**, not a blanket refusal. **(c2)** the walker's own seam: the deferred detail read truncates the pass (`Truncated` true, reason `WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF`, no `Failure`, one window listed and none walked, one report listed and none fetched, no skips, nothing inserted, no wait slept); the persisted cursor still sits at the window's start, the report is not in its seen set and no replay tombstone was spent, and the depth row records `truncated: true` with the reason; the next pass over that stored cursor lists the same window, fetches and writes the report (`Truncated` false, one window walked, one inserted) and advances the cursor to the window's end — the resume, proven rather than inferred. **(d)** the journal order is `[probe, report, probe, report]` and the three recorded gaps are exactly `[12 s, 3 s, 12 s]` — the export floor on the detail path, the unchanged general pace on the list path; with the floor at 0 the single gap is 3 s (the export path falls back to the general pace), and with the general pace disabled both paths sleep nothing (the L2 harness binding). **(e)** every shipped key equals its code default — `ExportRequestDelayMs` 12000, `QuotaBackoffMs` 8000, `TlsShapeEnabled` `"true"`, `TlsMinimumVersion` `"Tls12"`, `TlsAlpnProtocols` `"h2,http/1.1"`, the 11-suite `TlsCipherSuites` list — the shipped values bound onto the options equal the code defaults, and the factory's handler offers **exactly** `Tls12 | Tls13` and **exactly** `[h2, http/1.1]`; the cipher-suite policy carries the 11 parsed suites in order on a platform that lets a client order them (Linux) and is **null** on Windows (a recorded platform fact, never faked); with the shaping stood down the handler carries no TLS opinions (`SslProtocols.None`, null policy, no ALPN) — the pre-ticket stack; the shipped `RequestDelayMs` is unchanged (3,000 ms) and the shipped UA equals `DefaultUserAgent`, contains `Degerli/0.1` and not `DegerliBot` — the 091 pin frozen.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-103 (§20 — the lane's pacing, UA and access-posture contract this extends; its pins unchanged and re-run green); TC-MDF-121 (§32 — the walk's truncation/budget seam the quota deferral rides); TC-MDF-105/126 (the identity lane's pace and UA behaviour unchanged); `KapPublicSourceTests`, `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests`, `KapPublicIdentityTests`, `KapPublicIdentityJobTests` re-run green. **Not** this section's business: any UA change (091's pin is frozen input), any politeness regression (single-flight and the ≥ 3 s general pace stand), any quota circumvention (no IP rotation, parallel clients or header games), any walk-budget semantics change beyond the honest truncation, any identity/decode/store/serving work (099/101 and the wave own those), and the relay's live dev override (an orchestrator ops step). The **live criterion** (sustained export fetches through the adapter's own fingerprint without the transport burst; a 429 named, backed off and resumed — counts verbatim) is the orchestrator's post-merge paced probe with the relay **disabled**, never CI.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the sub-cases are **(a)**, **(b)**, **(c)**, **(c2)**, **(d)**, **(e)** here, in v1.51, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.77. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_127_a_a_429_is_named_with_one_bounded_reissue_and_never_a_ladder` = (a), `…_b_a_retry_after_header_is_honored_verbatim` = (b), `…_c_a_backoff_past_the_per_item_bound_truncates_instead_of_sleeping` = (c), `…_c2_a_deferred_quota_read_truncates_the_walk_at_its_own_seam_and_resumes` = (c2), `…_d_the_export_pacing_knob_delays_detail_fetches_and_only_them` = (d), `…_e_the_tls_shape_is_config_equivalence_pinned` = (e). (c2) carries a letter suffix rather than a new letter because it is the same claim as (c) one layer up — the deferral proven at the walker's own seam instead of the reader's — the `TC-MDF_105_a2` naming precedent. No TC id is renamed.

## 39. The refused-payload observability rule and the bare-currency presentation declaration (TKT-foundation-101, architecture v1.5.80 §10.9.19)

A statements payload the store refuses used to leave the system unable to say **why**. Two holes, one
finding: the refusal path wrote its `quarantined_facts` row with a **NULL** payload
(`StatementsJob.cs:576` passed `null`), and the log line named only the bare reason code
(`statements: refused public-site payload for GUBRF (SCHEMA_MISMATCH)`). On 2026-10-28 that produced
292 undiagnosable `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` rows across companies; GUBRF's five fetched reports were among
them and nothing about them survived except the word. The second hole was the cause of the first
refusal wave: the committed `1605802` body declares `Sunum Para Birimi: TL` — a **currency and no
multiplier** — and the decoder's `^(number) (unit)$` grammar could not read it, so scale and currency
both came back null and the store refused the whole filing. A declaration naming a currency and no
multiplier says **one unit per currency unit**: it is the store's `units` scale, not a missing
declaration, and inferring a multiplier for it would be the guess 02 §3.1 forbids. This section pins
both halves — the evidence and the detail a future refusal must keep, and the ruled decode of the
bare form — with the five pre-existing committed bodies as the non-regression fence.

**TC-MDF-128 — A refused payload keeps bounded evidence and a log line that names the clause that fired, a bare-currency presentation declaration reads as units and the filing decodes and stores, and every pre-existing committed body's decode is unchanged** (new v1.56)
- Traces: FR-MDF-003 (statement decode correctness — a whole company refused for one readable field), FR-MDF-012 (named/counted skips — a bare `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` with NULL evidence names nothing), NFR-MDF-001 (no silent ledger hole — the NULL-payload rows are holes in the evidence), FR-MDF-016; TKT-foundation-092 (the replay doctrine the post-merge re-walk rides), TKT-foundation-093/098 (the decoder surface this diagnoses — frozen input, no map entry, canonical code or fail-safe changed); `01` v1.5.80 §10.9.19 (the observability rule and the bare-currency reading) and 02 §3.1 (the mandatory scale/currency declaration this keeps mandatory) · Level: L2
- Design: a two-branch matrix — {a store-refused public-site payload (the committed interim body with its declaration rewritten to an unknown currency)} × {what the row and the log must carry} for the observability half, and {the committed `1605802` body} × {header reading, statement set, store acceptance} for the decode half, closed by a non-regression table over the five pre-existing committed bodies
- Preconditions: the production `statements` job runner over the production composition, the lane's paced single-flight client retargeted at WireMock with pacing bound to 0, a real migrated Testcontainers PostgreSQL, and the committed bodies `kap-public-report-1643141` (mutated in memory for the refusal case) and `kap-public-report-1605802` (the ticket's committed capture, honestly labelled **ASTOR ENERJİ A.Ş.** index `1605802` — not a GUBRF body; no GUBRF fact is manufactured); **no CI test calls kap.org.tr**.
- Steps: (a) run one `statements` pass whose listed interim report answers the committed body with `1.000 TL` rewritten to `1.000 QAR`, read the run result, the `quarantined_facts` row for `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` and the captured log; (b) decode the committed `1605802` body and read its header, findings, statement set and canonical values, then write the same payload through the production `FinancialStatementStore` and read the write result; (c) decode each of the five pre-existing committed bodies and read its presentation-unit reading, decode size, finding count and conflict set.
- Expected: **(a)** the run is `partial`, writes nothing, and counts three quarantines (the refused interim plus the two reports the double answers a 500 for); the `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` row's **`payload_json` is not null** and is bounded (under 16 KiB) — a JSON document naming `symbol`, the `kapPublicDisclosureIndex`, the `reasonCode`, the filing's identity block with `presentationUnit` **`1.000 QAR`** and `unitScale` null, the decode's `statementCount`/`lineCount`/`findings` (with `findingsTruncated`), and a **`detail`** that names the clause that fired (`never guessed`) and quotes the declaration (`Sunum Para Birimi '1.000 QAR'`); the log carries one `refused public-site payload for ASELS (SCHEMA_MISMATCH): …` line containing that same detail — the bare-word line is gone. **(b)** the header reads `PresentationUnit` `TL`, `UnitScale` **`units`**, `Currency` `TRY`, `CompanyName` `ASTOR ENERJİ A.Ş.`, submitted 2026-05-11, `3 Aylık`, `Konsolide`, with **zero** `PRESENTATION_UNIT_UNRECOGNISED` findings; two `BS` and two `IS` statements decode with no conflicts (`TOTAL_ASSETS` 58.630.702.726 / `TOTAL_EQUITY` 38.403.565.895 at 2026-03-31; `REV` 9.286.457.701 / `GROSS_PROFIT` 3.524.361.357, `Q`, 3 months, at 2026-03-31); the store accepts the payload — no rejected reason codes, exactly **four** inserted statements — the refusal the live lane produced is gone rather than papered over. **(c)** the five bodies keep `thousands`/`thousands`/`thousands`/`thousands`/`millions` with `TRY`, zero `PRESENTATION_UNIT_UNRECOGNISED` findings, their line/statement/finding counts (116/3, 346/20, 383/35, 314/22, 497/41 — six statements each) and null `ConflictingItemCodes` throughout: the bare-currency branch is **additive**, so every multiplier-bearing declaration keeps the reading it always had.
- Dependencies: TC-MDF-106 (§22 — the presentation-unit reading and its unrecognised-unit refusal, whose `1.000 XAU` case this extends without weakening); TC-MDF-122/125 (§33/§36 — the five committed bodies' goldens, re-run unchanged); TC-MDF-109/110/121 (§23/§32 — the lane's counts, evidence and replay, whose suites re-run green); `KapPublicReportDecoderTests`, `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests`, `RealSourceStatementsTests`, `StatementNormalizationTests`, `StatementVersioningTests` re-run green. **Not** this section's business: any map entry, canonical code or fail-safe change (the 093/098 surface is frozen input), any store/schema change (the payload column exists — this section writes it), any serving/UI work, any capture beyond the one committed body, and any attempt to make the decoder accept everything — a named specific refusal was an equally valid ruling and is not exercised here because the diagnosis ruled the decode gap.
- **Case lettering (one vocabulary, three surfaces):** the sub-cases are **(a)**, **(b)**, **(c)** here, in v1.56, on the ticket and in `01` v1.5.80. The shipped method names carry the test-file's own separator and map one-to-one: `TC_MDF_128_a_a_refused_payload_keeps_bounded_evidence_and_the_log_names_the_detail` = (a), `TC_MDF_128_b_a_bare_currency_declaration_reads_as_units_and_the_filing_decodes` = (b), `TC_MDF_128_c_the_five_committed_bodies_keep_their_decodes` = (c). No TC id is renamed.

 **v1.13 2026-10-22 — §14 share-count store cases added: TC-MDF-092** (the `shares` lane's decode + honest store from VYK `/memberSecurities`) **and TC-MDF-093** (the balance-sheet serving seam), mirroring architecture v1.5.36 §10.9.11 / TKT-foundation-058 (which supersedes the TKT-foundation-033 goal — the source was never the XBRL disclosure layer, it is the gateway's `/memberSecurities`). The lane writes one row per **matched** instrument, the value being the sum of that instrument's security lines' `currentCapital` (the tertip-aggregation rule the live ASELS A+B = 4.56B / THYAO C+A = 1.38B / ISDMR 2.9B shapes evidence); matching is `borsaKodu`-primary / `isin`-fallback, keyed on a trading-open line, and an instrument with no line — or only closed lines — gets **no row**, never a zero (the 031/052 doctrine). A line without `currentCapital` is skipped and counted, and its matched group is withheld rather than served as a partial sum. The re-run is input-aware (identical → `unchanged` and no `asOf` movement; changed → repaired in place and counted). The serving seam adds an **additive** `shareCount { sharesOutstanding, asOf }` block to `GET /api/v1/stocks/{symbol}/balance-sheet` — honest-null when absent, never `0` — leaving the statement's own `lines.sharesOutstanding`/`sharesDiluted`/BVPS figures untouched. Doubles only, through the production job runner and the L2 harness; the committed `CannedSourceCatalog.MemberSecuritiesOk` double is derived from the 2026-10-22 dev-gateway capture (the ephemeral `/tmp` snapshot is not referenced) and carries two explicitly labeled **derived anomalies** (no-`currentCapital` lines) because the capture itself had none while the contract requires the skip path exercised. Implemented as `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/MemberSecuritiesIngestTests.cs` (new, 10 cases after the review-round-1 additions — the trading-open flag's string/numeric spellings, the single-object member body, and the withheld-group stale-row removal) plus one case in `StockFundamentalsEndpointsTests.cs`; red→green evidence is recorded in the ticket's PR body (7 failed / 1 passed on the pre-implementation behaviour, 8/8 green after; the golden-stability suites — `StockFundamentalsEndpointsTests` 18/18, `CannedSourceCatalogTests`/`VykCompanyDirectoryTests`/`VykStatementsSourceTests` 27/27 — re-run green). Coverage matrix: TC-MDF-092 → FR-MDF-002/003/008/010/011, TC-MDF-093 → FR-MDF-011. The **live unit/aggregation cross-validation** (ASELS/THYAO/TUPRS/GARAN/AEFES served values vs the known real counts, one paced `/memberSecurities` fetch on the dev stack) is the ticket's post-merge orchestrator step and is recorded on the ticket, not asserted here. A genuine source-down — a directory/list transport failure or a non-ER005 status on every company — still ends `failed` so the retry ladder stays honest. **TC-MDF-097:** a persisted cursor past the gateway tail that answers HTTP 400 `{"code":"ER005","message":"Bildirim bulunamadı. Disclosure not found."}` triggers exactly one reset from `EarlyDisclosureIndex`; the reset is recorded verbatim, the re-walked facts are written, and the corrected cursor is persisted (or cleared when the re-walk is itself honest-empty) so the next run makes one list call and repeats no stale reset; a non-ER005 400/500 is not recovered as an honest empty. **v1.18 2026-10-24 — §17 disclosures-lane throughput cases added (TC-MDF-099/100/101) and implemented (TKT-foundation-078's implementation lane).** Mirroring architecture v1.5.52 §10.9.15/§10.9.11. **TC-MDF-099:** a company whose archive spans a huge index range (the run-208 shape — 2,000 dense rows far below the head, ≈41 pages from zero) completes inside the configured page budget and pages only the head window; a cursor-less company never starts at index 0; the seed is recorded as provenance (a counted `BOOTSTRAPPED` log line plus a per-symbol stat with `written 0`/`failed false`) and never as "no disclosures"; and the window is configuration (a 200-position window starts at `head − 200`), with the shipped defaults (5,000 / 1,000 / 24 / 200) pinned as constants. **TC-MDF-100:** a persisted cursor is honoured unchanged — one incremental page, no re-walk, no re-bootstrap, `0` written / `0` unchanged on the second run (the disclosures lane does not replay its cursor row) — while a stale cursor past the tail catches `400 ER005` and resets **once** to the bounded window, never to 0, recording and persisting the correction so the next run makes one call with no second reset. **TC-MDF-101:** the market-wide feed (`Feed = market`, the shipped default) pages `GET /disclosures?disclosureIndex={n}` with **no** `companyId`, writes only bound-universe rows, counts + logs (`OUTSIDE_BOUND_UNIVERSE`) the rest without fetching their details, advances and resumes its own `scope = market` cursor in one page, treats a market `ER005` as an honest empty that persists no cursor (never a backwards reset), keeps a genuine list `500` a `failed` run, and — under `MaxListPages = 1` — truncates, persists the last **completed** index and resumes exactly there. Everything is L2 doubles through the production job runner (WireMock `/members`, `/lastDisclosureIndex`, `/disclosures`, `/disclosureDetail`; **no live gateway call**), pacing bound to 0. Red→green: with the pre-078 from-zero start restored, **3 failed / 5 passed** — the huge-range company writes `0` rows instead of `2` because the from-zero walk exhausts its 24-page budget inside the dense archive, the configuration case starts at `0`, and the stale-cursor case's reset target is `0` — **8/8 green** after; the golden-stability suites re-run green (`StatementsAndEventsIngestionTests` 55/55 incl. 034/046/061/063, `VykCompanyDirectoryTests`, `VykStatementsSourceTests`, `EodChainTests`, `MorningChainTests`, `SchedulerTests`, `EodStallWatchdogTests`, `MarketSnapshotTests`, `MetricsRecomputeIdempotencyTests`, `EodStaleServingTests` — 113/113) and `dotnet build` is clean. The ticket's live criterion (a paced dev-stack run with `kap_disclosures` > 0, the newest stored index within ~50 of `lastDisclosureIndex`, a completed pass and an incremental second run) is the orchestrator's post-merge step, recorded on the ticket, not an L2 gate. Applied by the 078 implementation lane under the delivery planner's authored cases; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.19 2026-10-24 — §17's TC-MDF-100/101 amended by TKT-foundation-078's review round 1 (four correctness repairs).** The cases are pinned to the repaired behaviour: **(100 c)** a stale-cursor reset whose re-walk is honest-empty **clears** the cursor row (the 063 precedent) instead of leaving it to repeat the identical two-call reset every run — the ticket's "must not trigger an infinite or repeated reset" pin, which the shipped lane previously violated; **(100 d)** a persisted cursor no longer depends on `/lastDisclosureIndex` (a named `BOOTSTRAP_ANCHOR_UNAVAILABLE` warning, one incremental page, no failure) — the anchor is a secondary read and only a bootstrap window needs it; **(101 e)** a **30-row page followed by rows at higher indexes** is paged past — the market walk terminates on "the page did not advance the cursor" plus reaching the platform head, never on a short page (the live gateway serves 39–48-row pages with rows above them, so the row-count heuristic would advance ~48 positions per run and miss the live criterion); and **(101 a)** now pins the walk's head-confirmation page (`["1230017", "1230066", "1230077"]` for a double whose rows stop below the head) and that the resumed cursor's own boundary row is **not** re-fetched (one paced detail call saved per incremental run). No assertion was weakened: every amended case fails at the pre-round head (7 failed / 4 passed) and the 078 lane is 11/11 green after. The company walk keeps its pre-078 short-page and non-advancing-page guards. Applied by the 078 fix lane from its round-1 findings (DR-3/4/5/7); test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.20 2026-10-24 — §17's TC-MDF-101 extended with the wall-clock deadline cases (TKT-foundation-078 review round 2, DR-1).** The walk now enforces `Ingestion:Disclosures:MaxWalkDuration` (shipped 90 min, strictly below the 120-min per-step bound) through one deadline per pass on the injected `TimeProvider`, checked before every list and detail request; on expiry it records `truncated`, persists the last **completed** index (and every completed company's cursor) and the next run resumes there. **(101 f)** pins that a fake clock crossing a 10-min deadline truncates a market walk after four details, persists `1230020`, and the next run resumes there without re-fetching the boundary row; **(101 g)** pins that `MaxWalkDuration ≤ 0` disables the deadline (all six rows written); the shipped-config guard pins `DefaultMaxWalkDuration < DefaultMaxStepDuration` and config ↔ code equality. The tests are deterministic (a stepping fake clock, no real sleeps) and L2 doubles only. Applied by the 078 fix lane from its round-2 finding; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.21 2026-10-24 — §18 BIST holiday calendar case added: TC-MDF-087 (delivery-planner-applied on the ticket; authored here with the implementation lane),** mirroring architecture v1.5.44 §10.9.13 (extended) / TKT-foundation-048 (planner pins: the calendar is a static configuration list seeded from the official Borsa İstanbul publication — never a live source; `weekend ∪ configured holiday` is non-trading; a gated stream is never silent). The case spans the three surfaces the doctrine reaches: the **calendar** semantics (`TradingCalendarTests` — weekend ∪ holiday, the last-trading-day walk across a weekend / a holiday Monday / a plateau / a Fri–Mon closure cluster, the shipped list equal to the publication, the shipped `TradingDaysOnly` posture, a malformed entry rejected), the **serve-side** freshness (`MarketOverviewFreshnessTests` — a stored row dated on a configured holiday no longer makes Friday's served row stale; a holiday Monday with only Friday covered reads fresh; and a genuinely newer *trading* session still reads stale), and the **gate** (`EodChainTests` — a holiday occurrence writes one dated `holiday-skip` row and fetches nothing; `SchedulerTests` — the intraday per-job cadence is gated and explains itself once per holiday, and the amended `TC_MDF_011_Holiday_calendar_is_config` asserts the skip row instead of the superseded empty ledger). Doubles only, never a live source; the holiday list is configuration and is asserted against the published dates as a constant. Implemented as `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/TradingCalendarTests.cs` (new), plus three cases in `MarketOverviewFreshnessTests.cs`, one in `EodChainTests.cs` and one in `SchedulerTests.cs`; red→green evidence is recorded in the ticket's PR body (7 failed / 26 passed across the four classes before the fix — the holiday-freshness `stale: true`, the missing `holiday-skip` rows, the ungated per-job stream and the unseeded shipped list — 33/33 green after). **Honest limits recorded, not asserted:** the list is static (a newly declared closure reads `stale` until configured — the conservative direction), past-2026 closures are not re-seeded, and `StockOverviewAndValuationEndpoints.FreshnessAsync` keeps its pre-048 weekday rule (a separate semantic outside this ticket's acceptance, flagged for a follow-up). The ticket's live criterion (the next BIST holiday — or a temporarily-configured past holiday date, re-verified read-only — showing the overview fresh-not-stale on the following trading morning with the `holiday-skip` row present) is the orchestrator's post-merge step, recorded verbatim on the ticket, not an L2 gate.
