**v1.24 2026-10-25 — §16's TC-MDF-113 added and implemented (TKT-foundation-085's implementation lane),** mirroring architecture v1.5.59 §10.9.17 (amended: the post-cancel re-check is a bounded settle window). The defect is the one PR #141's review round 2 escalated (E3) in **merged 059-heritage code**: the watchdog re-checked `IsCompletedSuccessfully` **once, synchronously**, after cancelling a bound-expired attempt, which sees a step's terminal state only when its continuation resumed **inline on the cancelling thread** — when the runtime queues it (the normal async case under load) the check sees a pending task, writes `RUN_HUNG`, and the queued continuation then writes the step's own success row: **two `ingest_runs` rows for one attempt, a spurious alert, a spurious retry** — and the CI flake class of `TC_MDF_079_DR_2` on any head. **TC-MDF-113(a)** restructures that case as a *strengthening*: the swallowing double gains one `await Task.Yield()` so its continuation is **queued**; before the fix the collection deterministically holds two rows (3/3 runs: the step's `succeeded` row plus the `RUN_HUNG` row), after it exactly one — the DR-2 assertion set (one row, `attempt: 1`, no `reasonCode`, no `RUN_HUNG` row, no alert, no retry, no exhausted alert) asserted verbatim. **TC-MDF-079 DR-2 is not deleted or relaxed** — the inline-resume half is kept beside it. **TC-MDF-113(b)** pins 064's unweakened guarantee under the grace: a never-completing step under a 10-min per-step bound and a configured 30 s window is named once (`bound: "step"`, `maxStepDurationSeconds`, **`settleWindowSeconds: 30`**), alerted once (step, bound, grace), logged once (`Hung run detected` incl. the grace, plus a `Settling …` warning when the grace is armed), recorded with `finished_at` = the bound's instant, and the ladder recovers the stream with no second `RUN_HUNG`. **TC-MDF-113(c)** pins the window as configuration and as a *grace*: shipped default 5 s, bound from `Ingestion:Scheduler:PostCancelSettleWindow`, two orders of magnitude below the shortest ladder rung (5 min) and both bounds (120 min / 4 h), and `0` disables it — the verdict is then reached with no clock advance past a grace and the record claims none (`settleWindowSeconds: 0`). The grace itself is `Task.Delay(window, timeProvider)` on the injected clock (**no real `Task.Delay` bounds any case**); the row's timestamps and the retry base stay the **attempt's own end**, so the grace never shifts the ladder. **One harness-only delta, premise-preserving and assertion-neutral (064's TC-MDF-081(b) precedent):** every hung-path case that ends in `RUN_HUNG` (`SchedulerTests` DR-3; `EodStallWatchdogTests` TC-MDF-079 ×2, TC-MDF-098(b)/responsive/(c)/zero-step/zero-chain) completes its tick through an `AwaitTickAsync(tick, host, window)` overload that waits for the watchdog's `Settling …` line — the observable side of an armed clock delay — and then advances the fake clock by the whole window in one deterministic step; no expected value or assertion changed. **Residual recorded honestly:** a cancellation-swallowing step completing *after* the window still lands its row beside `RUN_HUNG` (production writes through the attempt token, so no second row is written there); the declared durable follow-up is the single per-attempt claim shared through `IngestionRunContext` into `IngestionJobRunner.RecordAsync`, pre-declared in the ticket's scope (`src/Ingestion/Jobs/Jobs.cs`) as shape (b). Red→green: **1 failed / 26 passed** across the two in-scope classes with the pre-085 single synchronous re-check restored (the 113(a) double-row collection; 113(b)/(c) are guards and hold before and after), **27/27 green** after (3 consecutive runs), plus `EodChainTests`/`MorningChainTests`/`EodStaleServingTests`/`FundSchedulingCoverageTests`/`MacroIngestionTests` **31/31**. **Deploy sequencing (orchestrator, load-bearing):** merge freely, but do not deploy to the dev box until 064's live criterion (the Monday 2026-10-26 05:30 UTC chain, zero `RUN_HUNG` on shipped defaults) has been observed and recorded verbatim. Applied by the delivery planner per the ticket's allocation (test-planner confirmation pending — the v1.2/v1.3 precedent); the 089–091 IDs remain permanently unused per the v1.23 record.

**v1.25 2026-10-25 — §21's TC-MDF-105 implemented: the stored public-oid ↔ instrument identity map (TKT-foundation-080's implementation lane),** mirroring architecture v1.5.54 §10.9.19 and `02` v1.5 §3.1/§8. The case is unchanged in scope; three implementation-time clarifications are recorded here so a later reader does not have to infer them from code. **(1) The "three-space table" is literal — one row, three space-labelled columns, each with its own provenance.** `kap_public_company_map` (PK `instrument_id`; `kap_public_oid` + `kap_public_oid_source` + `kap_public_oid_as_of`; `vyk_company_id` + `vyk_company_id_source` + `vyk_company_id_as_of`, nullable as a pair; `recorded_at`) records ASELS's public oid **and** its VYK `866` as facts about different spaces; the VYK id is written only from VYK-space evidence a run actually holds (the case takes `866` out of the committed `/memberSecurities` capture rather than typing it in) and is never derived from the oid. TC-MDF-105 (iii) is therefore asserted three ways: the two facts coexisting in their own columns, the types (`PublicCompanyOid`, `VykCompanyId`) exposing **no** conversion between them, and the **store itself** refusing a VYK id in the oid column and an oid in the VYK column (`ck_kap_public_company_map_oid_shape` / `ck_kap_public_company_map_vyk_shape`, SQLSTATE 23514, asserted by constraint name). **(2) An ambiguous identity answer gets its own named reason.** (ii)'s "never a guess" is pinned by a *derived* (non-capture) two-oid answer: the symbol is skipped and named `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` — a new code in the public lane's vocabulary, recorded beside `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` (which stays the **same** 044 word the VYK lane records; the sources stay apart by evidence class, not by spelling). **(3) The store, not the run, is the authority.** (i) reads the oid back through `KapPublicCompanyMapStore` in a fresh scope and then issues the byCriteria query with that stored oid — the committed ten-row ASELS FR capture answers — so "stored, never assumed" is observable end-to-end. **Red→green, honestly:** red-1 with the seam absent (the suite does not compile: `CS0246` on `KapPublicInstrumentBinding`/`KapPublicIdentityResolutionResult`), red-2 with the seam present but the migration withheld (**5 failed / 0 passed**; the observed message: `42P01: relation "kap_public_company_map" does not exist`), **5/5 green** with the migration in place. **Non-regression:** `KapPublicSourceTests`, `VykCompanyDirectoryTests`, `MemberSecuritiesIngestTests`, `MigrationsAndSeedTests`, `RealSourcePricesTests`, `CannedSourceCatalogTests` **54/54 green** (the VYK adapter, its directory and every VYK cursor untouched). **Doubles only — zero live kap.org.tr requests in this lane** (user politeness directive, 2026-10-25); the live criterion stays the orchestrator's post-merge step (paced resolution over XU100, resolved/unresolved counts recorded verbatim, unresolved symbols listed by name). Applied by the delivery planner per the ticket's allocation; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.28 2026-10-26 — §23's TC-MDF-110 amended (the overlap-read clause split out) and §24's TC-MDF-111 extended with the deferred read-preference rule — the recorded 082 blocker's remedy B, executed on the user's decision B of 2026-10-26 (~01:50 UTC).** Rationale: TKT-foundation-082's implementation lane blocked on TC-MDF-110's overlap expectation — the pinned preference rule ("for a period both sources cover … public-site current wins for the periods it covers; VYK rows retained and as-of queryable") is unsatisfiable inside 082's frozen scope, because the statement store keys facts without source (`FinancialStatementStore.cs:283,157-163`), `version` is the restatement sequence (`02` §91-93), and the served payload has no source preference (`StockFundamentalsEndpoints.cs:583-587`); every in-scope workaround was dishonest (a silent `unchanged` swallow, a forbidden cross-source in-place overwrite, or a false `restated` declaration corrupting `is_restated`). The user chose the split remedy. **TC-MDF-110 (amended):** the overlap expectation becomes — for a period both sources cover, the public-site fact is **not written** and the overlap is surfaced as a named, counted `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` finding (the VYK row retained as-is, as-of queryable, nothing overwritten, nothing swallowed silently, no false restatement flag); the coexistence, per-row evidence-class and no-cross-source-overwrite assertions are unchanged and exactly as strong. **TC-MDF-111 (extended):** the deferred read-preference rule lands as a sub-clause of the existing §24 case — the blocker remedy-B wording, and the only reading that keeps 083's Acceptance line accurate (the ID was already taken by this same ticket's v1.23 recompute case, so no new ID was minted and nothing was renumbered): with `source_ref` in the store's natural key (one forward-only migration, 083's scope) the mixed-world read verification runs — both sources present for a period → the public-site current fact is served; public absent → the VYK fact is served; an as-of query reaches both — and the served payload selects by the pinned preference in `StockFundamentalsEndpoints.Pick`, never a silent latest-write and never an order-nondeterministic tie-break. **No coverage was deleted:** the moved clause re-emerges as TC-MDF-111's overlap-read sub-clause with the store+persistence+API scope that makes it true; the split strengthens 110's write path (the overlap is named and counted, never silently swallowed as `unchanged`). Version note: v1.27 was the landed head (081's review round 4); v1.28 is genuinely next-free — no in-flight reservation exists in any committed ticket or the live 082/086 worktrees (082's ticket records only the architecture v1.5.56 soft reservation, not a test-plan version). Sequencing note for the 111 lane *(supersession clause corrected v1.34, USER DECISION DR-1 option (ii), 2026-10-26 — the original clause is withdrawn, see v1.34)*: TC-MDF-110's `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` skip is verified by 082 against the pre-migration store and **stands unchanged in 083** — the source-in-key migration makes the overlapped public fact *storable* as its own row and the pinned read preference *serves* it wherever both rows exist (the hand-seeded proof of TC-MDF-111), but the production write of an overlapped public-site fact stays withheld by the lane and is a recorded residue routed to a future overlap-supersession slice; v1.31's "(c) the write side is untouched" is the true implementation record. **v1.29 2026-10-26 — §23 implemented (TKT-foundation-082's implementation lane; §14 change-propagation).** TC-MDF-109 (i)–(v) and TC-MDF-110 (as amended by v1.28) are implemented in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicStatementsIngestTests.cs` — **16 cases** (12 at implementation; 15 after review round 1: the two-company per-pass-ceiling case, the mid-walk list-outage case and the cross-source period-type-disagreement case, with the wall-clock case rewritten as a walk-level deadline case rather than a budget-unit drive; 16 after review round 2: the pass-order case that proves the fair ordering); every one tagged `TC_MDF_109`/`TC_MDF_110` — against L2 doubles only: the entire input is the committed `kap-public-bycriteria-asels-fr` list and the committed `kap-public-report-1643141` body, served by WireMock through the production paced single-flight client. **No test calls kap.org.tr; this lane issued zero live requests** (user politeness directive, 2026-10-25). Implementation-time clarifications recorded so a later reader need not infer them from code. **(1) The request's *window* is the wire assertion, the report's *selection* is the journal assertion.** The only committed report **body** is the 6-month interim `1643141`; the annual `1561039` is listed by the committed capture but has no committed body, so no case fakes an annual column set (v1.27's ledger already says the FY basis has no committed golden). Selection is therefore proven where it is honest: the byCriteria body carries the **stored** oid (`mkkMemberOidList`) and the class filter, and the export route journal shows `1643141` fetched while the `ODA`-categorised `1643142` — which shares the capture's `disclosureClass: FR` — never is. **(2) The financial-report discriminator is the site's own `disclosureCategory: "FR"`, not the class.** The committed ten-row capture carries **nine** `disclosureType: FR` rows and only **three** `disclosureCategory: FR` ones — exactly the three with the `Finansal Rapor` subject (`1643141`, `1598316`, `1561039`); the other six are responsibility statements and activity reports with no financial tables, which a class-only filter would fetch and quarantine as unparseable. **(3) The walk budget is per *pass*, not per company** (the politeness caps bound a run: page ceiling, detail ceiling and the 90-minute deadline are shared across the universe the run walks), so a wide universe cannot multiply the allowance; a bounded pass records `truncated` with its reason, persists the last **completed** report and the next pass resumes exactly there — asserted both for the detail ceiling and for the page ceiling wired from `Ingestion:KapPublic:MaxListPages`. **(4) Resume accumulates the seen set, it does not replace it** — a window that took two passes to walk has one seen set, so the second pass's completion cannot forget the first's completions (an early draft replaced it and re-fetched; the case now pins "the resumed pass fetches nothing it completed and writes no duplicate", and a completed pass re-runs with **zero** report requests). **(5) The overlap rule is per natural key, not per report.** Verified end-to-end on the interim: with the incumbent covering 2026-06-30 `Q`, the run quarantines exactly **3** `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` findings (IS/BS/CF at that period, each naming both provenances and the disclosure index) and still **writes 3** — the same report's 2025-06-30 and 2025-12-31 comparatives — while the incumbent rows keep `source_ref = kap`, `version = 0`, `restatement_date = null` and their line items (as-of queryable, nothing swallowed as `unchanged`, no false `restated`). The expected split is derived from the committed body through 081's decoder, never from this lane's output. **(6) Status vocabulary:** per-report degradation is a named counted skip (`DISCLOSURE_DETAIL_UNFETCHABLE` with the source's own body verbatim, or `UNPARSEABLE_PAYLOAD` with the body); an all-isolated zero-write run is `partial`, never `failed`; a genuine list source-down is `failed` with no rows and no quarantine (a transport failure is not a parse event); an unrecognised `Ingestion:Statements:Source` fails by name (`INVALID_SOURCE_SELECTION`) and fetches nothing from either source. **(7) Depth is provenance, not silence:** the configured lookback (`KapPublicLookbackMonths`, shipped 24) is a *request* — asserted from the byCriteria `fromDate`/`toDate` on the wire — and `kap-public-statements-depth` records requestedFrom/requestedTo against achievedFrom/achievedTo plus the bootstrap flag, so an unwalked range can never read as "no statements exist" (FR-MDF-010). Cursor and depth rows use their own data types (`kap-public-statements-cursor`, `kap-public-statements-depth`), outside `CoverageDataTypes.InstrumentScoped` and distinct from the VYK `kap-disclosure-cursor`, so the two index spaces cannot meet (a public `1643141` never enters the VYK cursor and a VYK `1231017` never enters the public one). **Red→green, honestly:** the implementation was authored before the cases in this pass, so the red evidence is recorded as what it is — red-1 with the pre-082 job restored, the suite does not compile against the missing seam (`CS0117` on `StatementsJob.InvalidSourceSelection`/`SourceOverlapRetained`); red-2 with the seam present and only the public dispatch withheld (the lane inert), **10 failed / 2 passed** on assertions, never on environment or syntax; **12/12 green** with the dispatch restored. **Review round 1 — 15/15.** The three added cases do not compile against `83cb557` (the reviewed head): `CS0117` on `KapPublicReasonCodes.WalkNotStarted` (three sites — the two-company case's discrimination is exactly that code, which did not exist before round 1) and on `StatementsJob.SourcePeriodTypeDisagreement`; `KapPublicStatementFailure` is absent at `83cb557` too, though no test names the type directly, so the compile red is the two `CS0117`s. With the seam present and only the behavioural remedy reverted to `83cb557`'s, each new case fails on its own assertion: the mid-walk case at `Assert.Equal(stored, result.Written)` — **Expected: 6, Actual: 0** (`KapPublicStatementsIngestTests.cs:536`: the old throw-and-zero-stat path reports a false zero beside six stored rows, the D1 lie); the period-type case at `Assert.Equal(writeableFacts.Count, result.Written)` — **Expected: 5, Actual: 6** (`:1126`: the withheld FY/Q collision is written as a duplicate row instead of `SOURCE_PERIOD_TYPE_DISAGREEMENT`). Both go green with the remedy restored; **15/15 green** after round 1. **Review round 2 — 16/16.** The pass-order case is red against the bare constituent order — `Assert.Equal(1, ListRequestsFor(ThyaoPublicOid))` **Expected: 1, Actual: 0** (`:670`: pass 2 re-walked the head company and the never-started company's list was never requested, i.e. the permanent starvation) — and green with the least-recently-walked order restored; **16/16 green** on the reviewed head. Two cases were corrected while going green and neither is a relaxation of a TC: the unmapped-company evidence is parsed as JSON (jsonb normalises key order and spacing, so a substring match on `"symbol":"THYAO"` was a harness artefact, not a contract), and the coexistence split is derived from the decoded report instead of a hand-counted constant (the interim carries six statement rows across 2026-06-30, 2025-06-30 and 2025-12-31, not the two periods first assumed). **Non-regression re-run green and unrelaxed:** the statements/kap-public/coverage/stock-fundamentals filter **177/177**, `EodChainTests`/`LiveActivationTests`/`BackfillAndCoverageTests`/`DcfFinancialSectorTests` **15/15**; `dotnet build` 0 warnings. the file's remaining fact is TC-MDF-115 (i)/(ii), tagged `TC_MDF_115`): the decoder carries `PD` beside the close; the store writes one row per instrument with `shares_outstanding = 4,560,000,000` and **both operands** + `price_date` + `source_ref` + `recorded_at` (the stored operands reproduce the stored count exactly); **v1.31 2026-10-26 — §24 implemented (TKT-foundation-083's implementation lane; §14 change-propagation, incl. the v1.28/v1.29-foretold re-pin of §23's overlap-write cases; **renumbered from v1.30 at the rebase over TKT-foundation-086's landing** — 086's §26 record holds v1.30 on `dev`, so this lane's three §24 records shift by one, no content changed).** TC-MDF-111 is implemented in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicRecomputeTests.cs` — **6 cases**, every one tagged `TC_MDF_111`, L2 doubles only through the production job runner and the in-process API host (`Docker`/Testcontainers PostgreSQL + the FU fixture world), **zero live kap.org.tr requests**. **The mixed world, seeded explicitly** (the FU fixture is the incumbent half; four symbols' statements are replaced after `FixtureSeeder.ApplyAsync`): `ALFA` holds **both** sources' FY2025 (`kap`: REV 1,000 / EBIT 100 / NI 200 / equity 1,000 / **shares 100**; `kap-public`: REV 2,000 / EBIT 400 / NI 400 / equity 2,000 and **no share count**) plus an incumbent-only FY2023 (REV 900); `BETA` is incumbent-only at FY2025; `GAMA` is public-site-only at FY2025; `UNSEC` is FY2023-deep and nothing newer. All values are stored canonical, so every golden below is hand-derived from the seed, never re-derived from the implementation. **The six cases:** (1) *recompute + formula version* — `metrics-recompute` writes the full **416** rows and **every** row carries `CanonicalMetrics.Version` (070's provenance), `medians` likewise; the preference is observable in the numbers (ALFA's operating margin **0.20** is the public-site fact — 0.10 would be the incumbent's — while ALFA's `pe`/`pb`/`ev_ebitda` are honestly **NULL** even though the retained incumbent row for the same period carries 100 M shares: no cross-source borrowing), BETA serves its incumbent (operating margin 0.10, `pe` **4** = 10.00 ÷ (200/80)), GAMA's current metrics come from the new lane (0.30 / 0.25), and UNSEC serves its 2023 facts (0.10 / 0.15). (2) *overlap read (i)* — `GET /api/v1/stocks/ALFA/financials` returns **one** FY2025 entry built wholly from the public-site row (REV 2,000, NI 400, FCF = OCF 300 − |CAPEX 100| = **200**), no false `restated` flag, the incumbent-only FY2023 period still served, and the store untouched: two IS rows (each `version 0`, no restatement date) with their own lines (REV 1,000 beside REV 2,000). (3) *overlap read (ii)+(iii)* — where the public site holds nothing the incumbent's fact is served (BETA FY2025 REV 500 / EBIT 50; ALFA FY2023 REV 900; UNSEC serves exactly `["2023-12-31"]`), and the as-of read (`published_at <= 2026-03-01` → the incumbent alone; `<= 2026-08-20` → **both** rows) proves the preference masks one row on read and deletes nothing. (4) *market P/E honesty* — the share-less current filing is **counted** in `market_pe_excluded_count`; deleting exactly that one public-site BS row makes the incumbent's count-bearing BS the served fact, the exclusion falls by **one**, the snapshot is repaired in place (one row per day) and `market_pe` moves — the 065 Option-A / BR-MOV-010 posture as a differential, never a fabricated cap. (5) *per-source coverage depth* — `CoverageRecorder.ReconcileUniverseAsync` writes `statements:kap` (2023-12-31 → 2025-12-31) **and** `statements:kap-public` (2025-12-31 only) for ALFA beside the aggregate union row, gives BETA/GAMA only the source that wrote there (**no row** for the source that wrote nothing — honest absence, never a zero), UNSEC reads 2023-deep per source, and the per-source family is asserted **outside** `CoverageDataTypes.InstrumentScoped`. (6) *store key* — the same period from a second source is a **new** version-0 fact (not a restatement, not an idempotent swallow), a replay within its own source is honestly `unchanged`, and the incumbent's restatement takes **version 1 while the public row stays 0**; `StatementSourcePreference.Pick` then serves the public row over the incumbent's *newer* restatement — the preference is the source's, never the write order's or the version's. **Implementation-time clarifications recorded** (so a later reader need not infer them): (a) the served-financials `Pick` and the three engine pickers share **one** implementation, `StatementSourcePreference` — rank (`kap-public` < `kap` < any other class) → highest `version` → row id, a **total** order, so two same-version sources can never be resolved by arrival order; (b) the preference is **whole-statement, never per line**, which is exactly what keeps the per-share figures honestly NULL instead of borrowing another filing's count; (c) the write side is untouched — the lane still withholds an overlapped fact and names it `SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED`. **The §23 re-pin, executed and recorded (no weakening):** TC-MDF-110's two `TC_MDF_110_b_*` cases are **unchanged** and still bind against the migrated store — the overlap question is asked of `source_ref != laneSource` (`KapPublicStatementTargets.GetForeignPeriodsAsync`), not of the uniqueness rule, so the withheld-fact behaviour holds exactly as v1.28/v1.29 pinned it (`SOURCE_OVERLAP_RETAINED` per covered key, `SOURCE_PERIOD_TYPE_DISAGREEMENT` for the calendar-period decode disagreement, the incumbent rows untouched, the same report's other periods written). The migration's behavioural delta is asserted *positively* in case (6) instead: two sources may now coexist for one key without a restatement. **Red evidence, stated honestly:** the environment this ticket was implemented in has **no Docker/Testcontainers** (`/usr/bin/docker` is a dangling WSL link, no `/var/run/docker.sock`, no local PostgreSQL), so the suite could not be executed locally — every case fails at container start (`Docker.DotNet` ping), which is **environment, not red**. The red/green count is therefore recorded from **CI** (ubuntu-latest has Docker) on the ticket's PR, and the red shape is predicted from the pre-migration schema rather than observed: with the source-less unique index the seeded mixed world **cannot be written at all** — cases (1)–(5) fail at `SeedMixedWorldAsync` with the store's own `23505` unique violation on `(instrument, period, statement type, version)`, which is the honest pre-ticket answer ("this world is unspeakable in the old schema"), and case (6), which drives the store API instead of raw seeding, fails on its assertion (**`Expected: 1, Actual: 0`** on `second.Inserted` — the second source's payload is swallowed as a `version 0` idempotent no-op, the exact silent `unchanged` v1.28 named as dishonest). The green direction is the same six cases passing on the migrated store. **Non-regression to re-run green and unrelaxed:** `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests` (the v1.29 sixteen, incl. the two re-pinned `TC_MDF_110_b_*`), `MetricsRecomputeIdempotencyTests`, `MarketSnapshotTests`, `MetricsEngineTests`, `ProfileMetricServingTests`, `StatementsAndEventsIngestionTests`, `DcfBaselineRegenerationTests`, `BackfillAndCoverageTests`, `MigrationsAndSeedTests`. Live criterion (orchestrator, post-merge): on the dev stack after a real public-site statements pass, `metrics-recompute`/`medians`/`market-snapshot` complete with current-period metrics for covered companies, every written row carrying the current `formula_version`, per-source coverage visible in `coverage_metadata`, market P/E honestly null, and the stock pages showing current-period financials — recorded verbatim on the ticket. **v1.32 2026-10-26 — §24's first CI run: the migration's three collateral collisions repaired, and TC-MDF-032 re-pinned (TKT-foundation-083; §14 change-propagation; user-authorized 2026-10-26 after run 40336652606).** The first PR run of §24 gave the lane its real red/green split: **13 failed / 636 passed / 649**. Key: 1|FY|2025-12-31|IS|0` from `FixtureSeeder.SeedStatementsAsync`. **Root cause, and why it is the migration's exposure rather than flakiness:** the seeder's two statement bookkeeping maps (the "already stored" set and the statement→id lookup) were keyed `instrument|periodType|periodEnd|statementType|version` — the store's *pre-migration* natural key. A world in which a second provenance holds one `(period, statement type, version)` — exactly what the source-in-key migration makes storable, and what any suite reaches by re-seeding after a `kap` ingest — collapses two distinct facts onto one dictionary key. **Repair (no assertion, golden or fixture value touched):** `FixtureSeeder.Key` gains `source_ref`, matching the store's shape, and both maps carry it. **Two further pre-existing cases encoded the old identity, both re-pinned rather than weakened:** (1) `SchemaConformanceTests.Required_uniqueness_is_enforced`'s `financial_statements` data row now names the frozen six columns (`instrument_id, source_ref, period_type, period_end_date, statement_type, version`) — the same "this identity is unique" claim, one column *stronger*; (2) **`TC-MDF-032` (RestatementMetricsTests, §10/TC list for 009)** — its "both versions remain stored" counts assumed one series per key, so with the KAP lane's re-ingest now its own provenance series beside the fixture world's the as-reported version is held once per provenance (Actual 6 vs Expected 3). The re-pin asserts the identical intent per series — 3 restated, 2 provenances × 3 as-reported, each series IS/BS/CF named — and selects the served IS rows through the pinned preference (`kap` ranks above the fixture provenance), then keeps **every** original golden verbatim: `pe` 12.5 as-reported-only → **10.0** restated (`15 / (75/50)`), `IsRested` on all 26 metric rows, restated NI **75** over as-reported NI **60**, restatement date **2026-06-20**, and a NULL restatement date on the as-reported row. The observable §24 numbers are unchanged from v1.31. **v1.38 2026-10-27 — §30 authored and implemented: cumulative-vs-period (YTD) column semantics and the honest fail-safe (TC-MDF-119), TKT-foundation-090's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture v1.5.64 §10.9.19 (the period-semantics subsection landed by the same ticket). **Section 30 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-119** (sub-cases (i)–(iv)) — the ID is the next free (the ledger stopped at TC-MDF-118). **Doubles only, zero live requests:** the inputs are the three bodies 088 already committed (`kap-public-report-1648240` MİGROS 2026-06, `kap-public-report-1554106` TÜPRAŞ 2025 Yıllık, `kap-public-report-asels-1561039` ASELSAN 2025 Yıllık) plus in-memory mutations of the interim (declared ranges stripped; `Periyot:2` → `Periyot:4`); **no test calls kap.org.tr**, and this lane issued none. **The determination, recorded before any fix and unchanged by it — the suspicion is unfounded on the interim path:** the MİGROS `1648240` body's main IS/CF column headers declare `Cari Dönem 01.01.2026 - 30.06.2026` (a six-month cumulative range), and in the income-statement (and OCI) tables the discrete quarter is a **separate** `Cari Dönem 3 Aylık 01.04.2026 - 30.06.2026` column that the decoder already names and skips as `SUB_PERIOD_COLUMN`; the **cash-flow table carries only the two declared-range columns** (current and prior), no quarterly sibling, so the CF span rests on its own declaration. The decoded `NI` is therefore the six-month cell `1.078.256`, not the three-month `−644.936`; the decoded `REV` is `241.298.039`, not the three-month `124.399.203`. The decoder decodes the column whose declared duration is the report's own; it never substitutes a three-month cell for a cumulative one at decode time (the store still carries no span, so the served label stays the store's `FY`/`Q` vocabulary — 057's surfaced gap). **The annual bodies are twelve-month columns** — TÜPRAŞ `1554106` CF header `01.01.2025 - 31.12.2025`, `49.464.734 − |19.118.339| = 30.346.395` thousand TL = ₺30,35bn (the recorded FY2025 FCF — matching, not contradicting, the live figure); ASELSAN `1561039` `48.901.969 − |38.168.467| = 10.733.502` — so the FY basis the DCF consumes is untouched by cumulative semantics, and 088's/089's annual goldens hold verbatim. **The one real defect found, and the fix:** a report that self-declares a period while **no** column declares a readable date range had undetermined semantics, and the pre-ticket decoder typed its instant (balance-sheet) rows `FY` silently — the shape that could put a half-year `debt`/`cash` into the DCF's annual basis. The decoder now raises one named, counted finding per report — **`PERIOD_SEMANTICS_UNDETERMINED`** (new code, joined to architecture §10.9.19's closed reason set) — and fails safe to **`Q`** for every statement row; the danger is closed for **both** the annual-declared and the interim-declared variant of the stripped body (an interim's `Q` must rest on evidence too). The defect's incidence is unmeasured — no captured report lacks declared ranges, so the case is a mutation — and in such a report the 2025-12-31 annual comparative balance sheet also types `Q` and drops out of the FY-only read (the safe direction, named). Flow tables whose columns cannot be attributed at all still invent nothing (`COLUMN_UNATTRIBUTED` drops) and the unattested balance sheet is still stored — honestly as an interim. **Additive provenance (decode-result only):** `KapPublicLine` gains `PeriodStartDate` (nullable) and `DurationMonths` (nullable), populated from the resolved column, so every decoded fact states the filing's own span **on the decode result**, available to the decode's own consumers and tests; instant balance-sheet lines carry `null`/`null`, and the span is not stored or served (`Group.ToFact()` drops it; the financials payload emits only `periodType`/`fiscalYear`/`periodEndDate`). **Implemented as four cases in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicReportDecoderTests.cs`, every one tagged `TC_MDF_119`:** (i) the interim column is the declared cumulative range, never a quarter (header + column spans + four skipped `SUB_PERIOD_COLUMN`s + the cumulative-vs-three-month cell arithmetic + per-line span); (ii) the annual columns are twelve months and their FCF is the year (both annual bodies); (iii) a cumulative interim is stored `Q` and the FY filter cannot reach it (six stored rows, scaled cells, empty `PeriodType == "FY"` selection); (iv) a period no column attests is named and fails safe to `Q`. **Red→green, honestly** (Docker/Testcontainers available, so the cases really executed): the verification found the decode *correct*, so (i)–(iii) are green by construction and recorded as such — **no red was manufactured** for a correct behaviour. (iv) carries the ticket's real red: forcing `semanticsUndetermined = false` (the pre-ticket behaviour) makes it fail at `Assert.DoesNotContain() Failure: Filter matched in collection`, the collection showing `StatementFact { PeriodType = FY, PeriodEndDate = 06/30/2026 … StatementType = BS }` beside the `12/31/2025` BS row — i.e. **the half-year balance sheet typed `FY`**, exactly the danger this ticket closes; **green** with the guard restored. **Green counts:** `KapPublicReportDecoderTests` **30/30** (26 pre-existing 106/107/108/117 cases + the four new), `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Non-regression re-run green and unrelaxed:** `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests`, `StatementVersioningTests`, `DcfBaselineEndpointTests`, `KapPublicRecomputeTests`, `RealSourceStatementsTests`, `StatementNormalizationTests`, `StockFundamentalsEndpointsTests`, `DcfBaselineRegenerationTests`, `RealSourcePricesTests`, `KapPublicSourceTests` — **88/88**. **The four suspect numbers, accounted for on the ticket, not here:** FY2025 ₺30,3bn is the body's own arithmetic (above); the TUPRS Q3-2025 and Q2-2026 figures stay **unattributed** — no TUPRS interim body is committed, so the cumulative-column mechanism is verified generically on committed interims, not on those filings; KCHOL FY2025 is an `FY`-typed annual row whose attribution needs a committed KCHOL body, leaving a mapping/label defect a live alternative to genuine magnitude. What is disposed of is YTD-vs-quarter column confusion in the decoder; the repair route for any genuinely wrong stored interim row is an explicit **cursor reset + re-walk** (FR-MDF-015 historical immutability stands; never a silent in-place rewrite). the **transport failure** is a real connection that never delivers (WireMock answers after 30 s against a 150 ms per-item bound), so no body ever reaches the lane — never a fabricated failure object, never a shape verdict. **Implemented as `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicStatementsIngestTests.cs` — four cases, every one tagged `TC_MDF_121`, driven through the production walker and the production `statements` job over the production paced client:** (i) three transport quarantines → each replayed once on a later pass (the interim recovers and its facts are written, the other two re-quarantine terminally with `"bodyReceived": false` evidence retained), the run line names `quarantine-replays 3 (recovered 1, still-quarantined 2)`, and a third pass over a **now-healthy** source issues zero report requests — the tombstone is persisted, not remembered in-process; (ii) a shape verdict (`UNPARSEABLE_PAYLOAD`) is terminal and fetched exactly once for its lifetime while the transport failure beside it replays once; (iii) a non-success status **carrying a body** is terminal while the transport failure beside it replays — the class is drawn from what the read carried, never from the status word; (iv) the replay is admitted by the pass's **detail** ceiling: a one-detail pass spends exactly one replay, reports `WALK_DETAIL_CAP`, journals no fetch of the recoverable interim and leaves two pending, and a later pass drains the backlog. **The state, recorded so a later reader need not infer it:** no migration — `kap-public-statements-cursor`'s `coverage_metadata.notes` JSON gains two **additive** arrays, `quarantined` (backlog owing its one replay) and `retried` (the once-only tombstone), written always even when empty, with an absent array read as empty so a pre-092 row keeps its position and is never reinterpreted as "all retried"; `CloseWindow` carries both markers through every window close. **Red→green, executed (Docker/Testcontainers available):** with the replay loop disabled **4 failed / 0 passed** — (i) at `Expected: 1, Actual: 0` on the recovered report's request (the live runs' cursor-locked world), (ii)/(iii) on the missing `quarantine-replays …` run line, (iv) at `Expected: "WALK_DETAIL_CAP", Actual: null` — and **4/4 green** with it. **v1.45 2026-10-28 — §33 authored and implemented: the statements-view label map (TC-MDF-122), TKT-foundation-093's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.69** §10.9.19 (the row-scope subsection landed by the same ticket, subsuming the planner's dated 2026-10-28 design note) and `02` §6.1's chart extension. **Section 33 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-122** (sub-cases (a)–(i)) — the ID is the next free (the ledger stopped at TC-MDF-121). **Doubles only, zero live requests:** the capture set grows by one, **`kap-public-report-1565996`** (THYAO 2025 Yıllık, public index `1565996`, filed 04.03.2026 18:55:17, `1.000.000 TL`, `Finansal Tablo Niteliği: Konsolide`), committed as a **bounded-trim** double as this lane's first step from the already-fetched body — no new live request was made or needed for it; every negative case is an in-memory mutation of a committed body (the `DÖNEN VARLIKLAR` anchor renamed, `Konsolide`→`Solo`/`Bireysel`, the `Finansal Tablo Niteliği` field renamed). **The diagnosis, recorded before any map entry was written (the 088 precedent, and it corrects the ticket's premise):** the four bodies then committed already decoded every v0 row family **as valued `OTHER_*` passthroughs** — the gap was the canonical mapping, never the data — while a plain 1:1 `(statement, label)` map would have re-created the §28 failure on the front door, because the balance sheet repeats `Ticari Alacaklar`, `Ticari Borçlar`, `Kiralama İşlemlerinden Borçlar`, `Ertelenmiş Gelirler (…)` across its current/non-current blocks and the income statement repeats `Ana Ortaklık Payları` across its profit-or-loss/OCI parts. **The mechanism, landed as the planner ruling of 2026-10-28 (§6.1 / §10.9.19, now subsumed):** the map is carried as **two projections** — `KapPublicReportDecoder.LabelMap` (unqualified, still a function on `(statement, label)`, so TC-MDF-107 (i)/117 (f) hold) and `ScopedLabelMap` (`KapPublicLabelRule.Scope` qualified, a function on `(statement, label, scope)` over the closed `KapPublicScopes` vocabulary — `CURRENT`/`NONCURRENT`/`PL`/`OCI` — plus the four existing CF blocks) — with the row's scope resolved **before** label mapping exactly as `CashFlowSectionFor` already resolved the CF section: the balance sheet's block from the capture's own `DÖNEN VARLIKLAR`/`DURAN VARLIKLAR`/`KISA VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER`/`UZUN VADELİ YÜKÜMLÜLÜKLER` anchors (the equity block and every row before the first anchor resolve to `null`), the income statement's part from its own `Kar veya Zararda Yeniden Sınıflandırılmayacaklar` anchor (a standalone `Diğer Kapsamlı Gelir Tablosu` starts in OCI). A **qualifier-free** rule matches in every scope, which is what makes the design additive and keeps every pre-093 entry byte-identical; a row whose scope is `null` while the map carries a **scope-qualified** entry for its caption matches nothing qualified, keeps its section-scoped `OTHER_*` passthrough, and is a named, counted **`BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`** finding — emitted **once per row**, not per cell — never a guessed block. **The chart extension (41 new codes, `StatementMapper.CanonicalCodes` 23 → 64 — corrected 2026-10-28, PR #155 review round 1 DR-1, user-arbitrated docs amendment: the landed map adds 42 entries of which the 42nd wires pre-existing `MINORITY_INT`, so 41 codes are new; the originally stated "42 new / 65" double-counted the wiring entry; `02` §6.1 rewritten to name them):** balance sheet `INVENTORY`, `PPE_NET`, `ROU_ASSET`, `INTANGIBLES_NET`, `GOODWILL`, `PAID_IN_CAPITAL`, `RETAINED_EARNINGS`, `EQUITY_PARENT`, `TOTAL_ASSETS`, `TOTAL_LIAB`, `CUR_ASSETS_OTHER`, `CUR_LIAB_OTHER`, `NONCUR_ASSETS_OTHER`, `NONCUR_LIAB_OTHER` plus the scope-qualified `ST_TRADE_RECV`/`LT_TRADE_RECV`, `ST_TRADE_PAY`/`LT_TRADE_PAY`, `ST_LEASE_LIAB`/`LT_LEASE_LIAB`, `ST_DEFERRED_REV`/`LT_DEFERRED_REV`; income statement `RND`, `OP_INCOME_OTHER`, `OP_EXPENSE_OTHER`, `INVEST_INCOME`, `INVEST_EXPENSE`, `FIN_INCOME`, `NET_MONETARY_GL`, `NI_PARENT`; cash flow `IMPAIRMENT`, `PROVISIONS`, `MONETARY_ADJ`, `ASSET_SALE_PROCEEDS`, `ACQUISITION_CASH`, `EQUITY_ISSUE_CASH`, `BUYBACK`, `DIVIDENDS_PAID`, `INTEREST_PAID`, `DEBT_ISSUED`, `DEBT_REPAID`; and `MINORITY_INT` (already in §6.1) is now **wired** to its KAP spelling `Kontrol Gücü Olmayan Paylar`. **The §6.1 choice recorded so a later reader need not infer it:** the **non-current** repeats of trade receivables and trade payables get their **own** codes (`LT_TRADE_RECV`/`LT_TRADE_PAY`) rather than being folded into the `NONCUR_ASSETS_OTHER`/`NONCUR_LIAB_OTHER` families — a caption two blocks carry is two lines of the same kind, and routing the non-current occurrence to an "other" bucket would have produced a figure the view could not name; every other repeated family is separated per block on the same principle. **The filing flag:** `KapPublicReport.Consolidated` derives from `Header.ConsolidationType` (the header-table field `Finansal Tablo Niteliği`) via `KapPublicReportDecoder.ConsolidatedFor` — `Konsolide`→`true`, `Solo`→`false`, absent or any other value→`null` (the declared string is retained verbatim either way) — never inferred from the `<h1>` company heading and never from the presence of minority interests. **Red→green, measured (Docker/Testcontainers available; the red was re-run, not remembered):** with the 41 new map entries stripped from `Rules`/`ScopedRules` on the pre-ticket map (the decoder's scope machinery, the models and the assertions all kept — so the red is the *map's* absence, never a compile or environment failure), the `TC_MDF_122` filter reads **7 failed / 2 passed** — (a) at `Assert.NotEmpty() Failure: Collection was empty` on `ScopedLabelMap` (line 1489, the first assertion of the projection check), (b) `KeyNotFoundException: 'ST_TRADE_RECV'`, (c) `KeyNotFoundException: 'ST_TRADE_RECV'`, (d) `Assert.Single() Failure: The collection did not contain any matching items` for `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` (the finding exists only because a scope-qualified entry exists for the caption — with the map empty there is nothing to fail safe *from*, which is exactly why the fail-safe half cannot be proven by deletion alone), (f) `KeyNotFoundException: 'NI_PARENT'`, (g) `KeyNotFoundException: 'IMPAIRMENT'`, (i) `KeyNotFoundException: 'ST_LEASE_LIAB'`; (e) and (h) pass **by construction in this red** — the 088/090/092 goldens are what the unmodified map already produces (the additive design's own claim), and (h) rests on the model-side flag decode, which this red run deliberately kept (it is the ticket's other half, not a map entry, so deleting map entries cannot falsify it; its own falsification is the pre-ticket absence of `KapPublicReport.Consolidated`, a compile-time seam) — both stated rather than manufactured as red. **39/39 green** on the reviewed head for `KapPublicReportDecoderTests` (the 30 pre-existing 106/107/108/117/119 cases + the nine new). **The v1.45 re-pin round (USER-APPROVED 2026-10-28: a minimal re-pin of assertions unavoidably invalidated by the expansion, every TC id kept, recorded here, in `01` v1.5.69 and on the ticket).** Six pinned values moved because the map grew, and **not one of them was weakened or re-scoped**: (1) `TC_MDF_107 (iii)`'s `47.167.326` assertion moved from `OTHER_BS_Ticari_Alacaklar` to `ST_TRADE_RECV` — the same captured value under the code the expansion assigns it — and the case's passthrough proof moved to the still-unmapped `OTHER_BS_İlişkili_Taraflardan_Ticari_Alacaklar` (21.831.106), so the "unmapped label keeps a labelled passthrough" intent is unchanged and is asserted on a label the map genuinely does not map; disagreement is null); the envelope gains `filing` (the latest served period's `periodEndDate`/`sourceRef`/`consolidated`/`tms29`/`kapSource`), `evHeader` (share price, market cap, gross debt, cash (the stored `CASH` line — no short-term-investments code exists in the chart), NCI — each its own `{value, asOf}` fact, **display-only**, the DCF's own inputs untouched) and `layoutNotice` (for `accounting_profile ∈ {bank, insurance}`: `applies: true`, `state: "not-applicable"`, an explicit message, and **no industrial row set served** — each period's `lines`/`otherLines` keep the IS/BS/CF shape with every map empty and `derived` is empty, while the compat five, `span` and `consolidated` still serve; `reit`/`industrial` keep the natural row set and a null notice). **The [C] rows, honest-null on any missing operand** (the `Line()`/`Sum()` discipline): `revenueGrowthYoy` (prior **same period type** one year earlier, absent → null; served as a fraction, i.e. `REV/prior − 1`), `grossMargin`, `operatingMargin`, `ebitdaMargin`, `effectiveTaxRate`, `netMarginParent`, `netWorkingCapital` (`CUR_ASSETS − CUR_LIAB`), `grossDebt` (**`ST_DEBT + LT_DEBT` exactly — DR-2's fence consumed as stored, never widened to the lease codes 093 materialized beside it**), `netDebt`, `tangibleBookValue` (`TOTAL_EQUITY − GOODWILL − INTANGIBLES_NET`), `bvps`/`tbvPerShare`/`fcfPerShare` (the pinned precedence: the statement's `SHARES_DILUTED` first, 086's price-derived count second, otherwise **null with `state: "no-data"`** — never a paid-in-capital-derived guess, the user's exclusion #4, never zero). **v1.48 2026-10-28 — §36 authored and implemented: decoder fail-safe hardening (TC-MDF-125), TKT-foundation-098's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.74** §10.9.19 (the fail-safe-hardening subsection landed by the same ticket) and `02` §6.1 (the closed reason set now carries `PART_UNDETERMINED`). **Section 36 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-125** (sub-cases (a)–(g)) — the ID is the next free above the wave's reservations (§34 = TC-MDF-123/094, §35 = TC-MDF-124/096). **Doubles only, zero live requests:** every load-bearing case is an in-memory mutation of a committed body — the THYAO liabilities anchors renamed, the MİGROS OCI heading suffixed / replaced by a bare `Diğer Gelir Tablosu`, the MİGROS and ASELSAN-annual OCI anchors re-worded, a scoped caption's raw `title` attributes blanked — and the non-regression case decodes the five committed bodies as committed. **No map entry, no canonical code, no capture and no dependency was added:** the map is exactly the reviewed 093 map and `StatementMapper.CanonicalCodes` stays 64. **The four fixes, recorded as landed.** (1) **DR-4:** `BalanceSheetBlockFor` now returns `null` on any label beginning `TOPLAM` — every balance-sheet total row closes the block state, so the side that follows must re-declare its block; `ARA TOPLAM` subtotals are deliberately **not** boundary rows (they sit inside a block, and making them boundaries would starve the captured non-current blocks of the rows between them). The boundary finding is the **existing** `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` (the reviewer's own alternative), **not** a new `BLOCK_BOUNDARY_UNDECLARED`: the outcome is identical — a scoped caption whose block could not be determined — and a second reason for the same condition would split one ledger fact in two. (2) **DR-5 (i):** a new `IncomeScopeForHeading(heading)` reads the starting part from the heading's **content** (`Contains("Kar veya Zarar")` → `PL`, else `Contains("Kapsamlı Gelir")` → `OCI`, else `null` = undetermined), replacing the exact-equality check; the new closed reason **`PART_UNDETERMINED`** (`KapPublicReportModels.cs`, `02` §6.1) names an undetermined part. (3) **DR-5 (ii):** the stale-part branch — a part-qualified caption the statement already holds with a **different value** while no anchor declares the other part — now falls back to its section-scoped passthrough and is named `PART_UNDETERMINED`, instead of colliding and quarantining the whole row; the conflict quarantine itself is untouched (two genuinely mapped unequal duplicates still conflict). (4) **DR-8/DR-9:** the scope question is computed **once per row** (`scope is null && HasScopedRule(...) && valueCells.Any(IsValued)`) and emitted after the cell loop, once per row, and `HasScopedRule` now reads a precomputed `ScopedLabelsByStatement` set (`IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlySet<string>>`) instead of scanning the rule keys per row. **DR-6/DR-7 delivered in the same file:** `TC_MDF_122 (h)` additionally asserts the retained-verbatim declaration (`Assert.Equal("Bireysel", other.Header.ConsolidationType)` beside the existing `Assert.Null(other.Consolidated)`), and `TC_MDF_122 (a)` runs the provenance/spelling/registry loop over the **unqualified** projection too — both pure strengthenings, no assertion removed or weakened. **Red→green, measured (Docker/Testcontainers available; the red was re-run, not remembered):** with only `KapPublicReportDecoder.cs` reverted to the pre-ticket file (the models kept, so the new reason compiles and the red is behaviour, never a compile or environment failure), the `FullyQualifiedName~TC_MDF_125` filter reads **5 failed / 2 passed** — (a) `Expected: 0, Actual: 4` on `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` (the THYAO wrong-block codes quarantining); (c) and (e) `Expected: 0, Actual: 2` conflicts with `conflict=NI_PARENT` (the quarantined income statement); (d) the missing `PART_UNDETERMINED` finding plus the collision; (f) `Assert.Single()` finding nothing — **the DR-8 gap itself**; — while (b) passes **by construction on this red**, stated rather than hidden: this committed body's OCI table also declares the anchor before the repeated caption, so on that mutation the anchor attests the part even under the old exact-equality heading check; (c) is its red counterpart and (b) pins the invariant. (g) passes on both heads because it pins the unchanged goldens. **7/7 green** with the fix. **Byte-identical non-regression, proved not inferred:** a full decode dump (every line, every finding with its detail, every statement line and conflicting code) of all five bodies on the pre-ticket head and on the patched head `diff`s **empty** — THYAO 497 lines / 40 disambiguated, TUPRS 346 / 18, ASELSAN 383 / 34, MİGROS 314 / 16, ASELS 116 / 0, **zero `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED` and zero `PART_UNDETERMINED` on all five**. **Suites:** `KapPublicReportDecoderTests` **46/46** (the 39 pre-existing 106/107/108/117/119/122 cases + the seven new), `KapPublicStatementsIngestTests | RealSourceStatementsTests | StatementNormalizationTests | StatementVersioningTests` **31/31**; `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Live criteria:** none specific to this ticket (the hardening's incidence is unmeasured on captured reports because no committed body is malformed); the wave's paced `Source = kap-public` walk criteria sequenced behind 094/096 stand unchanged. New sub-case **(h)** pins the corrected behaviour — the ASELSAN 2025 combined body with its profit-or-loss `Kontrol Gücü Olmayan Paylar` row re-labelled `Ana Ortaklık Payları` yields two unequal same-part occurrences, so `NI_PARENT` = −31.790 (first retained), `ConflictingItemCodes` = `[NI_PARENT]`, two `DUPLICATE_LABEL_CONFLICT` findings and **zero** `PART_UNDETERMINED`. (h) is red on the pre-fix head (`2` `PART_UNDETERMINED`, `ConflictingItemCodes` null, zero conflicts — measured) and green with the fix; `TC_MDF_125` reads **8/8** and `KapPublicReportDecoderTests` **47/47** (the 39 pre-existing cases + the eight `TC_MDF_125` cases), and the five-body full decode dump `diff`s **empty** against the review head (THYAO 497 / 40, TUPRS 346 / 18, ASELSAN 383 / 34, MİGROS 314 / 16, ASELS 116 / 0, zero conflicts, zero `BLOCK_UNDETERMINED`/`PART_UNDETERMINED`). Two wording corrections ride along, no behaviour change: (f)'s description now states that the two blanked decoded cells lose their **display text as well as** the raw `title` attribute (the mutation truly leaves only skipped-column values — DR-02 was a false positive on the helper, verified by blanking all four valued cells and watching the finding disappear), and the `TOPLAM` rule records that **any** `TOPLAM`-prefixed row closes the block, not only a closing one (DR-03). **v1.50 2026-10-29 — §37 authored and implemented: Turkish dotless-ı identity resolution (TC-MDF-126), TKT-foundation-099's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.75** §10.9.19 (the dotless-ı subsection landed by the same ticket, immediately after the 091 comma-token subsection). **Section 37 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-126** (sub-cases (a), (a2), (b)–(f)) — the ID is the next free above the wave's reservations (§34 = TC-MDF-123/094, §35 = TC-MDF-124/096, §36 = TC-MDF-125/098). **Doubles only, zero live CI requests:** the four positive cases run against the four **captured** identity answers committed by the ticket's orchestrator-owned paced pass (`kap-public-identity-BIMAS`, `-ENKAI`, `-SISE`, `-ISCTR`; provenance `Captured`, bodies verbatim) — the repository's first *captured* dotless answers, where 091's two comma-coded doubles remain honestly `Constructed` — and every negative case is an in-memory mutation of the committed BIMAS body or a derived answer in its shape. **The gap:** after 091's comma-token rule mapped 26 of 30 XU030 companies, the live pass (run 362, 2026-10-28) left BIMAS, ENKAI, ISCTR and SISE `COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED` because their `cmpOrFundCode` carries the Turkish dotless `ı` (U+0131) — `'bımas'`, `'enkaı'`, `'sıse'`, `'ısatr,ısbtr,ısctr,ıskur,tıb'` — and `OrdinalIgnoreCase` folds ASCII case but not `ı`↔`i`. **The rule:** both sides of the token comparison are folded (`ı`→`i`, `İ`→`i`) **at comparison time only**, in `CarriesToken` and nowhere else; the stored mapping, the answer body, the provenance string and our own symbol keep their verbatim spelling. **The 091 semantics restated because the fold touches them, and pinned by the case:** comma splitting/trimming, the empty-token rule and the no-substring rule stand; the fold is not a transliteration rule (a near-miss outside its two letters stays unresolved); and **several distinct company candidates sharing the folded token are still `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`** — named, counted, nothing stored, never a pick — the fold widens the candidate set and is explicitly not a tie-break. **Implemented as seven cases in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicIdentityTests.cs`, every one tagged `TC_MDF_126`:** (a) BIMAS resolves through `'bımas'`; (a2) ENKAI (`'enkaı'`) and ISCTR (the five-token dotless list) resolve in one pass — the fold composing with the comma-token rule; (b) SISE resolves through `'sıse'`; (c) the fold is comparison-only — the committed 386-byte body is unmodified (U+0131 present, ASCII `'bimas'` absent), the stored row carries the source's oid and the lane/keyword provenance, the instrument keeps its ASCII symbol; (d) a dotless multi-candidate answer (one `'bımas'`, one ASCII `'bimas'`, distinct oids) is `COMPANY_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS` on the first **and** the second pass, both codes named, nothing stored; (e) the fold is exactly `ı`/`İ`→`i` — `'bımasxyz'` is no match (never a substring rule), `'bımaş'` stays unresolved (not an accent rule), `'bİmas'` matches (the `İ` half, which no capture exercises); (f) every 080/091 golden holds — ASELS' committed single-token capture, the constructed comma-coded ISCTR answer, the constructed GARAN ambiguity. **Red→green, measured (Docker/Testcontainers available, so the cases really executed):** with only `KapPublicCompanyIdentityResolver.cs` reverted to `origin/dev` the `TC_MDF_126` filter reads **6 failed / 1 passed** — (a), (a2), (b), (c) on `Resolved` (`Expected: 1/2, Actual: 0` — the live run's own unresolved state), (d) `Expected: 0, Actual: 1` on `Resolved` (pre-fold only the ASCII candidate is visible and is *picked* — the behaviour the fold must not create), (e) `Expected: 1, Actual: 0` on the `İ` half — while (f) passes by construction because it pins the unchanged goldens, recorded as such rather than manufactured red; **7/7 green** with the fold. **Non-regression 72/72 green and unrelaxed:** `KapPublicIdentityTests | KapPublicIdentityJobTests | KapPublicSourceTests | KapPublicStatementsIngestTests | RealSourcePricesTests` (the last including TC-MDF-063's catalog `Names`/`Build` contract, whose four additions are consistent and honestly labelled as captures); `dotnet build` 0 warnings. **Version note (last-lander rebase):** §37 / v1.50 are the ticket's soft reservation; 096 (§35, v1.49) and 100/101 land independently, so this entry re-pins at merge if the wave's numbers move. **Live criterion (orchestrator, post-merge, paced, never CI):** one identity pass resolving all four symbols (or the residue honestly named if any root cause differs — none does per the captures), then their statements walk landing data. Applied by the ticket's implementation lane; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.51 2026-10-29 — §38 authored and implemented: the durable KAP public-site access fix (TC-MDF-127), TKT-foundation-100's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.77** §10.9.19 (the access-posture amendment landed by the same ticket, subsuming the planner's dated 2026-10-28/29 fingerprint/quota findings and the option-(c) ruling). **Section 38 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-127** (sub-cases (a)–(e)) — the ID is the next free above the wave's reservations (§34 = TC-MDF-123/094, §35 = TC-MDF-124/096, §36 = TC-MDF-125/098, §37 = TC-MDF-126/099). **Doubles only, zero live requests:** the whole case runs over a `RecordingHandler` (no socket, no WireMock server needed) and a `FakeTimeProvider` with an **injectable quota-delay delegate** that records each demanded wait instead of sleeping, so the bounded backoff is measured without spending wall-clock time; the successful detail payload is the committed `kap-public-report-1643141` body; the shipped `src/Api/appsettings.json` is read from disk for the config-equivalence pin. **The adapter half, recorded as landed:** `KapPublicHttpHandlerFactory.Create` builds the lane's `SocketsHttpHandler` (`PooledConnectionLifetime` 10 min, no cookies) and, when `TlsShapeEnabled` is on, attaches `CreateSslOptions` — the TLS floor read from `TlsMinimumVersion` (**`Tls12` → `Tls12 | Tls13`**; `SslProtocols.Tls`/`Tls11` are SYSLIB0039-obsolete and the repo builds `TreatWarningsAsErrors`, so the parse collapses to the modern set rather than offering a dead protocol), ALPN from `TlsAlpnProtocols` (`h2,http/1.1`), and a browser-ordered `CipherSuitesPolicy` from `TlsCipherSuites` (an 11-suite list) guarded by `!OperatingSystem.IsWindows()` and a `PlatformNotSupportedException` catch — Windows cannot order a client's suites, so the policy stays unset there and the platform default stands (a recorded platform fact, asserted as such, never faked). The DI registration constructs the keyed `HttpClient` over that handler with `HttpVersion.Version20` / `RequestVersionOrLower`, so the shape the factory builds **is** the shape the lane uses. **The quota half, recorded as landed:** `KapPublicHttpSourceClient` treats a `429` as a first-class outcome — it computes the required wait (`RequiredQuotaWait`: the source's `Retry-After` in delta-seconds **or** HTTP-date form honored **verbatim**, otherwise `QuotaBackoffMs`, default 8,000 ms), and if that wait would meet or exceed the request's per-item bound it throws `KapPublicQuotaException` with `Deferred = true` and reason **`WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF`** — **no sleep, no re-issue** — letting the walk truncate honestly at its existing budget/ceiling seam and resume next pass; otherwise it sleeps the wait and re-issues **once** (`MaxQuotaAttempts = 2`), and a source still rate-limited after that re-issue throws `KapPublicQuotaException(Deferred = false, QUOTA_RATE_LIMITED)`. Two new reason codes land in `KapPublicReasonCodes` (`WalkQuotaBackoff`, `QuotaRateLimited`) and `KapPublicFetchResult.IsQuotaDeferred` carries the deferral to the walker, which treats it as **truncation** (replay loop, list and detail loops), never as a completed/skip. **The pacing half:** `KapPublicRequestPacer` gains a `KapPublicRequestKind` (`General`/`Export`) and `PaceAsync(kind, ct)`, and `KapPublicSourceOptions.DelayMsFor(kind)` returns the **export floor** (`ExportRequestDelayMs`, default **12,000 ms**, the relay's verified value) for export/detail fetches and the unchanged general pace (`3,000 ms`) for list/identity — with the floor disabled whenever the general pace is `<= 0` (the L2 harness binding, so a test that zeroes pacing zeroes both). **The ops half:** the Python TLS relay is committed under `deploy/kap-relay/` (`kap-tls-relay.py` + its README) with the when-to-use rule and the `Ingestion:KapPublic:BaseUrl` seam it rides, and `deploy/README.md` points to it — documented ops posture, not a hidden dev hack; the live dev override stays an orchestrator step. **Red→green, measured (the red re-run, not remembered):** with env-var gates temporarily injected to stand the three behaviours down (`TLS_SHAPED` off, `QUOTA_AWARE` off, `EXPORT_FLOOR` off) the `TC_MDF_127` filter reads **5 failed / 0 passed**, every one for the right reason — (a) on the missing `KapPublicQuotaException`/journal count, (b) on the missing verbatim `Retry-After` wait, (c) on the missing `WALK_QUOTA_BACKOFF` deferral, (d) on the missing export floor gap, (e) on the unshaped handler's TLS opinions — and **5/5 green** with the behaviour restored (the gates removed; the working tree carries no instrumentation). Non-regression **71/71 green and unrelaxed:** `KapPublicSourceTests | KapPublicStatementsIngestTests | KapPublicIdentityTests | KapPublicIdentityJobTests`; `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Recorded, not hidden — a scope note for the reviewers:** the ticket's declared `scope:` lists the client, pacer, options, models, DI, config, `deploy/`, the test file and the two docs; the deferral's **seam** necessarily touches `KapPublicSourceReader.cs`, `KapPublicIdentityReader.cs` (both add a `KapPublicQuotaException` catch mapping to `IsQuotaDeferred`) and `KapPublicStatementsWalk.cs` (the three truncation points), which are not on that list — no resolver (`KapPublicCompanyIdentityResolver.cs`, 099's merged d591414), no decoder, no store and no serving file was touched, and the changes are the minimum the ruled truncation requires; the omission is a planner scope gap routed to the orchestrator for amendment or acceptance, exactly as 091's UA pin and 094's persist half were. **Live criterion (orchestrator, post-merge, paced, never CI, relay disabled for the probe):** sustained export fetches through the adapter's own fingerprint without the transport-failure burst; a 429 encountered is named, backed off and resumed — counts verbatim. Applied by the ticket's implementation lane; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

FR-SCR-017 owns the later flag flip). **The gating extension:** the industrial-shaped additions join the profile gate (**26** gated codes), and the gate's definition is now **public** (`CanonicalMetrics.IsProfileGated`/`IsFinancialProfile`) so `SectorMedianEngine` excludes financial peers from a gated code's median on **one** definition and counts them in `excluded_count` (UXR-RES-024/025) — a bank's `ev_ebit` row is null by profile, so including it as an "assessed" peer would disclose nothing. **The median set:** `sector_metric_medians` covers **32 codes** (the five valuation-family codes the vs-sector strip always carried plus the panel's codes), i.e. 3 fixture sectors × 32 = 96 rows on the L2 world. **The company median is honest about its depth:** the endpoint computes the standard `MetricMath.Median` of the instrument's non-null stored values in the 5Y window and **withholds it below twelve observations**, always disclosing `windowYears` 5, `observations` and the actual `depthYears` — the user's own "5Y is marginal" caveat honoured rather than papered over (FR-MDF-014). **The per-share basis marker is computed per family** (`epsAdjusted` for the EPS/DPS families, `panelAdjusted` for `bvps_cagr_*`/`fcf_ps_cagr_*`), so an instrument whose only adjusted series belongs to a panel family never re-stamps a v1 EPS/DPS row — the v1 rows stay byte-identical (`02` §6.2). **The four re-pins the ticket's scope widening covers (USER-APPROVED 2026-10-28, implementation lane; recorded here and on the ticket):** four test files pin **fixture-contract constants bound to the old catalog size** — `MetricsRecomputeIdempotencyTests.cs`/`KapPublicRecomputeTests.cs` (`MetricRows = 16 * 26` → `16 * 43`, `MedianRows = 3 * 5` → `3 * 32`), `ScreenerMetricVisibilityTests.cs` (the visible catalog count 17 → 31 and 18 → 32), and `RegimeBreakTests.cs` (its growth-code loop is scoped to the computable v1 families, with the panel's new CAGR families asserted honestly NULL on the L2 world). Constants, loops and their doc comments only — no assertion added, removed or weakened, no product behaviour changed; the omissions from the ticket's original `scope:` list were a planning defect, resolved by the user rather than improvised. **Red→green, measured (Docker/Testcontainers available; the red was re-run, not remembered):** with `StockKeyMetricsEndpoints.cs` removed and `Program.cs`/`MetricsEngine.cs` reverted to the pre-ticket files (the code catalog and formulas kept, so the red is behaviour, never a compile or environment failure), the `TC_MDF_124` filter reads **8 failed / 0 passed** — (a) at `Assert.NotNull()` on the first new code's stored value, (e) at `Expected: 0.06, Actual: null`, the six endpoint cases at `Expected: OK, Actual: NotFound`, and (h) on an empty response body; **8/8 green** with the endpoint, the engine and the DI wiring. **Targeted suite green and unrelaxed, 78/78:** `StockKeyMetricsEndpointsTests | MetricsEngineTests | ProfileMetricServingTests | MetricsRecomputeIdempotencyTests | KapPublicRecomputeTests | RestatementMetricsTests | Screener* | RegimeBreakTests | StockOverviewAndValuationEndpointsTests | StockFundamentalsEndpointsTests`; `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Recorded, not delivered here:** the new codes are `is_screenable = false`, so the screener's criteria/UI surface is unchanged (a builder toggle later, FR-SCR-017); the panel's rendering is TKT-foundation-097's; the served statements surface is 094/095's — none of them touched by this lane. **v1.54 2026-10-29 — §35 amended in TKT-foundation-096's review round 1 (DR-4/DR-8): the per-family basis marker becomes a discriminating case, and two plan-text corrections.** **(DR-4)** the per-family basis marker (`02` §6.2: `epsAdjusted` for the v1 EPS/DPS families, `panelAdjusted` for `bvps_cagr_*`/`fcf_ps_cagr_*`) was claimed by (b cont.) but not proven there: (b cont.)'s only basis assertion is `isAdjusted == false` on `ebit_cagr_3y`, and ALFA carries no corporate actions — that assertion would hold identically with the two family flags collapsed into one. (b cont.) now has a second case, **(b cont., basis marker)** — `TC_MDF_124_the_basis_marker_is_per_family`, the ninth shipped case — which injects a panel-only adjusted series: a 2022-06-01 1:5 split on **ZETA** plus a FY2020–FY2024 equity/share-count history the world does not store, so `bvps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` and `fcf_ps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` come out `is_adjusted` **true** (the BVPS CAGRs with values — the marker is not a blanket swallow) while `eps_cagr_3y`/`_5y` and `div_cagr_3y`/`_5y` stay **false**. The injected lines and the action are removed in a `finally` (the DR-1 falsifiability pattern). (ZETA is the discriminating instrument because it has **no** dividend rows and no EPS year before FY2024, so the single action reaches the BVPS series alone — the fixture's only other adjusted instrument, EPSL, is adjusted on both families by construction.) Green measured locally: `TC_MDF_124` **9/9** (narrow filter, Testcontainers/Docker). **(DR-8)** two plan-text errors, no code, no assertion: the (b cont.) injection list named `PRETAX_INC` among the injected lines (it is not injected — 250 is the fixture's own FY line, and `NET_MONETARY_GL` alone makes `monetary_gain_pretax` computable), and (d) called the 32 median codes "the panel's code set" (the panel is **31**; the median set is the panel ∪ `ev_fcf`, the fifth standard valuation code the vs.-sector strip already carried — `02` §6.3's wording, now echoed here). The case-lettering vocabulary grows to nine shipped cases; architecture `01` v1.5.78's TC-MDF-124 record is amended in place to match (nine cases, (b cont., basis marker) described). **Still open by design, routed upstream:** DR-2 (the 12-observation floor is a session count, not a depth) and DR-3 (group-basis `roe`/`eps_cagr_*` where the user spec asks for Parent) are architect/user decisions and are untouched here.

**v1.56 2026-10-29 — §39 authored and implemented: the refused-payload observability rule and the bare-currency presentation declaration (TC-MDF-128), TKT-foundation-101's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.80** §10.9.19 (the observability rule and the bare-currency reading landed by the same ticket) and 02 §3.1 (the mandatory declaration, kept mandatory). **Section 39 is new and holds one case, TC-MDF-128** (sub-cases (a)–(c)) — the ID was this wave's reservation for this ticket, and the ticket's §39 pointer is now real rather than a reservation. **The diagnosis, recorded before any code was changed (and it corrects the ticket's hypothesis):** the body the orchestrator's 2026-10-29 paced pass produced (`/tmp/opencode/captures/kap-export-GUBRF-1605802.json`) is **not GUBRF** — it carries `ASTOR ENERJİ A.Ş.`'s heading and disclosure index `1605802` — and it is the **ordinary excel-HTML shape** (`financial-table`, `taxonomy-field-title`) the decoder has always read (BS 113 / IS 66 / CF 78 decoded lines, zero conflicts, on the untrimmed body). The refusal was therefore never a layout divergence: it was one field, `Sunum Para Birimi: TL` — a currency and **no multiplier** — which `PresentationUnitPattern` (`^(?<number>[\d.,]+)\s*(?<unit>\S+)$`) cannot match, so `ParsePresentationUnit` returned `(null, null)`, the store's mandatory-declaration clause fired and refused the whole filing as `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` with a `null` evidence payload. **The ruled outcome: case (a), the decoder gap fix** — a declaration naming a currency and no multiplier says `units`, and inferring a multiplier would be the guess 02 §3.1 forbids; the new branch reads the bare form through a shared `CurrencyFor` helper so the two halves of `ParsePresentationUnit` cannot drift, and every multiplier-bearing declaration keeps its existing reading (additive by construction, which is why no committed body's decode moves). **No new closed reason was needed** (`QuarantineReason.cs` is untouched): case (b) was the alternative, not the finding. **The observability fix, recorded as landed:** `StatementsJob`'s refusal path now builds a **bounded** evidence document (`RefusalEvidence`: symbol, disclosure index, source ref, reason code, the refusal's own detail, the filing's identity block verbatim — `presentationUnit`/`unitScale`/`currency`/`companyName`/`submittedAt`/`periodType`/`consolidationType` — the decode's statement/line/finding counts, and the first 12 findings with excerpts clipped to 240 characters; a few kilobytes against a ~1.3 MB body) and logs the reason's **detail**, obtained from the new `FinancialStatementStore.DescribeRefusal(payload, reasonCode, presentationUnit)` — which reads the **same** `DeclarationFailure` predicate the refusal itself uses, so a recorded detail can never describe a different rule than the one that fired. The evidence is the decode's digest rather than the raw body, stated rather than glossed: the sink receives a `KapPublicReport`, not the HTML, and the decoded digest names the filing and the exact findings a re-fetch would be spent to re-learn. **The capture is committed honestly labelled:** `kap-public-report-1605802` is a bounded trim of that body (header block + the full BS table `tbl_general_role_210015` + the full IS table `tbl_general_role_310003`; the CF and equity tables omitted whole), and its catalog label says it is ASTOR's, index `1605802`, **not** a GUBRF body — no GUBRF fact is manufactured from it anywhere. **Red→green, measured (the red re-run, not remembered):** with only the three product files (`KapPublicReportDecoder.cs`, `FinancialStatementStore.cs`, `StatementsJob.cs`) reverted to the pre-ticket head — models, fixtures and every assertion kept, so the red is behaviour, never a compile or environment failure — the `TC_MDF_128` filter reads **2 failed / 1 passed**: (b) `Expected: "units", Actual: null` on `UnitScale` (the gap itself), (a) `Assert.NotNull() Failure: Value is null` on the quarantine row's `PayloadJson` — the NULL-evidence hole this ticket closes — while (c) passes **by construction**, recorded as such rather than manufactured red, because it pins the five bodies' unchanged goldens. **3/3 green** with the fix. **Suites: 81/81 green and unrelaxed** on `KapPublicReportDecoderTests | KapPublicStatementsIngestTests | RealSourceStatementsTests | StatementNormalizationTests | StatementVersioningTests`, and **50/50** on the neighbours the fixture catalog addition touches (`RealSourcePricesTests` — the catalog `Names`/`Build` contract — `CannedSourceCatalogTests`, `KapPublicRecomputeTests`, `KapPublicSourceTests`); `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Live criteria (orchestrator, post-merge, paced, never CI):** one `Source = kap-public` statements pass where a filing with a bare-currency declaration (the `TL` form) is **written** rather than refused — counts verbatim — and any refusal that does occur is readable from its own row's evidence and log detail; the affected companies' 2026-10-28 `SCHEMA_MISMATCH` quarantines re-walk once under 092's recorded replay rule, replayed/recovered/still-quarantined counts verbatim. Applied by the ticket's implementation lane; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

**v1.57 2026-10-30 — §15 extended and implemented: the family-lane cursor checkpoint (TC-MDF-130), TKT-foundation-069's implementation lane;** mirroring architecture **v1.5.84** §10.9.15 (the residual paragraph replaced: the cursor is a checkpoint persisted after the write, not a walk product persisted before it) and §10.9.10 (the fail-fast rationale re-worded to the new shape). **What the case pins:** §10.9.15 recorded verbatim that the three event families persist their per-instrument cursors at the end of the adapter loop — *before* the job writes a single fact — and accepted, on the ticket, that a store failure in that window "can skip the indexes the cursor already covers"; 063 closed that window for **statements only**, and its ER005 reset cannot recover the family class at all (a cursor advanced past unstored facts produces no `ER005`, so the next run simply starts after them — a silent gap, which FR-MDF-010/011 forbid). The fix is the ticket's pinned option (a), return-and-persist-after-write: each adapter's walk collects its cursor work (per-instrument advances, the market-wide row, the clears of an honest-empty reset) and the job persists it only after its fact write and skip evidence succeeded. **The failure is injected where it really happens** — a PostgreSQL trigger raising on the fact table's `INSERT`, created and dropped by the test — so the walk genuinely emits facts before the store refuses them and the assertion is about the real window, not a proxy for it. **Red→green, measured (the red re-run, not remembered):** with only the six product files reverted to the pre-ticket head (tests kept, so the red is behaviour, never a compile or environment failure) the `TC_MDF_130` filter reads **4 failed / 0 passed** — the three family cases at `Expected: 0, Actual: 1` on the cursor-row count (the old adapter had already persisted the cursor before the store ran) and the incremental case at `Expected: "1138986", Actual: "1138987"` (the cursor advanced past facts the store never got) — and **4/4 green** with the fix; `StatementsAndEventsIngestionTests` **64/64** (034/046/061/063/078/094/095/096/097/099/100/101 goldens, TC-MDF-097's ER005 reset unchanged), `StockFundamentalsEndpointsTests` **18/18**, `EodChainTests | MorningChainTests | VykCompanyDirectoryTests` **13/13**, `dotnet build` 0 warnings / 0 errors. **Recorded, not asserted (the same note as the case):** a failed run of this class surfaces as a thrown exception **without** an `ingest_runs` row, because the runner's own ledger write re-attempts the inserts EF still tracks and fails identically — the pre-existing `Jobs.cs` posture (TKT-foundation-044's scope), deliberately left to that lane and pinned nowhere here, so a later fix cannot turn this case red. No other section's expectation, precondition or level changes. Applied by the ticket's implementation lane; test-planner confirmation pending (the v1.2/v1.3 precedent).

No test in this section reaches kap.org.tr; this ticket issued **zero** live requests. **Implemented as six cases in `tests/Api.IntegrationTests/KapPublicDisclosuresFeedTests.cs`, every one tagged `TC_MDF_112`,** over the L2 world with a test-local `STMT` bound (`Ingestion:Disclosures:UniverseIndex`) and the two bound companies' stored public oids (080's map): (i) the pass issues **one** list request carrying a watermark-derived **date window** (`fromDate` 2026-10-23, `toDate` 2026-10-24) and the empty `mkkMemberOidList`, and persists its **own** cursor row — `kap-public-disclosures-cursor`, scope `market`, no instrument/fund — with watermark `2026-10-24` and the capture's 12 public indices as the seen set;
